# mixxxlib v1 Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Build `mixxxlib`, an offline CLI and Python library that reads and writes a Mixxx library (the `mixxxdb.sqlite` database and audio-file tags) safely, deterministically and for programs first.

**Architecture:** A `src/` package where a Typer CLI is a thin shell over a `Library` façade that never imports Typer. Every mutation flows through one protocol (schema guard → Mixxx-running guard → validate → dry-run → `VACUUM INTO` backup → file tags → one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction → `integrity_check` → prune). Reads use `mode=ro` connections. Tests run against a trimmed copy of a real Mixxx 2.5.6 database with one-second audio files, never the live database.

**Tech Stack:** Python ≥ 3.12 (stdlib `sqlite3`, `autocommit=True`), Typer, mutagen; dev: pytest, shiv; ffmpeg only to regenerate fixtures.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-08-mixxxlib-design.md` (revision 4). Section references below (§n) point into that file. Read the spec section before implementing a task.

## Global Constraints

Copied from the spec; every task's requirements implicitly include these.

- Python ≥ 3.12. Runtime dependencies are exactly `typer` and `mutagen`. Dev dependencies are `pytest` and `shiv`. No `protobuf`, no ORM, no `rich`-dependent output of our own.
- `src/` layout; package, import name, command, zipapp, data dir (`~/.local/share/mixxxlib`) and env vars (`MIXXXLIB_*`) all use the one word `mixxxlib`.
- The library never imports Typer. Importing `mixxxlib` registers nothing global.
- `--json` on every command; stdout carries only the success payload; errors go to stderr. Exit codes per §4: 0 ok, 1 internal, 2 usage, 3 not_found, 4 validation, 5 mixxx_running, 6 schema_unsupported, 7 database, 8 confirmation_required, 9 tag_error, 10 unhealthy.
- Never prompt, never require a TTY. Destructive commands (`crate delete`, `crate remove`, `crate set-tracks`, `cue delete`, `cue clear`, `backup restore`, `track set` unless `--mixxx-only`) require `--yes`. `--dry-run` prints statements and changes nothing, and is never the default.
- **Never enable `PRAGMA foreign_keys`, never alter DDL.** Referential integrity is enforced in application code.
- Reads open `file:<path>?mode=ro`. Mutations: `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` lock probe, `VACUUM INTO` backup (never `cp`), one transaction per command, `PRAGMA integrity_check` afterwards.
- Schema ceiling 40; capability table in `db/schema.py` is the single place a revision is described. Nested-crate commands need revision 41 (exit 6 below it).
- Lists sort deterministically: crates by name, tracks by id, cues by type then slot then position. Same database → byte-identical JSON. SQLite `NULL` is never coerced.
- Scripts a human runs are POSIX `sh`; anything with logic is Python.
- Fixture tests never touch `~/.mixxx`. Every integration test ends by running `doctor` and asserting no new error-severity findings.
- Commit after every task with a conventional-commit message (`feat(scope): …`, `test: …`, `chore: …`, `docs: …`), matching the repo's existing style.

## Facts verified against the real database on 2026-10-09

These were measured on this machine (Mixxx 2.5.6, schema 39, SQLite 3.53.4, Python 3.14.7) and shape the code below.

- `library` DDL (verbatim column order): `id, artist, title, album, year, genre, tracknumber, location, comment, url, duration, bitrate, samplerate, cuepoint, bpm, wavesummaryhex, channels, datetime_added, mixxx_deleted, played, header_parsed, filetype, replaygain, timesplayed, rating, key, beats, beats_version, composer, bpm_lock, beats_sub_version, keys, keys_version, keys_sub_version, key_id, grouping, album_artist, coverart_source, coverart_type, coverart_location, coverart_hash, replaygain_peak, tracktotal, color, coverart_color, coverart_digest, last_played_at, source_synchronized_ms`.
- `track_locations`: `id, location, filename, directory, filesize, fs_deleted, needs_verification`. `crates`: `id, name, count, show, locked, autodj_source`. `crate_tracks`: `crate_id, track_id`. `cues`: `id, track_id, type, position, length, hotcue, label, color`. `settings`: `name, value, locked, hidden` (**the key column is `name`, not `key`**; the spec's `settings.value WHERE key = …` must read `WHERE name = 'mixxx.schema.version'`). `Playlists`: `id, name, position, hidden, date_created, date_modified, locked`. `PlaylistTracks`: `id, playlist_id, track_id, position, pl_datetime_added`. `track_analysis`: `id, track_id, type, description, version, created, data_checksum`.
- The real database contains **no hotcue rows (type 1)** and **no `key`/`key_id` mismatch**; the fixture script injects both. Cue types present: 2, 4, 6, 7, 8. One real loop has a negative `position` (-56.8). Cue `position` is `integer` for most rows and `real` for some.
- Beatgrid blobs: 566/566 decode as `0A 09 09 <double> 12 <len> 08 <varint> …`, lengths 15–24 bytes (extra optional fields appear in some), every BPM equal to `library.bpm`. `first_beat.frame_position` is per-channel frames.
- `mixxx.cfg` is **not `=`-separated INI**. Lines are `[Section]` headers (section names can themselves contain brackets, e.g. `[EqualizerRack1_[Channel1]]`) and `Key Value` pairs split on the first space; some keys have an empty value. `configparser` cannot parse it; the parser is hand-written.
- `datetime_added` is text like `2026-09-23T12:28:23.026Z`; `last_played_at` is text like `2026-09-26 00:40:35`; `source_synchronized_ms` is an integer; `played`, `mixxx_deleted`, `header_parsed`, `bpm_lock` are integers 0/1.
- `sqlite3.connect(..., autocommit=True)` (Python ≥ 3.12) lets us issue `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` / `COMMIT` ourselves. With another connection holding `BEGIN EXCLUSIVE`, `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` raises `sqlite3.OperationalError: database is locked` (`sqlite_errorname == "SQLITE_BUSY"`), and even a `mode=ro` read raises the same.
- Typer 0.27 / Click 8.5: a decorator that appends the four global options to each command's `inspect.Signature` makes `mixxxlib --json info` and `mixxxlib info --json` both work (verified). `typer-slim` is now a shim depending on `typer`, so plain `typer` is the dependency; `rich` comes along but is never used by our code (`rich_markup_mode=None`, `pretty_exceptions_enable=False`).
- mutagen 1.48.1: `mutagen.aiff.AIFF`, `mutagen.mp3.MP3` (both ID3; `add_tags()` when `tags is None`), `mutagen.flac.FLAC` (Vorbis, dict-like, lowercase keys on read). `GRP1` frame class exists. `COMM` frames key as `COMM::eng`.
- Tools present for fixture generation: `ffmpeg`, `lame`, `flac`, `sqlite3` CLI. The system Python has no `pip` module, but `python3 -m venv .venv` creates a venv whose `pip` works.

## Decisions made while planning (deviations from the spec text, all small)

1. `errors.py` names the base class `MixxxlibError` (the spec still says `MixxxctlError`, a leftover from the rename).
2. Settings parsing lives in `src/mixxxlib/settings.py` (library layer, no Typer) so `lib.settings` works without the CLI; `cli/settings.py` is the thin command.
3. `--clear key` writes `key = ''` and `key_id = 0` (Mixxx's own defaults) rather than `NULL`, because `key_id` is never set independently and `key` defaults to `''`. Every other `--clear` writes `NULL`.
4. The committed fixture stores `track_locations.location` as the repo-relative path `tests/fixtures/audio/<file>`; the test fixture copies the audio directory into the test's temp dir and rewrites locations to that absolute path, so tests can write tags without touching committed files.
5. Revisions below 39 are refused with exit 6 ("below the lowest known revision"), because no earlier schema was examined. The capability table starts at 39.
6. The CLI's unexpected-exception handler prints the traceback only when `MIXXXLIB_DEBUG` is set; otherwise one line on stderr, exit 1.
7. `--dry-run` does not require `--yes`: a dry run changes nothing, so it is not destructive. Without `--dry-run`, `--yes` is checked after the crate/track/cue selector resolves (so a bad selector is exit 3) and before the mutation runs.
8. Cue `--slot` is required for `hotcue`, **optional for `loop`** (the reference library stores saved loops in hotcue slots, which the spec's "forbidden otherwise" would make unwritable), and refused for `jump`/`intro`/`outro`.
9. `library.settings` uses the `name` column of the `settings` table (the spec's `WHERE key = ...` names a column that does not exist).

```
README.md                         # §11 (Task 19)

  library.py                      # Library façade: open(), crates/tracks/cues/backups APIs, doctor(), info()
  db/__init__.py

  db/crates.py                    # crate SQL, sibling uniqueness, cycle check, membership plans

  db/cues.py                      # cue SQL, validation, insert/update/delete/clear plans

  tags/__init__.py

  safety/__init__.py
  safety/guard.py                 # mixxx_process_running(), probe_lock(), assert_mixxx_closed()
  safety/backup.py                # backup_name(), create_backup(), list_backups(), prune(), Retention
  doctor.py                       # §8.5 checks → list[Finding]
  output.py                       # as_json(), envelope(), error payloads, human tables
  cli/__init__.py                 # root Typer app, main(), subapp registration

  cli/info.py  cli/settings.py  cli/crate.py  cli/track.py  cli/cue.py  cli/doctor.py  cli/backup.py
scripts/

  build-zipapp.sh                 # shiv -c mixxxlib -o dist/mixxxlib .

tests/
  conftest.py                     # fixture copies, sandbox object, run_cli(), doctor-at-teardown
  fixtures/mixxxdb.sqlite  fixtures/mixxxdb-42.sqlite  fixtures/manifest.json  fixtures/mixxx.cfg  fixtures/audio/*
```

`src/mixxxlib/cli/__init__.py` (placeholder):

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from typing import Annotated

import typer

from mixxxlib import __version__

app = typer.Typer(
    add_completion=False,
    rich_markup_mode=None,
    pretty_exceptions_enable=False,
    no_args_is_help=True,
)


@app.callback(invoke_without_command=True)
def root(
    version: Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--version", help="Print the version and exit.")] = False,
) -> None:
    if version:
        typer.echo(__version__)
        raise typer.Exit(0)


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    try:
        app(args=argv if argv is not None else sys.argv[1:], standalone_mode=False)
    except typer.Exit as exc:
        return int(exc.exit_code)
    return 0
```

### Task 2: Errors, exit codes and the data model

**Files:**
- Create: `src/mixxxlib/errors.py`, `src/mixxxlib/model.py`, `tests/unit/test_errors.py`, `tests/unit/test_model.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `errors.MixxxlibError(message: str, **details)` with class attributes `code: str`, `exit_code: int`, instance attrs `message`, `details: dict`; subclasses `InternalError(1)`, `UsageError(2)`, `NotFoundError(3)`, `ValidationError(4)`, `MixxxRunningError(5)`, `SchemaUnsupportedError(6)`, `DatabaseError(7)`, `ConfirmationRequiredError(8)`, `TagError(9)`, `UnhealthyError(10)`; `errors.EXIT_CODES: dict[str, int]`.
- Produces: `model.MusicalKey(id, text)` with `parse(value)`, `from_db(text, key_id)`; `model.parse_color(value, *, allow_zero=False) -> int`; `model.format_color(n) -> str | None`; `model.CUE_TYPES`, `CUE_TYPE_IDS`, `WRITABLE_CUE_TYPES`, `READ_ONLY_CUE_TYPES`; frozen dataclasses `Crate`, `CrateNode`, `Location`, `Metadata`, `Audio`, `Bookkeeping`, `Beatgrid`, `Analysis`, `CoverArt`, `Track`, `Cue`, `Finding`, `WarningMessage`, `SchemaInfo`, `MutationResult`, `BackupEntry`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`tests/unit/test_errors.py`:

```python
import pytest

from mixxxlib import errors


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "cls, code, exit_code",
    [
        (errors.InternalError, "internal", 1),
        (errors.UsageError, "usage", 2),
        (errors.NotFoundError, "not_found", 3),
        (errors.ValidationError, "validation", 4),
        (errors.MixxxRunningError, "mixxx_running", 5),
        (errors.SchemaUnsupportedError, "schema_unsupported", 6),
        (errors.DatabaseError, "database", 7),
        (errors.ConfirmationRequiredError, "confirmation_required", 8),
        (errors.TagError, "tag_error", 9),
        (errors.UnhealthyError, "unhealthy", 10),
    ],
)
def test_each_error_maps_to_its_exit_code(cls, code, exit_code):
    err = cls("boom", track_id=7)
    assert err.code == code
    assert err.exit_code == exit_code
    assert err.message == "boom"
    assert err.details == {"track_id": 7}
    assert str(err) == "boom"
    assert isinstance(err, errors.MixxxlibError)
    assert errors.EXIT_CODES[code] == exit_code


def test_exit_code_table_is_complete():
    assert errors.EXIT_CODES == {
        "ok": 0, "internal": 1, "usage": 2, "not_found": 3, "validation": 4,
        "mixxx_running": 5, "schema_unsupported": 6, "database": 7,
        "confirmation_required": 8, "tag_error": 9, "unhealthy": 10,
    }


def test_to_payload_shape():
    err = errors.ValidationError("slot 0 occupied", track_id=612, slot=0)
    assert err.to_payload() == {
        "ok": False,
        "error": {"code": "validation", "exit_code": 4, "message": "slot 0 occupied",
                  "details": {"track_id": 612, "slot": 0}},
    }
```

`tests/unit/test_model.py`:

```python
import pytest

from mixxxlib.errors import ValidationError
from mixxxlib.model import (
    CUE_TYPES, CUE_TYPE_IDS, WRITABLE_CUE_TYPES, MusicalKey, format_color, parse_color,
)


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "text, key_id",
    [
        ("C", 1), ("Db", 2), ("C#", 2), ("D", 3), ("Eb", 4), ("E", 5), ("F", 6), ("Gb", 7), ("F#", 7),
        ("G", 8), ("Ab", 9), ("A", 10), ("Bb", 11), ("B", 12),
        ("Cm", 13), ("C#m", 14), ("Dbm", 14), ("Dm", 15), ("Ebm", 16), ("Em", 17), ("Fm", 18),
        ("F#m", 19), ("Gbm", 19), ("Gm", 20), ("G#m", 21), ("Abm", 21), ("Am", 22), ("Bbm", 23), ("Bm", 24),
        ("E minor", 17), ("E min", 17), ("Emin", 17), ("E major", 5), ("Emaj", 5), ("e♭m", 16), ("A♯", 11),
    ],
)
def test_musical_key_parses_traditional_notation_keeping_the_text(text, key_id):
    key = MusicalKey.parse(text)
    assert key.id == key_id
    assert key.text == text.strip()


@pytest.mark.parametrize("value", [0, 17, "17", 24])
def test_musical_key_parses_bare_ids_with_canonical_text(value):
    key = MusicalKey.parse(value)
    assert key.id == int(value)
    assert key.text == {0: "", 17: "Em", 24: "Bm"}[int(value)]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("value", ["H", "Em7", 25, -1, "X minor", "C##"])
def test_musical_key_rejects_garbage(value):
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        MusicalKey.parse(value)


def test_musical_key_none_and_empty_mean_no_key():
    assert MusicalKey.parse(None) == MusicalKey(0, "")
    assert MusicalKey.parse("") == MusicalKey(0, "")
    assert MusicalKey.from_db(None, None) == MusicalKey(0, "")
    assert MusicalKey.from_db("Em", 17) == MusicalKey(17, "Em")


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    "value, expected",
    [("#C50A08", 0xC50A08), ("0xC50A08", 0xC50A08), ("12913160", 0xC50A08), (12913160, 0xC50A08),
     ("#FFC50A08", 0xC50A08), ("ffc50a08", None), (0xFFC50A08, 0xC50A08), ("0xFF8000", 0xFF8000)],
)
def test_parse_color_accepts_documented_forms_and_strips_alpha(value, expected):
    if expected is None:
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
            parse_color(value)
    else:
        assert parse_color(value) == expected


@pytest.mark.parametrize("value", ["#000000", 0, "0", 0xFF000000, -1, "#12345", "blue", True])
def test_parse_color_rejects_zero_negative_and_malformed(value):
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        parse_color(value)


def test_parse_color_allow_zero_for_track_colour():
    assert parse_color("#000000", allow_zero=True) == 0


def test_format_color():
    assert format_color(0xC50A08) == "#C50A08"
    assert format_color(0) == "#000000"
    assert format_color(None) is None


def test_cue_type_tables():
    assert CUE_TYPES == {0: "invalid", 1: "hotcue", 2: "main", 3: "beat", 4: "loop", 5: "jump",
                         6: "intro", 7: "outro", 8: "n60db"}
    assert CUE_TYPE_IDS["hotcue"] == 1
    assert WRITABLE_CUE_TYPES == frozenset({"hotcue", "loop", "jump", "intro", "outro"})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/mixxxlib/errors.py`**

```python
"""Error hierarchy. One subclass per exit-code row in spec §4."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Any


class MixxxlibError(Exception):
    """Base for every error mixxxlib raises on purpose."""

    code: str = "internal"
    exit_code: int = 1

    def __init__(self, message: str, **details: Any) -> None:
        super().__init__(message)
        self.message = message
        self.details: dict[str, Any] = details

    def to_payload(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "ok": False,
            "error": {
                "code": self.code,
                "exit_code": self.exit_code,
                "message": self.message,
                "details": self.details,
            },
        }


class InternalError(MixxxlibError):
    code = "internal"
    exit_code = 1


class UsageError(MixxxlibError):
    code = "usage"
    exit_code = 2


class NotFoundError(MixxxlibError):
    code = "not_found"
    exit_code = 3


class ValidationError(MixxxlibError):
    code = "validation"
    exit_code = 4


class MixxxRunningError(MixxxlibError):
    code = "mixxx_running"
    exit_code = 5


class SchemaUnsupportedError(MixxxlibError):
    code = "schema_unsupported"
    exit_code = 6


class DatabaseError(MixxxlibError):
    code = "database"
    exit_code = 7


class ConfirmationRequiredError(MixxxlibError):
    code = "confirmation_required"
    exit_code = 8


class TagError(MixxxlibError):
    code = "tag_error"
    exit_code = 9


class UnhealthyError(MixxxlibError):
    code = "unhealthy"
    exit_code = 10


ERROR_CLASSES: tuple[type[MixxxlibError], ...] = (
    InternalError, UsageError, NotFoundError, ValidationError, MixxxRunningError,
    SchemaUnsupportedError, DatabaseError, ConfirmationRequiredError, TagError, UnhealthyError,
)

EXIT_CODES: dict[str, int] = {"ok": 0, **{cls.code: cls.exit_code for cls in ERROR_CLASSES}}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/mixxxlib/model.py`**

```python
"""Frozen value types returned by the library API (spec §6)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import re
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any

from mixxxlib.errors import ValidationError

# ---------------------------------------------------------------- musical key

# Mixxx ChromaticKey: 0 none, 1–12 majors, 13–24 minors (spec §6.2).
CHROMATIC_KEYS: dict[int, str] = {
    0: "", 1: "C", 2: "Db", 3: "D", 4: "Eb", 5: "E", 6: "F", 7: "Gb", 8: "G", 9: "Ab", 10: "A",
    11: "Bb", 12: "B", 13: "Cm", 14: "C#m", 15: "Dm", 16: "Ebm", 17: "Em", 18: "Fm", 19: "F#m",
    20: "Gm", 21: "G#m", 22: "Am", 23: "Bbm", 24: "Bm",
}
_SEMITONE = {"C": 0, "D": 2, "E": 4, "F": 5, "G": 7, "A": 9, "B": 11}
_KEY_RE = re.compile(r"^([A-Ga-g])([#♯b♭])?\s*(m|min|minor|maj|major)?$")


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class MusicalKey:
    id: int
    text: str

    @classmethod
    def parse(cls, value: str | int | None) -> "MusicalKey":
        if value is None:
            return cls(0, "")
        if isinstance(value, bool):
            raise ValidationError(f"unrecognised key {value!r}", key=value)
        if isinstance(value, int):
            if not 0 <= value <= 24:
                raise ValidationError(f"key_id {value} is outside 0..24", key_id=value)
            return cls(value, CHROMATIC_KEYS[value])
        text = str(value).strip()
        if text == "":
            return cls(0, "")
        if text.isdigit():
            return cls.parse(int(text))
        match = _KEY_RE.match(text)
        if not match:
            raise ValidationError(f"unrecognised key {text!r}", key=text)
        letter, accidental, mode = match.group(1).upper(), match.group(2), (match.group(3) or "")
        semitone = _SEMITONE[letter]
        if accidental in ("#", "♯"):
            semitone += 1
        elif accidental in ("b", "♭"):
            semitone -= 1
        semitone %= 12
        minor = mode in ("m", "min", "minor")
        return cls(13 + semitone if minor else 1 + semitone, text)

    @classmethod
    def from_db(cls, text: str | None, key_id: int | None) -> "MusicalKey":
        return cls(int(key_id or 0), text or "")

    def to_json(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"id": self.id, "text": self.text}


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- colours

def parse_color(value: str | int, *, allow_zero: bool = False) -> int:
    """Accept #RRGGBB, 0xRRGGBB, decimal, or 8-digit AARRGGBB (alpha stripped). Returns 24-bit RGB."""
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        raise ValidationError(f"colour must be a number or hex string, not {value!r}", color=value)
    if isinstance(value, int):
        number = value
    else:
        text = str(value).strip().lower()
        try:
            if text.startswith("#"):
                digits = text[1:]
                if len(digits) not in (6, 8):
                    raise ValueError
                number = int(digits, 16)
            elif text.startswith("0x"):
                number = int(text[2:], 16)
            elif text.isdigit():
                number = int(text)
            else:
                raise ValueError
        except ValueError:
            raise ValidationError(f"unrecognised colour {value!r}", color=value) from None
    if number < 0:
        raise ValidationError(f"colour {value!r} is negative", color=value)
    if number > 0xFFFFFF:
        number &= 0xFFFFFF  # strip the alpha byte of AARRGGBB
    if number == 0 and not allow_zero:
        raise ValidationError("colour must be non-zero; Mixxx discards cues whose colour is 0", color=value)
    return number


def format_color(value: int | None) -> str | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    return f"#{value & 0xFFFFFF:06X}"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- cue types

CUE_TYPES: dict[int, str] = {
    0: "invalid", 1: "hotcue", 2: "main", 3: "beat", 4: "loop", 5: "jump", 6: "intro", 7: "outro", 8: "n60db",
}
CUE_TYPE_IDS: dict[str, int] = {name: type_id for type_id, name in CUE_TYPES.items()}
WRITABLE_CUE_TYPES: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"hotcue", "loop", "jump", "intro", "outro"})
READ_ONLY_CUE_TYPES: frozenset[str] = frozenset(CUE_TYPES.values()) - WRITABLE_CUE_TYPES


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- crates

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Crate:
    id: int
    name: str
    parent_id: int | None
    locked: bool
    show: bool
    autodj_source: bool
    track_count: int


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CrateNode:
    crate: Crate
    children: tuple["CrateNode", ...] = ()


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- tracks

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Location:
    id: int
    path: str | None
    filename: str | None
    directory: str | None
    filesize: int | None
    fs_deleted: bool


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Metadata:
    artist: str | None
    title: str | None
    album: str | None
    album_artist: str | None
    year: str | None
    genre: str | None
    tracknumber: str | None
    tracktotal: str | None
    composer: str | None
    grouping: str | None
    comment: str | None
    rating: int | None
    color: int | None
    url: str | None
    bpm_lock: bool
    bpm: float | None
    key: MusicalKey


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Audio:
    duration: float | None
    bitrate: int | None
    samplerate: int | None
    channels: int | None
    filetype: str | None
    replaygain: float | None
    replaygain_peak: float | None
    tuning_frequency_hz: float | None  # schema 40 only; None below


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Bookkeeping:
    datetime_added: str | None
    played: bool
    timesplayed: int | None
    last_played_at: str | None
    mixxx_deleted: bool
    header_parsed: bool
    source_synchronized_ms: int | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Beatgrid:
    version: str | None
    decoded: bool
    bpm: float | None = None
    first_beat_frame: int | None = None
    first_beat_seconds: float | None = None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Analysis:
    beats_version: str | None
    beats_sub_version: str | None
    keys_version: str | None
    keys_sub_version: str | None
    beatgrid: Beatgrid


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class CoverArt:
    source: int | None
    type: int | None
    location: str | None
    hash: int | None
    color: int | None


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Track:
    id: int
    location: Location
    metadata: Metadata
    audio: Audio
    bookkeeping: Bookkeeping
    analysis: Analysis
    coverart: CoverArt

    @property
    def samples_per_second(self) -> float | None:
        """Interleaved samples per second: samplerate × channels (cue unit, spec §6.3)."""
        if not self.audio.samplerate or not self.audio.channels:
            return None
        return float(self.audio.samplerate * self.audio.channels)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- cues

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Cue:
    id: int
    track_id: int
    type_id: int
    position_samples: float
    length_samples: float
    slot: int
    label: str
    color: int
    position_seconds: float | None
    length_seconds: float | None

    @property
    def type(self) -> str:
        return CUE_TYPES.get(self.type_id, "unknown")


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- misc

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class WarningMessage:
    code: str
    message: str
    details: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Finding:
    check: str
    severity: str  # "error" | "warning" | "info"
    message: str
    details: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class SchemaInfo:
    version: int | None
    raw: str | None
    ceiling: int
    capabilities: frozenset[str]
    verdict: str  # "supported" | "unverified" | "unsupported" | "missing"
    message: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class BackupEntry:
    path: str
    name: str
    kind: str  # "database" | "tags"
    size: int
    modified: str  # ISO 8601 UTC
    age_seconds: int


@dataclass
class MutationResult:
    data: dict[str, Any]
    statements: list[str]
    backup: str | None
    integrity: str | None
    dry_run: bool
    warnings: list[WarningMessage] = field(default_factory=list)
    file_operations: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/mixxxlib/paths.py`**

```python
"""Database, config and backup-directory resolution (spec §9, §7.6)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sys
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Mapping

DB_FILENAME = "mixxxdb.sqlite"
CONFIG_FILENAME = "mixxx.cfg"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ResolvedPaths:
    settings_dir: Path
    db: Path
    config: Path
    backup_dir: Path


def default_settings_dir(*, platform: str, home: Path, environ: Mapping[str, str]) -> Path:
    if platform == "darwin":
        container = home / "Library/Containers/org.mixxx.mixxx/Data/Library/Application Support/Mixxx"
        if container.is_dir():
            return container
        return home / "Library/Application Support/Mixxx"
    if platform.startswith("win"):
        local = environ.get("LOCALAPPDATA")
        base = Path(local) if local else home / "AppData/Local"
        return base / "Mixxx"
    return home / ".mixxx"


def default_backup_dir(*, home: Path, environ: Mapping[str, str]) -> Path:
    xdg = environ.get("XDG_DATA_HOME")
    base = Path(xdg) if xdg else home / ".local/share"
    return base / "mixxxlib" / "backups"


def _expand(value: str) -> Path:
    return Path(os.path.expanduser(value))


def resolve(
    settings_path: str | os.PathLike[str] | None = None,
    db: str | os.PathLike[str] | None = None,
    backup_dir: str | os.PathLike[str] | None = None,
    *,
    environ: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,
    platform: str | None = None,
    home: Path | None = None,
) -> ResolvedPaths:
    """Precedence: explicit argument, then MIXXXLIB_* environment, then platform default."""
    env = os.environ if environ is None else environ
    plat = sys.platform if platform is None else platform
    home_dir = Path.home() if home is None else Path(home)

    settings_value = settings_path or env.get("MIXXXLIB_SETTINGS_PATH")
    settings_dir = _expand(str(settings_value)) if settings_value else default_settings_dir(
        platform=plat, home=home_dir, environ=env
    )
    db_value = db or env.get("MIXXXLIB_DB")
    db_path = _expand(str(db_value)) if db_value else settings_dir / DB_FILENAME
    backup_value = backup_dir or env.get("MIXXXLIB_BACKUP_DIR")
    backup_path = _expand(str(backup_value)) if backup_value else default_backup_dir(home=home_dir, environ=env)
    return ResolvedPaths(settings_dir=settings_dir, db=db_path, config=settings_dir / CONFIG_FILENAME, backup_dir=backup_path)
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/mixxxlib/settings.py`**

```python
"""Parser for Mixxx's mixxx.cfg (spec §6.5).

The format is not INI: `[Section]` headers (whose names may contain brackets) followed by
`Key Value` lines split on the first space. Values may be empty or contain spaces.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from mixxxlib.errors import NotFoundError

Settings = dict[str, dict[str, str]]


def parse_mixxx_cfg(text: str) -> Settings:
    sections: Settings = {}
    current = ""
    for raw in text.splitlines():
        line = raw.strip()
        if not line:
            continue
        if line.startswith("[") and line.endswith("]"):
            current = line[1:-1]
            sections.setdefault(current, {})
            continue
        key, _, value = line.partition(" ")
        sections.setdefault(current, {})[key] = value.strip()
    return sections


def load_mixxx_cfg(path: Path) -> Settings:
    try:
        text = Path(path).read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace")
    except FileNotFoundError:
        raise NotFoundError(f"mixxx.cfg not found at {path}", path=str(path)) from None
    return parse_mixxx_cfg(text)


def select(settings: Settings, selector: str | None) -> Any:
    if not selector:
        return settings
    section, _, key = selector.partition(".")
    if section not in settings:
        raise NotFoundError(f"section {section!r} not in mixxx.cfg", section=section)
    if not key:
        return settings[section]
    if key not in settings[section]:
        raise NotFoundError(f"key {key!r} not in section {section!r}", section=section, key=key)
    return settings[section][key]
```

```python
import sqlite3

import pytest

from mixxxlib.db import schema
from mixxxlib.errors import SchemaUnsupportedError


def _conn(version):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(":memory:")
    conn.execute("CREATE TABLE settings (name TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL, value TEXT, locked INTEGER DEFAULT 0, hidden INTEGER DEFAULT 0)")
    if version is not None:
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO settings (name, value) VALUES ('mixxx.schema.version', ?)", (version,))
    return conn


def test_capabilities_are_cumulative():
    assert schema.capabilities_for(39) == frozenset()
    assert schema.capabilities_for(40) == {"tuning_frequency"}
    assert schema.capabilities_for(41) == {"tuning_frequency", "nested_crates"}
    assert schema.capabilities_for(42) == {"tuning_frequency", "nested_crates", "sibling_unique_rebuild"}
    assert schema.first_revision_with("nested_crates") == 41


def test_ceiling_and_source_are_pinned():
    assert schema.SCHEMA_CEILING == 40
    assert "94ccc34f" in schema.SCHEMA_SOURCE and schema.SCHEMA_SOURCE.endswith("res/schema.xml")


@pytest.mark.parametrize("version, verdict", [("39", "supported"), ("40", "unverified"), ("41", "unsupported"),
                                              ("38", "unsupported"), (None, "missing"), ("abc", "missing")])
def test_verdicts(version, verdict):
    info = schema.schema_info(_conn(version))
    assert info.verdict == verdict
    assert info.ceiling == 40
    assert info.raw == version


def test_require_schema_refuses_above_ceiling_and_names_it():
    with pytest.raises(SchemaUnsupportedError) as e:
        schema.require_schema(schema.schema_info(_conn("41")))
    assert e.value.details == {"version": 41, "ceiling": 40}
    assert "41" in e.value.message and "40" in e.value.message


def test_require_schema_warns_at_40():
    warnings = schema.require_schema(schema.schema_info(_conn("40")))
    assert [w.code for w in warnings] == ["schema_unverified"]


def test_require_schema_capability_names_needed_revision():
    with pytest.raises(SchemaUnsupportedError) as e:
        schema.require_schema(schema.schema_info(_conn("39")), capability="nested_crates")
    assert e.value.details == {"version": 39, "ceiling": 40, "capability": "nested_crates", "needs": 41}


def test_ceiling_override_admits_42():
    info = schema.schema_info(_conn("42"), ceiling=42)
    assert info.verdict == "unverified"
    assert "nested_crates" in info.capabilities
    assert schema.require_schema(info, capability="nested_crates")[0].code == "schema_unverified"


def test_table_columns_cover_the_tables_we_touch():
    assert set(schema.TABLE_COLUMNS) == {"library", "track_locations", "crates", "crate_tracks", "cues",
                                         "settings", "Playlists", "PlaylistTracks", "track_analysis"}
    assert "source_synchronized_ms" in schema.TABLE_COLUMNS["library"]
    assert schema.TABLE_COLUMNS["settings"] == ("name", "value", "locked", "hidden")
```

```python
"""Schema revisions, capabilities and the column sets mixxxlib relies on (spec §3, §7.3).

Supporting a new revision means adding a row to REVISION_CAPABILITIES and, after verifying
against a real database, raising SCHEMA_CEILING and adding the revision to VERIFIED_REVISIONS.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3

from mixxxlib.errors import SchemaUnsupportedError
from mixxxlib.model import SchemaInfo, WarningMessage

SCHEMA_CEILING = 40
SCHEMA_FLOOR = 39
VERIFIED_REVISIONS: frozenset[int] = frozenset({39})
# res/schema.xml at the Mixxx 2.5.6 release commit, which carries revision 39.
SCHEMA_SOURCE = "https://github.com/mixxxdj/mixxx/blob/94ccc34f/res/schema.xml"

# What each known revision adds. Capabilities are cumulative.
REVISION_CAPABILITIES: dict[int, frozenset[str]] = {
    39: frozenset(),
    40: frozenset({"tuning_frequency"}),        # library.tuning_frequency_hz, nullable
    41: frozenset({"nested_crates"}),           # PR #16871: crates.parent_id
    42: frozenset({"sibling_unique_rebuild"}),  # PR #16871: UNIQUE (parent_id, name) replaces UNIQUE (name)
}

# The tables and columns mixxxlib reads or writes, as found at revision 39.
TABLE_COLUMNS: dict[str, tuple[str, ...]] = {
    "library": (
        "id", "artist", "title", "album", "year", "genre", "tracknumber", "location", "comment", "url",
        "duration", "bitrate", "samplerate", "cuepoint", "bpm", "wavesummaryhex", "channels", "datetime_added",
        "mixxx_deleted", "played", "header_parsed", "filetype", "replaygain", "timesplayed", "rating", "key",
        "beats", "beats_version", "composer", "bpm_lock", "beats_sub_version", "keys", "keys_version",
        "keys_sub_version", "key_id", "grouping", "album_artist", "coverart_source", "coverart_type",
        "coverart_location", "coverart_hash", "replaygain_peak", "tracktotal", "color", "coverart_color",
        "coverart_digest", "last_played_at", "source_synchronized_ms",
    ),
    "track_locations": ("id", "location", "filename", "directory", "filesize", "fs_deleted", "needs_verification"),
    "crates": ("id", "name", "count", "show", "locked", "autodj_source"),
    "crate_tracks": ("crate_id", "track_id"),
    "cues": ("id", "track_id", "type", "position", "length", "hotcue", "label", "color"),
    "settings": ("name", "value", "locked", "hidden"),
    "Playlists": ("id", "name", "position", "hidden", "date_created", "date_modified", "locked"),
    "PlaylistTracks": ("id", "playlist_id", "track_id", "position", "pl_datetime_added"),
    "track_analysis": ("id", "track_id", "type", "description", "version", "created", "data_checksum"),
}


def capabilities_for(version: int) -> frozenset[str]:
    caps: set[str] = set()
    for revision, added in REVISION_CAPABILITIES.items():
        if revision <= version:
            caps |= added
    return frozenset(caps)


def first_revision_with(capability: str) -> int:
    for revision in sorted(REVISION_CAPABILITIES):
        if capability in REVISION_CAPABILITIES[revision]:
            return revision
    raise KeyError(capability)


def read_schema_version(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> str | None:
    row = conn.execute("SELECT value FROM settings WHERE name = 'mixxx.schema.version'").fetchone()
    return None if row is None else row[0]


def schema_info(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, ceiling: int = SCHEMA_CEILING) -> SchemaInfo:
    try:
        raw = read_schema_version(conn)
    except sqlite3.Error as exc:
        raw = None
        unreadable = str(exc)
    else:
        unreadable = ""
    try:
        version: int | None = int(raw) if raw is not None else None
    except ValueError:
        version = None
    if version is None:
        message = unreadable or f"schema version row is missing or unreadable (found {raw!r})"
        return SchemaInfo(None, raw, ceiling, frozenset(), "missing", message)
    caps = capabilities_for(version)
    if version > ceiling:
        verdict, message = "unsupported", f"schema revision {version} is above the supported ceiling {ceiling}"
    elif version < SCHEMA_FLOOR:
        verdict, message = "unsupported", f"schema revision {version} is below the lowest known revision {SCHEMA_FLOOR}"
    elif version in VERIFIED_REVISIONS:
        verdict, message = "supported", f"schema revision {version} is supported"
    else:
        verdict, message = "unverified", f"schema revision {version} is allowed but has never been exercised against a real database"
    return SchemaInfo(version, raw, ceiling, caps, verdict, message)


def require_schema(info: SchemaInfo, *, capability: str | None = None) -> list[WarningMessage]:
    """Raise SchemaUnsupportedError when the schema cannot be mutated; return warnings otherwise."""
    if info.verdict in ("missing", "unsupported"):
        raise SchemaUnsupportedError(info.message, version=info.version, ceiling=info.ceiling)
    if capability is not None and capability not in info.capabilities:
        needs = first_revision_with(capability)
        raise SchemaUnsupportedError(
            f"{capability} needs schema revision {needs}; this database is revision {info.version}",
            version=info.version, ceiling=info.ceiling, capability=capability, needs=needs,
        )
    if info.verdict == "unverified":
        return [WarningMessage("schema_unverified", info.message, {"version": info.version})]
    return []


def has_column(conn: sqlite3.Connection, table: str, column: str) -> bool:
    return any(row[1] == column for row in conn.execute(f"PRAGMA table_info({table})"))
```

**Files:**

- Generate (committed): `tests/fixtures/mixxxdb.sqlite`, `tests/fixtures/mixxxdb-42.sqlite`, `tests/fixtures/manifest.json`, `tests/fixtures/manifest-42.json`, `tests/fixtures/audio/*.{aiff,mp3,flac}`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (conftest): `Sandbox` dataclass with `settings_dir: Path`, `db: Path`, `audio: Path`, `backup_dir: Path`, `.connect()` (read-only connection), `.query(sql, *params) -> list[tuple]`, `.scalar(sql, *params)`, `.run(*args) -> CliResult` (prepends `--settings-path` and `--backup-dir`), `.library(**kwargs) -> Library` (from Task 10 on). `CliResult(code: int, stdout: str, stderr: str)` with `.json` (parsed stdout) and `.error` (parsed stderr). Fixtures `sandbox`, `sandbox_42` (with `schema.SCHEMA_CEILING` monkeypatched to 42), `manifest`, `manifest_42`. Module-level `run_cli(*args) -> CliResult`.
- Produces (manifest.json keys, all ints or lists of ints unless noted): `tracks`, `tracks_by_ext` (`{"aiff": [...], "mp3": [...], "flac": [...]}`), `hotcue_track`, `hotcue_rate` (samplerate × channels), `hotcue_ids` (`{"slot0": id, "zero_colour": id, "dup_a": id, "dup_b": id}`), `key_mismatch_track`, `coloured_track`, `unanalyzed_tracks`, `real_position_cue_track`, `crates` (`{"loops": id, "locked": id, "empty": id, "original": [ids]}`), `crate_members` (`{"loops": [...], "locked": [...]}`), `audio` (`{"<track id>": "NN-slug.ext"}`).
- Produces (manifest-42.json): `crates` (`{"genres": id, "bass": id, "dubstep": id, "dupe_a": id, "dupe_b": id}`), `crate_members` (`{"bass": [...], "dubstep": [...]}`).

**Privacy note:** the fixture keeps the artist/title/album text of about twenty real tracks. - [ ] **Step 1: Write `scripts/make-fixture.py`**

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Trim a real Mixxx database into the committed test fixture (spec §12.2).

    .venv/bin/python scripts/make-fixture.py trim ~/.mixxx/mixxxdb.sqlite tests/fixtures [--tracks 20]
    .venv/bin/python scripts/make-fixture.py nested tests/fixtures/mixxxdb.sqlite tests/fixtures/mixxxdb-42.sqlite

`trim` copies the source read-only, keeps ~N tracks chosen by rule (not by id), rewrites their
locations to one-second silent audio files generated with ffmpeg and tagged from the database row,
and injects the anomalies doctor must find: hotcues with colour 0 and a duplicate slot, a key/key_id
mismatch, unanalyzed tracks, plus three synthetic crates. `nested` applies PR #16871's revisions 41 and
42 and builds a three-level crate tree with two same-named roots. Both write a manifest JSON.
Refuses to run while Mixxx is running. Requires ffmpeg on PATH and mutagen (dev venv).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import re
import shutil
import sqlite3
import subprocess
import sys
import unicodedata
from pathlib import Path

try:
    from mutagen import id3
    from mutagen.aiff import AIFF
    from mutagen.flac import FLAC
    from mutagen.mp3 import MP3
except ImportError:  # pragma: no cover
    sys.exit("mutagen missing: run with .venv/bin/python")

AUDIO_REL = "tests/fixtures/audio"
QUOTAS = {"aiff": 8, "mp3": 6, "flac": 4}
ID3_FRAMES = {"artist": "TPE1", "title": "TIT2", "album": "TALB", "album_artist": "TPE2", "year": "TDRC",
              "genre": "TCON", "composer": "TCOM", "grouping": "TIT1"}
VORBIS_TAGS = {"artist": "ARTIST", "title": "TITLE", "album": "ALBUM", "album_artist": "ALBUMARTIST",
               "year": "DATE", "genre": "GENRE", "composer": "COMPOSER", "grouping": "GROUPING",
               "tracknumber": "TRACKNUMBER", "tracktotal": "TRACKTOTAL", "comment": "COMMENT",
               "bpm": "BPM", "key": "INITIALKEY"}


def refuse_if_mixxx_running() -> None:
    if shutil.which("pgrep") and subprocess.run(["pgrep", "-x", "mixxx"], capture_output=True).returncode == 0:
        sys.exit("Mixxx is running; close it first")


def slug(text: str | None) -> str:
    ascii_text = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", text or "track").encode("ascii", "ignore").decode()
    return (re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9]+", "-", ascii_text).strip("-").lower()[:24]) or "track"


def pick_tracks(conn: sqlite3.Connection, total: int) -> list[int]:
    keep: list[int] = []
    filetype = dict(conn.execute("SELECT id, filetype FROM library"))

    def add(sql: str, *params) -> None:
        for (tid,) in conn.execute(sql, params):
            if tid not in keep and len(keep) < total:
                keep.append(tid)

    add("SELECT DISTINCT track_id FROM cues WHERE typeof(position) = 'real' ORDER BY track_id LIMIT 1")
    add("SELECT DISTINCT track_id FROM cues WHERE type = 4 AND hotcue >= 0 ORDER BY track_id LIMIT 1")
    add("SELECT id FROM library WHERE beats IS NULL OR bpm = 0 ORDER BY id LIMIT 2")
    for ext, quota in QUOTAS.items():
        have = sum(1 for t in keep if filetype.get(t) == ext)
        add("SELECT l.id FROM library l WHERE l.filetype = ? AND l.beats IS NOT NULL "
            "AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM cues c WHERE c.track_id = l.id) ORDER BY l.id LIMIT ?", ext, max(0, quota - have))
    add("SELECT id FROM library ORDER BY id")
    return sorted(keep)


def trim(conn: sqlite3.Connection, keep: list[int]) -> None:
    ids = ",".join(map(str, keep))
    conn.executescript(f"""
        DELETE FROM library WHERE id NOT IN ({ids});
        DELETE FROM track_locations WHERE id NOT IN (SELECT location FROM library);
        DELETE FROM cues WHERE track_id NOT IN ({ids});
        DELETE FROM crate_tracks WHERE track_id NOT IN ({ids});
        DELETE FROM PlaylistTracks WHERE track_id NOT IN ({ids});
        DELETE FROM track_analysis WHERE track_id NOT IN ({ids});
        DELETE FROM LibraryHashes;
        DELETE FROM directories;
        INSERT INTO directories (directory) VALUES ('{AUDIO_REL}');
        INSERT INTO LibraryHashes (directory_path, hash, directory_deleted, needs_verification) VALUES ('{AUDIO_REL}', 0, 0, 0);
        UPDATE library SET wavesummaryhex = NULL, coverart_digest = NULL, coverart_location = '', coverart_hash = 0;
        DELETE FROM settings WHERE name LIKE 'mixxx.db.model.%';
    """)


def make_audio(ext: str, out: Path) -> None:
    codec = {"aiff": ["-c:a", "pcm_s16be"], "mp3": ["-c:a", "libmp3lame", "-b:a", "128k"], "flac": ["-c:a", "flac"]}[ext]
    subprocess.run(["ffmpeg", "-loglevel", "error", "-y", "-f", "lavfi", "-i", "anullsrc=r=44100:cl=stereo",
                    "-t", "1", *codec, str(out)], check=True)


def write_tags(path: Path, ext: str, row: sqlite3.Row) -> None:
    def present(value) -> bool:
        return value not in (None, "", "//", 0, 0.0)

    if ext == "flac":
        flac = FLAC(path)
        flac.delete()
        for field_name, tag in VORBIS_TAGS.items():
            value = row[field_name]
            if present(value):
                flac[tag] = [format(value, "g") if isinstance(value, float) else str(value)]
        flac.save()
        return
    audio = AIFF(path) if ext == "aiff" else MP3(path)
    if audio.tags is None:
        audio.add_tags()
    for key in list(audio.tags.keys()):
        del audio.tags[key]
    for field_name, frame in ID3_FRAMES.items():
        if present(row[field_name]):
            audio.tags.add(getattr(id3, frame)(encoding=3, text=[str(row[field_name])]))
    if present(row["tracknumber"]):
        text = f"{row['tracknumber']}/{row['tracktotal']}" if present(row["tracktotal"]) else str(row["tracknumber"])
        audio.tags.add(id3.TRCK(encoding=3, text=[text]))
    if present(row["comment"]):
        audio.tags.add(id3.COMM(encoding=3, lang="eng", desc="", text=[row["comment"]]))
    if present(row["bpm"]):
        audio.tags.add(id3.TBPM(encoding=3, text=[format(row["bpm"], "g")]))
    if present(row["key"]):
        audio.tags.add(id3.TKEY(encoding=3, text=[row["key"]]))
    audio.save()


def relocate(conn: sqlite3.Connection, keep: list[int], audio_dir: Path) -> dict[str, str]:
    audio_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    for old in audio_dir.iterdir():
        old.unlink()
    names: dict[str, str] = {}
    for n, tid in enumerate(keep, 1):
        row = conn.execute("SELECT l.*, tl.id AS tl_id FROM library l JOIN track_locations tl ON tl.id = l.location "
                           "WHERE l.id = ?", (tid,)).fetchone()
        ext = row["filetype"]
        name = f"{n:02d}-{slug(row['title'])}.{ext}"
        path = audio_dir / name
        make_audio(ext, path)
        write_tags(path, ext, row)
        conn.execute("UPDATE track_locations SET location = ?, filename = ?, directory = ?, filesize = ?, "
                     "fs_deleted = 0, needs_verification = 0 WHERE id = ?",
                     (f"{AUDIO_REL}/{name}", name, AUDIO_REL, path.stat().st_size, row["tl_id"]))
        names[str(tid)] = name
    return names


def inject(conn: sqlite3.Connection, keep: list[int]) -> dict:
    analysed = [r[0] for r in conn.execute("SELECT id FROM library WHERE beats IS NOT NULL ORDER BY id")]
    hot, mismatch = analysed[0], analysed[1]
    rate = conn.execute("SELECT samplerate * channels FROM library WHERE id = ?", (hot,)).fetchone()[0]

    def cue(slot: int, seconds: float, label: str, colour: int) -> int:
        cur = conn.execute("INSERT INTO cues (track_id, type, position, length, hotcue, label, color) "
                           "VALUES (?, 1, ?, 0, ?, ?, ?)", (hot, seconds * rate, slot, label, colour))
        return cur.lastrowid

    hotcue_ids = {"slot0": cue(0, 1.0, "drop", 0xC50A08), "zero_colour": cue(1, 2.5, "zero colour", 0),
                  "dup_a": cue(2, 3.0, "dup a", 0xFF8000), "dup_b": cue(2, 4.0, "dup b", 0x00FF00)}
    conn.execute("UPDATE library SET key = 'Am', key_id = 17 WHERE id = ?", (mismatch,))  # Am is 22
    conn.execute("UPDATE library SET color = ? WHERE id = ?", (0xFF8000, hot))

    def crate(name: str, **flags) -> int:
        cols = ["name", *flags]
        cur = conn.execute(f"INSERT INTO crates ({', '.join(cols)}) VALUES ({', '.join('?' * len(cols))})",
                           (name, *flags.values()))
        return cur.lastrowid

    original = [r[0] for r in conn.execute("SELECT id FROM crates ORDER BY id")]
    loops, locked, empty = crate("Fixture Loops"), crate("Locked Crate", locked=1), crate("Empty Crate", show=0)
    members = {"loops": keep[:5], "locked": keep[5:7]}
    conn.executemany("INSERT INTO crate_tracks (crate_id, track_id) VALUES (?, ?)",
                     [(loops, t) for t in members["loops"]] + [(locked, t) for t in members["locked"]])
    return {
        "hotcue_track": hot, "hotcue_rate": rate, "hotcue_ids": hotcue_ids, "key_mismatch_track": mismatch,
        "coloured_track": hot,
        "unanalyzed_tracks": [r[0] for r in conn.execute("SELECT id FROM library WHERE beats IS NULL OR bpm = 0 ORDER BY id")],
        "real_position_cue_track": (conn.execute("SELECT track_id FROM cues WHERE typeof(position) = 'real' "
                                                 "ORDER BY track_id LIMIT 1").fetchone() or [None])[0],
        "crates": {"loops": loops, "locked": locked, "empty": empty, "original": original},
        "crate_members": members,
    }


def cmd_trim(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    refuse_if_mixxx_running()
    out_dir = Path(args.out_dir)
    out_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    target = out_dir / "mixxxdb.sqlite"
    if target.exists():
        target.unlink()
    source = sqlite3.connect(f"{Path(args.source).resolve().as_uri()}?mode=ro", uri=True)
    dest = sqlite3.connect(target)
    source.backup(dest)
    source.close()
    dest.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    keep = pick_tracks(dest, args.tracks)
    trim(dest, keep)
    audio = relocate(dest, keep, out_dir / "audio")
    manifest = inject(dest, keep)
    by_ext: dict[str, list[int]] = {}
    for tid, ext in dest.execute("SELECT id, filetype FROM library ORDER BY id"):
        by_ext.setdefault(ext, []).append(tid)
    manifest.update({"tracks": keep, "tracks_by_ext": by_ext, "audio": audio})
    dest.commit()
    dest.execute("VACUUM")
    dest.close()
    (out_dir / "manifest.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n")
    print(f"wrote {target} with {len(keep)} tracks; manifest at {out_dir / 'manifest.json'}")


REV_41_42 = """
ALTER TABLE crates ADD COLUMN parent_id INTEGER REFERENCES crates(id) DEFAULT NULL;
CREATE TABLE crates_new (
    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,
    name varchar(48) NOT NULL,
    count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    show INTEGER DEFAULT 1, locked INTEGER DEFAULT 0, autodj_source INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
    parent_id INTEGER REFERENCES crates(id) DEFAULT NULL,
    UNIQUE (parent_id, name));
INSERT INTO crates_new (id, name, count, show, locked, autodj_source, parent_id)
    SELECT id, name, count, show, locked, autodj_source, parent_id FROM crates;
DROP TABLE crates;
ALTER TABLE crates_new RENAME TO crates;
UPDATE settings SET value = '42' WHERE name IN ('mixxx.schema.version', 'mixxx.schema.last_used_version');
"""


def cmd_nested(args: argparse.Namespace) -> None:
    src, dst = Path(args.source), Path(args.dest)
    shutil.copy(src, dst)
    conn = sqlite3.connect(dst)
    conn.executescript(REV_41_42)
    keep = [r[0] for r in conn.execute("SELECT id FROM library ORDER BY id")]

    def crate(name: str, parent: int | None) -> int:
        return conn.execute("INSERT INTO crates (name, parent_id) VALUES (?, ?)", (name, parent)).lastrowid

    genres = crate("Genres", None)
    bass = crate("Bass", genres)
    dubstep = crate("Dubstep", bass)
    dupe_a, dupe_b = crate("Dupe", None), crate("Dupe", None)  # NULL parent_id defeats UNIQUE (parent_id, name)
    members = {"bass": keep[:3], "dubstep": keep[3:5]}
    conn.executemany("INSERT INTO crate_tracks (crate_id, track_id) VALUES (?, ?)",
                     [(bass, t) for t in members["bass"]] + [(dubstep, t) for t in members["dubstep"]])
    conn.commit()
    conn.execute("VACUUM")
    conn.close()
    manifest = {"crates": {"genres": genres, "bass": bass, "dubstep": dubstep, "dupe_a": dupe_a, "dupe_b": dupe_b},
                "crate_members": members}
    (dst.parent / "manifest-42.json").write_text(json.dumps(manifest, indent=2, sort_keys=True) + "\n")
    print(f"wrote {dst} at schema 42")


def main() -> None:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    trim_p = sub.add_parser("trim")
    trim_p.add_argument("source")
    trim_p.add_argument("out_dir")
    trim_p.add_argument("--tracks", type=int, default=20)
    trim_p.set_defaults(func=cmd_trim)
    nested_p = sub.add_parser("nested")
    nested_p.add_argument("source")
    nested_p.add_argument("dest")
    nested_p.set_defaults(func=cmd_nested)
    args = parser.parse_args()
    args.func(args)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Generate the fixtures**

Mixxx must be closed (the script refuses otherwise).

```sh
.venv/bin/python scripts/make-fixture.py trim ~/.mixxx/mixxxdb.sqlite tests/fixtures --tracks 20
.venv/bin/python scripts/make-fixture.py nested tests/fixtures/mixxxdb.sqlite tests/fixtures/mixxxdb-42.sqlite
ls -la tests/fixtures tests/fixtures/audio
sqlite3 tests/fixtures/mixxxdb.sqlite "select count(*) from library; select name, value from settings where name='mixxx.schema.version'; select type, count(*) from cues group by type;"
sqlite3 tests/fixtures/mixxxdb-42.sqlite "select id, name, parent_id from crates; select value from settings where name='mixxx.schema.version';"
```

Expected: 20 library rows, version `39`, cue type 1 count 4, the 42 fixture lists `Genres`/`Bass`/`Dubstep`/`Dupe`/`Dupe` with parent ids and version `42`. Each audio file is ≈ 9–180 KB.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `tests/conftest.py`**

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import io
import json
import shutil
import sqlite3
from contextlib import closing, redirect_stderr, redirect_stdout
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pytest

FIXTURES = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "fixtures"
AUDIO_REL = "tests/fixtures/audio"
ENV_VARS = ("MIXXXLIB_DB", "MIXXXLIB_SETTINGS_PATH", "MIXXXLIB_BACKUP_DIR",
            "MIXXXLIB_BACKUP_TTL_DAYS", "MIXXXLIB_BACKUP_KEEP_MIN", "MIXXXLIB_DEBUG")


@dataclass
class CliResult:
    code: int
    stdout: str
    stderr: str

    @property
    def json(self) -> Any:
        return json.loads(self.stdout)

    @property
    def error(self) -> Any:
        return json.loads(self.stderr)


def run_cli(*args: str) -> CliResult:
    from mixxxlib.cli import main

    out, err = io.StringIO(), io.StringIO()
    with redirect_stdout(out), redirect_stderr(err):
        code = main(list(args))
    return CliResult(code, out.getvalue(), err.getvalue())


@dataclass
class Sandbox:
    settings_dir: Path
    db: Path
    audio: Path
    backup_dir: Path
    extra_args: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    def connect(self) -> sqlite3.Connection:
        return sqlite3.connect(f"{self.db.resolve().as_uri()}?mode=ro", uri=True)

    def query(self, sql: str, *params: Any) -> list[tuple]:
        with closing(self.connect()) as conn:
            return [tuple(r) for r in conn.execute(sql, params).fetchall()]

    def scalar(self, sql: str, *params: Any) -> Any:
        return self.query(sql, *params)[0][0]

    def run(self, *args: str) -> CliResult:
        return run_cli("--settings-path", str(self.settings_dir), "--backup-dir", str(self.backup_dir),
                       *self.extra_args, *args)

    def library(self, **kwargs: Any):
        from mixxxlib.library import Library

        return Library.open(settings_path=str(self.settings_dir), backup_dir=str(self.backup_dir), **kwargs)


def materialise(fixture_name: str, root: Path) -> Sandbox:
    settings = root / "settings"
    settings.mkdir()
    audio = root / "audio"
    shutil.copytree(FIXTURES / "audio", audio)
    db = settings / "mixxxdb.sqlite"
    shutil.copy(FIXTURES / fixture_name, db)
    shutil.copy(FIXTURES / "mixxx.cfg", settings / "mixxx.cfg")
    with closing(sqlite3.connect(db, autocommit=True)) as conn:
        conn.execute("UPDATE track_locations SET location = ? || substr(location, ?), directory = ?",
                     (f"{audio}/", len(AUDIO_REL) + 2, str(audio)))
        conn.execute("UPDATE directories SET directory = ?", (str(audio),))
        conn.execute("UPDATE LibraryHashes SET directory_path = ?", (str(audio),))
    return Sandbox(settings_dir=settings, db=db, audio=audio, backup_dir=root / "backups")


@pytest.fixture
def clean_env(monkeypatch):
    for var in ENV_VARS:
        monkeypatch.delenv(var, raising=False)


@pytest.fixture
def manifest() -> dict:
    return json.loads((FIXTURES / "manifest.json").read_text())


@pytest.fixture
def manifest_42() -> dict:
    return json.loads((FIXTURES / "manifest-42.json").read_text())


@pytest.fixture
def sandbox(tmp_path, clean_env) -> Sandbox:
    return materialise("mixxxdb.sqlite", tmp_path)


@pytest.fixture
def sandbox_42(tmp_path, clean_env, monkeypatch) -> Sandbox:
    from mixxxlib.db import schema

    monkeypatch.setattr(schema, "SCHEMA_CEILING", 42)
    return materialise("mixxxdb-42.sqlite", tmp_path)
```

```python
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM cues WHERE typeof(position) = 'real'") >= 1
```

```python
import os
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from mixxxlib.errors import ValidationError
from mixxxlib.safety import backup

NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 8, 14, 22, 33, tzinfo=timezone.utc)


def test_backup_name_uses_utc_basic_timestamp_and_hyphenated_label():
    assert backup.backup_name("crate add", NOW) == "mixxxdb-20261008T142233Z-crate-add.sqlite"
    assert backup.backup_name("manual label!", NOW) == "mixxxdb-20261008T142233Z-manual-label.sqlite"


def test_retention_from_env_and_defaults():
    assert backup.Retention.from_env({}) == backup.Retention(30, 10)
    assert backup.Retention.from_env({"MIXXXLIB_BACKUP_TTL_DAYS": "7", "MIXXXLIB_BACKUP_KEEP_MIN": "3"}) == backup.Retention(7, 3)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        backup.Retention.from_env({"MIXXXLIB_BACKUP_TTL_DAYS": "soon"})


def _touch(path: Path, when: datetime) -> None:
    path.write_bytes(b"x")
    os.utime(path, (when.timestamp(), when.timestamp()))


def test_prune_keeps_the_floor_even_when_everything_is_stale(tmp_path):
    for i in range(12):
        _touch(tmp_path / f"mixxxdb-2026010{i % 10}T00000{i}Z-x.sqlite", NOW - timedelta(days=100 + i))
    removed = backup.prune(tmp_path, backup.Retention(ttl_days=30, keep_min=10), now=NOW)
    assert len(removed) == 2
    assert len(list(tmp_path.glob("mixxxdb-*.sqlite"))) == 10
    assert all("00000" in p.name for p in removed)  # the two oldest by mtime went


def test_prune_keeps_recent_files_beyond_the_floor(tmp_path):
    for i in range(15):
        _touch(tmp_path / f"mixxxdb-20261001T0000{i:02d}Z-x.sqlite", NOW - timedelta(days=i))
    removed = backup.prune(tmp_path, backup.Retention(ttl_days=30, keep_min=10), now=NOW)
    assert removed == []


def test_prune_treats_tag_snapshots_as_their_own_series(tmp_path):
    for i in range(11):
        _touch(tmp_path / f"tags-20260101T0000{i:02d}Z-7.json", NOW - timedelta(days=200 + i))
    _touch(tmp_path / "mixxxdb-20260101T000000Z-x.sqlite", NOW - timedelta(days=200))
    removed = backup.prune(tmp_path, backup.Retention(30, 10), now=NOW)
    assert [p.suffix for p in removed] == [".json"]
    assert (tmp_path / "mixxxdb-20260101T000000Z-x.sqlite").exists()


def test_create_backup_is_a_consistent_copy(tmp_path):
    db = tmp_path / "db.sqlite"
    conn = sqlite3.connect(db, autocommit=True)
    conn.execute("CREATE TABLE t (x)")
    conn.executemany("INSERT INTO t VALUES (?)", [(i,) for i in range(100)])
    path = backup.create_backup(conn, tmp_path / "backups", "crate add", now=NOW)
    assert path == tmp_path / "backups" / "mixxxdb-20261008T142233Z-crate-add.sqlite"
    copy = sqlite3.connect(path)
    assert copy.execute("SELECT count(*) FROM t").fetchone()[0] == 100
    assert copy.execute("PRAGMA integrity_check").fetchone()[0] == "ok"
    second = backup.create_backup(conn, tmp_path / "backups", "crate add", now=NOW)
    assert second.name == "mixxxdb-20261008T142233Z-crate-add-2.sqlite"


def test_list_backups_newest_first_with_kind_and_age(tmp_path):
    _touch(tmp_path / "mixxxdb-20261001T000000Z-a.sqlite", NOW - timedelta(days=7))
    _touch(tmp_path / "tags-20261007T000000Z-9.json", NOW - timedelta(days=1))
    entries = backup.list_backups(tmp_path, now=NOW)
    assert [e.kind for e in entries] == ["tags", "database"]
    assert entries[0].age_seconds == 86400
    assert entries[1].size == 1
    assert backup.list_backups(tmp_path / "missing") == []
```

```python
import sqlite3

import pytest

from mixxxlib.errors import MixxxRunningError
from mixxxlib.safety import guard


def test_probe_lock_passes_on_idle_database(sandbox):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(sandbox.db, timeout=0.2, autocommit=True)
    guard.probe_lock(conn)
    assert not conn.in_transaction


def test_probe_lock_refuses_while_another_connection_holds_exclusive(sandbox):
    holder = sqlite3.connect(sandbox.db, autocommit=True)
    holder.execute("BEGIN EXCLUSIVE")
    conn = sqlite3.connect(sandbox.db, timeout=0.2, autocommit=True)
    with pytest.raises(MixxxRunningError) as e:
        guard.probe_lock(conn)
    assert e.value.details == {"reason": "locked"}
    holder.execute("ROLLBACK")


def test_assert_mixxx_closed_uses_process_check_first(sandbox):
    conn = sqlite3.connect(sandbox.db, timeout=0.2, autocommit=True)
    with pytest.raises(MixxxRunningError) as e:
        guard.assert_mixxx_closed(conn, process_check=lambda: True)
    assert e.value.details == {"reason": "process"}
    guard.assert_mixxx_closed(conn, process_check=lambda: None)  # unknown → fall through to the lock probe


def test_process_check_returns_none_without_pgrep(monkeypatch):
    monkeypatch.setattr(guard.shutil, "which", lambda name: None)
    assert guard.mixxx_process_running() is None
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/mixxxlib/safety/guard.py`**

```python
"""Refuse to mutate while Mixxx is running (spec §7.1, §7.3 step 3)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import shutil
import sqlite3
import subprocess
from collections.abc import Callable

from mixxxlib.errors import MixxxRunningError

PROCESS_NAME = "mixxx"


def mixxx_process_running() -> bool | None:
    """Best-effort `pgrep -x mixxx`. None when pgrep is unavailable or fails."""
    if shutil.which("pgrep") is None:
        return None
    try:
        result = subprocess.run(["pgrep", "-x", PROCESS_NAME], capture_output=True, timeout=5, check=False)
    except (OSError, subprocess.TimeoutExpired):
        return None
    return result.returncode == 0


def is_locked_error(exc: sqlite3.OperationalError) -> bool:
    name = getattr(exc, "sqlite_errorname", None) or ""
    text = str(exc).lower()
    return name in ("SQLITE_BUSY", "SQLITE_LOCKED") or "locked" in text or "busy" in text


def probe_lock(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> None:
    """The authoritative probe: BEGIN IMMEDIATE then ROLLBACK. journal_mode=delete makes this reliable."""
    try:
        conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
    except sqlite3.OperationalError as exc:
        if is_locked_error(exc):
            raise MixxxRunningError(
                "the database is locked by another connection (is Mixxx running?)", reason="locked"
            ) from None
        raise
    conn.execute("ROLLBACK")


def assert_mixxx_closed(
    conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, process_check: Callable[[], bool | None] = mixxx_process_running
) -> None:
    if process_check() is True:
        raise MixxxRunningError("Mixxx is running; close it before changing the library", reason="process")
    probe_lock(conn)
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/mixxxlib/safety/backup.py`**

```python
"""VACUUM INTO backups, naming and retention (spec §7.6)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import re
import sqlite3
from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from mixxxlib.errors import DatabaseError, ValidationError
from mixxxlib.model import BackupEntry

DB_PREFIX = "mixxxdb-"
TAG_PREFIX = "tags-"


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Retention:
    ttl_days: int = 30
    keep_min: int = 10

    @classmethod
    def from_env(cls, environ: Mapping[str, str] | None = None) -> "Retention":
        env = os.environ if environ is None else environ
        values = {}
        for attr, var in (("ttl_days", "MIXXXLIB_BACKUP_TTL_DAYS"), ("keep_min", "MIXXXLIB_BACKUP_KEEP_MIN")):
            raw = env.get(var)
            if raw is None:
                continue
            try:
                values[attr] = int(raw)
            except ValueError:
                raise ValidationError(f"{var} must be an integer, got {raw!r}", variable=var, value=raw) from None
            if values[attr] < 0:
                raise ValidationError(f"{var} must be non-negative", variable=var, value=raw)
        return cls(**values)


def utc_stamp(now: datetime) -> str:
    return now.astimezone(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y%m%dT%H%M%SZ")


def _slug(label: str) -> str:
    return re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9._]+", "-", label.strip()).strip("-") or "backup"


def backup_name(label: str, now: datetime) -> str:
    return f"{DB_PREFIX}{utc_stamp(now)}-{_slug(label)}.sqlite"


def _unique(path: Path) -> Path:
    candidate, n = path, 1
    while candidate.exists():
        n += 1
        candidate = path.with_name(f"{path.stem}-{n}{path.suffix}")
    return candidate


def create_backup(conn: sqlite3.Connection, backup_dir: Path, label: str, *, now: datetime | None = None) -> Path:
    """Copy the open database with VACUUM INTO (never cp: a copied file can hold a torn page set)."""
    stamp = now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    try:
        backup_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    except OSError as exc:
        raise DatabaseError(f"cannot create backup directory {backup_dir}: {exc}", backup_dir=str(backup_dir)) from exc
    target = _unique(backup_dir / backup_name(label, stamp))
    literal = str(target).replace("'", "''")
    try:
        conn.execute(f"VACUUM INTO '{literal}'")
    except sqlite3.Error as exc:
        raise DatabaseError(f"backup failed: {exc}", backup=str(target)) from exc
    return target


def _entries(backup_dir: Path) -> list[tuple[Path, str, float]]:
    if not backup_dir.is_dir():
        return []
    found = []
    for path in backup_dir.iterdir():
        if path.name.startswith(DB_PREFIX) and path.suffix == ".sqlite":
            found.append((path, "database", path.stat().st_mtime))
        elif path.name.startswith(TAG_PREFIX) and path.suffix == ".json":
            found.append((path, "tags", path.stat().st_mtime))
    found.sort(key=lambda e: (-e[2], e[0].name))
    return found


def list_backups(backup_dir: Path, *, now: datetime | None = None) -> list[BackupEntry]:
    current = (now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)).timestamp()
    return [
        BackupEntry(
            path=str(path), name=path.name, kind=kind, size=path.stat().st_size,
            modified=datetime.fromtimestamp(mtime, timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"),
            age_seconds=int(current - mtime),
        )
        for path, kind, mtime in _entries(backup_dir)
    ]


def prune(backup_dir: Path, retention: Retention, *, now: datetime | None = None) -> list[Path]:
    """Delete entries older than ttl_days, but always keep the keep_min newest of each kind."""
    cutoff = (now or datetime.now(timezone.utc)).timestamp() - retention.ttl_days * 86400
    removed: list[Path] = []
    for kind in ("database", "tags"):
        series = [e for e in _entries(backup_dir) if e[1] == kind]
        for path, _, mtime in series[retention.keep_min:]:
            if mtime < cutoff:
                path.unlink()
                removed.append(path)
    return removed
```

```python
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime, timezone

import pytest

from mixxxlib.db import connection as c
from mixxxlib.errors import DatabaseError, MixxxRunningError, NotFoundError, SchemaUnsupportedError, TagError

NOW = datetime(2026, 10, 8, 14, 22, 33, tzinfo=timezone.utc)
NEVER_RUNNING = lambda: False  # noqa: E731


def _rename_plan(new_name, crate_id):
    def build(conn):
        return c.MutationPlan(
            statements=[c.Statement("UPDATE crates SET name = ? WHERE id = ?", (new_name, crate_id))],
            data={"crate_id": crate_id},
            after_commit=lambda conn: {"name": conn.execute("SELECT name FROM crates WHERE id = ?", (crate_id,)).fetchone()[0]},
        )
    return build


def test_open_ro_refuses_writes_and_missing_paths(sandbox, tmp_path):
    conn = c.open_ro(sandbox.db)
    with pytest.raises(sqlite3.OperationalError):
        conn.execute("INSERT INTO crates (name) VALUES ('x')")
    with pytest.raises(NotFoundError) as e:
        c.open_ro(tmp_path / "nope.sqlite")
    assert e.value.details["path"].endswith("nope.sqlite")


def test_render_statement_binds_parameters():
    st = c.Statement("INSERT INTO cues (track_id, label, position, color) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)", (7, "it's", 1.5, None))
    assert c.render_statement(st) == "INSERT INTO cues (track_id, label, position, color) VALUES (7, 'it''s', 1.5, NULL)"
    assert c.render_statement(c.Statement("UPDATE library SET source_synchronized_ms = ?", (c.Deferred("mtime_ms"),))) == \
        "UPDATE library SET source_synchronized_ms = <mtime_ms>"


def test_dry_run_changes_nothing_and_takes_no_backup(sandbox, manifest):
    crate_id = manifest["crates"]["loops"]
    conn = c.open_rw(sandbox.db)
    result = c.run_mutation(conn, label="crate rename", backup_dir=sandbox.backup_dir, build=_rename_plan("Renamed", crate_id),
                            schema_ceiling=40, dry_run=True, process_check=NEVER_RUNNING)
    assert result.dry_run is True and result.backup is None and result.integrity is None
    assert result.statements == [f"UPDATE crates SET name = 'Renamed' WHERE id = {crate_id}"]
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT name FROM crates WHERE id = ?", crate_id) == "Fixture Loops"
    assert not sandbox.backup_dir.exists()


def test_successful_mutation_backs_up_commits_and_verifies(sandbox, manifest):
    crate_id = manifest["crates"]["loops"]
    conn = c.open_rw(sandbox.db)
    result = c.run_mutation(conn, label="crate rename", backup_dir=sandbox.backup_dir, build=_rename_plan("Renamed", crate_id),
                            schema_ceiling=40, process_check=NEVER_RUNNING, now=NOW)
    assert result.backup == str(sandbox.backup_dir / "mixxxdb-20261008T142233Z-crate-rename.sqlite")
    assert result.integrity == "ok" and result.dry_run is False
    assert result.data == {"name": "Renamed"}
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT name FROM crates WHERE id = ?", crate_id) == "Renamed"
    backup = sqlite3.connect(result.backup)
    assert backup.execute("SELECT name FROM crates WHERE id = ?", (crate_id,)).fetchone()[0] == "Fixture Loops"


def test_failed_statement_rolls_back_and_names_the_backup(sandbox, manifest):
    crate_id = manifest["crates"]["loops"]

    def build(conn):
        return c.MutationPlan(statements=[
            c.Statement("UPDATE crates SET name = ? WHERE id = ?", ("Half", crate_id)),
            c.Statement("INSERT INTO no_such_table VALUES (1)"),
        ])

    conn = c.open_rw(sandbox.db)
    with pytest.raises(DatabaseError) as e:
        c.run_mutation(conn, label="x", backup_dir=sandbox.backup_dir, build=build, schema_ceiling=40, process_check=NEVER_RUNNING)
    assert e.value.details["backup"].endswith(".sqlite")
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT name FROM crates WHERE id = ?", crate_id) == "Fixture Loops"
    assert not conn.in_transaction


def test_validation_errors_from_build_happen_before_any_backup(sandbox):
    def build(conn):
        raise NotFoundError("crate 999 does not exist", crate_id=999)

    conn = c.open_rw(sandbox.db)
    with pytest.raises(NotFoundError):
        c.run_mutation(conn, label="x", backup_dir=sandbox.backup_dir, build=build, schema_ceiling=40, process_check=NEVER_RUNNING)
    assert not sandbox.backup_dir.exists() and not conn.in_transaction


def test_lock_and_process_guards(sandbox, manifest):
    conn = c.open_rw(sandbox.db)
    with pytest.raises(MixxxRunningError):
        c.run_mutation(conn, label="x", backup_dir=sandbox.backup_dir, build=_rename_plan("R", 1), schema_ceiling=40, process_check=lambda: True)
    holder = sqlite3.connect(sandbox.db, autocommit=True)
    holder.execute("BEGIN EXCLUSIVE")
    with pytest.raises(MixxxRunningError):
        c.run_mutation(conn, label="x", backup_dir=sandbox.backup_dir, build=_rename_plan("R", 1), schema_ceiling=40, process_check=NEVER_RUNNING)
    holder.execute("ROLLBACK")
    assert not sandbox.backup_dir.exists()


def test_schema_guard_refuses_41_on_ceiling_40(sandbox, manifest):
    with sqlite3.connect(sandbox.db, autocommit=True) as w:
        w.execute("UPDATE settings SET value = '41' WHERE name = 'mixxx.schema.version'")
    conn = c.open_rw(sandbox.db)
    with pytest.raises(SchemaUnsupportedError) as e:
        c.run_mutation(conn, label="x", backup_dir=sandbox.backup_dir, build=_rename_plan("R", 1), schema_ceiling=40, process_check=NEVER_RUNNING)
    assert e.value.details == {"version": 41, "ceiling": 40}
    with sqlite3.connect(sandbox.db, autocommit=True) as w:
        w.execute("UPDATE settings SET value = '39' WHERE name = 'mixxx.schema.version'")


class RecordingAction:
    def __init__(self, name, fail=False):
        self.name, self.fail, self.applied, self.rolled_back = name, fail, False, False

    def describe(self):
        return f"write {self.name}"

    def apply(self):
        if self.fail:
            raise TagError(f"{self.name} exploded")
        self.applied = True
        return {"mtime_ms": 1234}

    def rollback(self):
        self.rolled_back = True


def test_file_actions_run_before_the_transaction_and_feed_deferred_params(sandbox, manifest):
    track_id = manifest["tracks"][0]
    action = RecordingAction("a.aiff")

    def build(conn):
        return c.MutationPlan(
            statements=[c.Statement("UPDATE library SET source_synchronized_ms = ? WHERE id = ?", (c.Deferred("mtime_ms"), track_id))],
            file_actions=[action],
        )

    conn = c.open_rw(sandbox.db)
    result = c.run_mutation(conn, label="track set", backup_dir=sandbox.backup_dir, build=build, schema_ceiling=40, process_check=NEVER_RUNNING)
    assert action.applied and result.file_operations == ["write a.aiff"]
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT source_synchronized_ms FROM library WHERE id = ?", track_id) == 1234


def test_failed_file_action_rolls_back_earlier_actions_and_skips_the_database(sandbox, manifest):
    first, second = RecordingAction("one"), RecordingAction("two", fail=True)

    def build(conn):
        return c.MutationPlan(statements=[c.Statement("UPDATE crates SET name = 'X' WHERE id = ?", (manifest["crates"]["loops"],))],
                              file_actions=[first, second])

    conn = c.open_rw(sandbox.db)
    with pytest.raises(TagError):
        c.run_mutation(conn, label="track set", backup_dir=sandbox.backup_dir, build=build, schema_ceiling=40, process_check=NEVER_RUNNING)
    assert first.rolled_back and not second.applied
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT name FROM crates WHERE id = ?", manifest["crates"]["loops"]) == "Fixture Loops"


def test_database_failure_after_file_write_restores_the_file(sandbox, manifest):
    action = RecordingAction("one")

    def build(conn):
        return c.MutationPlan(statements=[c.Statement("INSERT INTO no_such_table VALUES (1)")], file_actions=[action])

    conn = c.open_rw(sandbox.db)
    with pytest.raises(DatabaseError) as e:
        c.run_mutation(conn, label="track set", backup_dir=sandbox.backup_dir, build=build, schema_ceiling=40, process_check=NEVER_RUNNING)
    assert action.rolled_back
    assert e.value.details["file_rollback"] == ["write one"]
```
