- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/mixxxlib/db/connection.py`**

```python
"""Connections and the mutation protocol (spec §7.2, §7.3).

Every mutating command, in this order: schema guard → Mixxx-running guard → validate inside a read
transaction → dry-run exit → VACUUM INTO backup → file tags → one BEGIN IMMEDIATE transaction →
PRAGMA integrity_check → prune backups.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
from collections.abc import Callable
from contextlib import contextmanager
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterator, Protocol

from mixxxlib.db import schema as schema_mod
from mixxxlib.errors import DatabaseError, MixxxRunningError, MixxxlibError, NotFoundError, TagError
from mixxxlib.model import MutationResult, WarningMessage
from mixxxlib.safety import backup as backup_mod
from mixxxlib.safety.guard import assert_mixxx_closed, is_locked_error, mixxx_process_running

BUSY_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 1.0


def _uri(path: Path, mode: str) -> str:
    return f"{Path(path).resolve().as_uri()}?mode={mode}"


def _require_file(path: Path) -> None:
    if not Path(path).is_file():
        raise NotFoundError(f"database not found at {path}", path=str(path))


def open_ro(path: Path, *, timeout: float = BUSY_TIMEOUT_SECONDS) -> sqlite3.Connection:
    """A connection that is incapable of mutation (spec §7.2)."""
    _require_file(path)
    conn = sqlite3.connect(_uri(path, "ro"), uri=True, timeout=timeout, autocommit=True)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    return conn


def open_rw(path: Path, *, timeout: float = BUSY_TIMEOUT_SECONDS) -> sqlite3.Connection:
    _require_file(path)
    conn = sqlite3.connect(_uri(path, "rw"), uri=True, timeout=timeout, autocommit=True)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    return conn


def translate(exc: sqlite3.Error) -> MixxxlibError:
    if isinstance(exc, sqlite3.OperationalError) and is_locked_error(exc):
        return MixxxRunningError("the database is locked by another connection (is Mixxx running?)", reason="locked")
    return DatabaseError(f"sqlite error: {exc}", sqlite=type(exc).__name__)


@contextmanager
def translating_errors() -> Iterator[None]:
    try:
        yield
    except sqlite3.Error as exc:
        raise translate(exc) from exc


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- statements

@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Deferred:
    """A bound parameter whose value is known only after the file step (e.g. the file's new mtime)."""

    key: str


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Statement:
    sql: str
    params: tuple[Any, ...] = ()


def quote_literal(value: Any) -> str:
    if value is None:
        return "NULL"
    if isinstance(value, Deferred):
        return f"<{value.key}>"
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        return "1" if value else "0"
    if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
        return repr(value)
    if isinstance(value, bytes):
        return f"X'{value.hex()}'"
    return "'" + str(value).replace("'", "''") + "'"


def render_statement(statement: Statement) -> str:
    parts = statement.sql.split("?")
    if len(parts) - 1 != len(statement.params):
        raise ValueError(f"{len(statement.params)} params for {len(parts) - 1} placeholders in {statement.sql!r}")
    out = parts[0]
    for param, rest in zip(statement.params, parts[1:]):
        out += quote_literal(param) + rest
    return out


def _resolve(param: Any, results: dict[str, Any]) -> Any:
    if isinstance(param, Deferred):
        if param.key not in results:
            raise DatabaseError(f"deferred parameter {param.key!r} was never produced by a file action", key=param.key)
        return results[param.key]
    return param


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- plans

class FileAction(Protocol):
    def describe(self) -> str: ...
    def apply(self) -> dict[str, Any]: ...
    def rollback(self) -> None: ...


@dataclass
class MutationPlan:
    statements: list[Statement] = field(default_factory=list)
    data: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    warnings: list[WarningMessage] = field(default_factory=list)
    file_actions: list[FileAction] = field(default_factory=list)
    after_commit: Callable[[sqlite3.Connection], dict[str, Any]] | None = None


def integrity_check(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> str:
    rows = [row[0] for row in conn.execute("PRAGMA integrity_check")]
    return "ok" if rows == ["ok"] else "; ".join(rows)


def _try_rollback(action: FileAction) -> bool:
    try:
        action.rollback()
        return True
    except Exception:  # best effort; the error we are already raising matters more
        return False


def run_mutation(
    conn: sqlite3.Connection,
    *,
    label: str,
    backup_dir: Path,
    build: Callable[[sqlite3.Connection], MutationPlan],
    schema_ceiling: int,
    requires: str | None = None,
    dry_run: bool = False,
    retention: backup_mod.Retention | None = None,
    process_check: Callable[[], bool | None] | None = None,
    now: datetime | None = None,
) -> MutationResult:
    """Run spec §7.3 steps 2–11 around `build`, which validates and returns the plan."""
    # 2. schema guard
    info = schema_mod.schema_info(conn, ceiling=schema_ceiling)
    warnings = list(schema_mod.require_schema(info, capability=requires))
    # 3. Mixxx-running guard
    assert_mixxx_closed(conn, process_check=process_check or mixxx_process_running)
    # 4. validate inside a read transaction; nothing is written yet
    with translating_errors():
        conn.execute("BEGIN")
        try:
            plan = build(conn)
        finally:
            conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
    warnings.extend(plan.warnings)
    rendered = [render_statement(s) for s in plan.statements]
    file_ops = [a.describe() for a in plan.file_actions]
    # 6. dry run
    if dry_run:
        return MutationResult(data=plan.data, statements=rendered, backup=None, integrity=None, dry_run=True,
                              warnings=warnings, file_operations=file_ops)
    # 7. backup, only when the database will be written
    backup_path: Path | None = None
    if plan.statements:
        backup_path = backup_mod.create_backup(conn, backup_dir, label, now=now)
    # 8. file tags first
    results: dict[str, Any] = {}
    applied: list[FileAction] = []
    try:
        for action in plan.file_actions:
            results.update(action.apply())
            applied.append(action)
    except TagError:
        for action in reversed(applied):
            _try_rollback(action)
        raise
    # 9. one transaction
    integrity: str | None = None
    if plan.statements:
        try:
            conn.execute("BEGIN IMMEDIATE")
            for statement in plan.statements:
                conn.execute(statement.sql, tuple(_resolve(p, results) for p in statement.params))
            conn.execute("COMMIT")
        except (sqlite3.Error, DatabaseError) as exc:
            try:
                conn.execute("ROLLBACK")
            except sqlite3.Error:
                pass
            restored = [a.describe() for a in reversed(applied) if _try_rollback(a)]
            err = exc if isinstance(exc, DatabaseError) else translate(exc)
            err.details.update(backup=str(backup_path), file_rollback=restored)
            raise err from exc
        # 10. verify
        integrity = integrity_check(conn)
        if integrity != "ok":
            raise DatabaseError(f"integrity_check failed after commit: {integrity}",
                                backup=str(backup_path), integrity=integrity)
        # 11. prune
        backup_mod.prune(backup_dir, retention or backup_mod.Retention.from_env(), now=now)
    data = plan.after_commit(conn) if plan.after_commit is not None else plan.data
    return MutationResult(data=data, statements=rendered, backup=str(backup_path) if backup_path else None,
                          integrity=integrity, dry_run=False, warnings=warnings, file_operations=file_ops)
```

```python
import sqlite3
import struct
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

from mixxxlib.blobs import beats
from mixxxlib.model import Beatgrid

FIXTURE_DB = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "fixtures" / "mixxxdb.sqlite"


def grid_blob(bpm: float, frame: int, extra: bytes = b"") -> bytes:
    bpm_msg = b"\x09" + struct.pack("<d", bpm)
    frame_varint = bytearray()
    value = frame
    while True:
        byte = value & 0x7F
        value >>= 7
        if value:
            frame_varint.append(byte | 0x80)
        else:
            frame_varint.append(byte)
            break
    beat_msg = b"\x08" + bytes(frame_varint) + extra
    return b"\x0a" + bytes([len(bpm_msg)]) + bpm_msg + b"\x12" + bytes([len(beat_msg)]) + beat_msg


def test_read_varint():
    assert beats.read_varint(b"\x88\x02", 0) == (264, 2)
    assert beats.read_varint(b"\x01", 0) == (1, 1)
    with pytest.raises(beats.BlobError):
        beats.read_varint(b"\x80", 0)


def test_decode_known_blob_from_the_reference_library():
    blob = bytes.fromhex("0A090900000000004060401203088802")
    assert beats.decode_beatgrid_v2(blob) == (130.0, 264)


def test_decode_tolerates_extra_optional_fields():
    blob = grid_blob(145.0, 87, extra=b"\x10\x01\x18\x02")  # enabled=true, source=2
    assert beats.decode_beatgrid_v2(blob) == (145.0, 87)


def test_decode_rejects_truncated_blob():
    with pytest.raises(beats.BlobError):
        beats.decode_beatgrid_v2(bytes.fromhex("0A0909000000"))


def test_decode_dispatches_on_version():
    grid, warnings = beats.decode(grid_blob(128.0, 1234), "BeatGrid-2.0", 44100)
    assert grid == Beatgrid("BeatGrid-2.0", True, 128.0, 1234, 1234 / 44100) and warnings == []
    grid, warnings = beats.decode(b"\x00", "BeatMap-2.0", 44100)
    assert grid == Beatgrid("BeatMap-2.0", False) and warnings == []
    grid, warnings = beats.decode(b"\x00", "BeatSoup-9", 44100)
    assert grid == Beatgrid("BeatSoup-9", False) and [w.code for w in warnings] == ["unknown_blob_version"]
    assert beats.decode(None, None, 44100) == (Beatgrid(None, False), [])
    grid, warnings = beats.decode(b"\x0a\x00", "BeatGrid-2.0", 44100)
    assert grid.decoded is False and [w.code for w in warnings] == ["beats_undecodable"]


def test_frames_are_per_channel_but_cue_samples_are_interleaved():
    """Spec §6.4: beatgrid seconds = frame / samplerate; cue seconds = samples / (samplerate × channels)."""
    samplerate, channels = 44100, 2
    grid, _ = beats.decode(grid_blob(120.0, 44100), "BeatGrid-2.0", samplerate)
    assert grid.first_beat_seconds == 1.0
    cue_position_samples = 88200.0
    assert cue_position_samples / (samplerate * channels) == 1.0


def test_golden_every_fixture_blob_matches_library_bpm():
    conn = sqlite3.connect(f"{FIXTURE_DB.resolve().as_uri()}?mode=ro", uri=True)
    rows = conn.execute("SELECT id, bpm, beats, beats_version, samplerate FROM library WHERE beats IS NOT NULL").fetchall()
    assert len(rows) >= 10
    for track_id, bpm, blob, version, samplerate in rows:
        grid, warnings = beats.decode(blob, version, samplerate)
        assert grid.decoded, track_id
        assert grid.bpm == bpm, track_id
        assert warnings == []
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/mixxxlib/blobs/beats.py`**

```python
"""Decoder for Mixxx's `library.beats` blob (spec §6.4). Pure Python; no protobuf dependency.

BeatGrid-2.0 is `message BeatGrid { Bpm bpm = 1; Beat first_beat = 2; }` with
`Bpm { double bpm = 1; }` and `Beat { int32 frame_position = 1; bool enabled = 2; Source source = 3; }`.
`frame_position` is in per-channel frames: seconds = frame_position / samplerate.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import struct
from typing import Any, Iterator

from mixxxlib.model import Beatgrid, WarningMessage

KNOWN_VERSIONS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"BeatGrid-1.0", "BeatGrid-2.0", "BeatMap-1.0", "BeatMap-2.0"})


class BlobError(ValueError):
    """The blob does not parse as the protobuf message its version claims."""


def read_varint(buf: bytes, pos: int) -> tuple[int, int]:
    result = shift = 0
    while True:
        if pos >= len(buf):
            raise BlobError("truncated varint")
        byte = buf[pos]
        pos += 1
        result |= (byte & 0x7F) << shift
        shift += 7
        if not byte & 0x80:
            return result, pos
        if shift > 70:
            raise BlobError("varint too long")


def iter_fields(buf: bytes) -> Iterator[tuple[int, int, Any]]:
    """Yield (field_number, wire_type, value) for each top-level field in a protobuf message."""
    pos = 0
    while pos < len(buf):
        tag, pos = read_varint(buf, pos)
        field_number, wire_type = tag >> 3, tag & 7
        if wire_type == 0:
            value, pos = read_varint(buf, pos)
        elif wire_type == 1:
            value, pos = buf[pos:pos + 8], pos + 8
        elif wire_type == 2:
            length, pos = read_varint(buf, pos)
            value, pos = buf[pos:pos + length], pos + length
        elif wire_type == 5:
            value, pos = buf[pos:pos + 4], pos + 4
        else:
            raise BlobError(f"unsupported wire type {wire_type}")
        if pos > len(buf):
            raise BlobError("field runs past end of blob")
        yield field_number, wire_type, value


def _int32(value: int) -> int:
    return value - (1 << 32) if value >= 1 << 31 else value


def decode_beatgrid_v2(blob: bytes) -> tuple[float, int]:
    bpm: float | None = None
    frame: int | None = None
    for number, wire_type, value in iter_fields(blob):
        if number == 1 and wire_type == 2:
            for sub_number, sub_type, sub_value in iter_fields(value):
                if sub_number == 1 and sub_type == 1 and len(sub_value) == 8:
                    bpm = struct.unpack("<d", sub_value)[0]
        elif number == 2 and wire_type == 2:
            for sub_number, sub_type, sub_value in iter_fields(value):
                if sub_number == 1 and sub_type == 0:
                    frame = _int32(sub_value)
    if bpm is None or frame is None:
        raise BlobError("BeatGrid-2.0 blob lacks bpm or first_beat.frame_position")
    return bpm, frame


def decode(blob: bytes | None, version: str | None, samplerate: int | None) -> tuple[Beatgrid, list[WarningMessage]]:
    """Dispatch on beats_version. Nothing is guessed: only BeatGrid-2.0 is decoded in v1."""
    if blob is None or version is None:
        return Beatgrid(version, False), []
    if version == "BeatGrid-2.0":
        try:
            bpm, frame = decode_beatgrid_v2(bytes(blob))
        except BlobError as exc:
            return Beatgrid(version, False), [WarningMessage("beats_undecodable", str(exc), {"version": version})]
        seconds = frame / samplerate if samplerate else None
        return Beatgrid(version, True, bpm, frame, seconds), []
    if version in KNOWN_VERSIONS:
        return Beatgrid(version, False), []
    return Beatgrid(version, False), [
        WarningMessage("unknown_blob_version", f"beats_version {version!r} is not recognised", {"version": version})
    ]
```

```python
import pytest

from mixxxlib.errors import NotFoundError, SchemaUnsupportedError, ValidationError
from mixxxlib.model import Crate, CrateNode


def test_list_is_sorted_by_name_with_live_track_count(sandbox, manifest):
    crates = sandbox.library().crates.list()
    assert [c.name for c in crates] == sorted(c.name for c in crates)
    loops = next(c for c in crates if c.id == manifest["crates"]["loops"])
    assert loops == Crate(loops.id, "Fixture Loops", None, False, True, False, 5)


def test_get_by_name_and_id(sandbox, manifest):
    lib = sandbox.library()
    assert lib.crates.get("Fixture Loops").id == manifest["crates"]["loops"]
    assert lib.crates.get(manifest["crates"]["loops"], by_id=True).name == "Fixture Loops"
    with pytest.raises(NotFoundError) as e:
        lib.crates.get("Nope")
    assert e.value.details == {"name": "Nope"}
    with pytest.raises(NotFoundError):
        lib.crates.get(999999, by_id=True)


def test_create_rename_update(sandbox):
    lib = sandbox.library()
    result = lib.crates.create("New Crate")
    crate = result.data["crate"]
    assert crate.name == "New Crate" and crate.track_count == 0 and crate.parent_id is None
    assert result.backup and result.integrity == "ok"
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError) as e:
        lib.crates.create("New Crate")
    assert e.value.details["name"] == "New Crate"
    for bad in ["", " ", "x" * 49]:
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
            lib.crates.create(bad)
    renamed = lib.crates.rename(crate, "Renamed").data["crate"]
    assert renamed.name == "Renamed" and renamed.id == crate.id
    updated = lib.crates.update(renamed, locked=True, autodj_source=True).data["crate"]
    assert (updated.locked, updated.show, updated.autodj_source) == (True, True, True)
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        lib.crates.update(updated)


def test_locked_crate_refuses_membership_and_deletion(sandbox, manifest):
    lib = sandbox.library()
    locked = lib.crates.get("Locked Crate")
    for call in (lambda: lib.crates.add(locked, [manifest["tracks"][0]]),
                 lambda: lib.crates.remove(locked, manifest["crate_members"]["locked"]),
                 lambda: lib.crates.set_tracks(locked, []),
                 lambda: lib.crates.delete(locked)):
        with pytest.raises(ValidationError) as e:
            call()
        assert e.value.details["crate_id"] == locked.id
    assert not sandbox.backup_dir.exists()
    unlocked = lib.crates.update(locked, locked=False).data["crate"]
    assert lib.crates.delete(unlocked).data["deleted"][0]["track_count"] == 2
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM crate_tracks WHERE crate_id = ?", locked.id) == 0


def test_add_is_idempotent_and_refuses_unknown_tracks_before_writing(sandbox, manifest):
    lib = sandbox.library()
    crate = lib.crates.get("Empty Crate")
    t = manifest["tracks"]
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError) as e:
        lib.crates.add(crate, [t[0], 987654])
    assert e.value.details == {"track_ids": [987654]}
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM crate_tracks WHERE crate_id = ?", crate.id) == 0
    result = lib.crates.add(crate, [t[0], t[1], t[0]])
    assert result.data["added"] == [t[0], t[1]] and result.data["already_member"] == []
    result = lib.crates.add(crate, [t[1], t[2]])
    assert result.data["added"] == [t[2]] and result.data["already_member"] == [t[1]]
    assert [w.code for w in result.warnings] == ["already_member"]
    result = lib.crates.remove(crate, [t[2], t[9]])
    assert result.data["removed"] == [t[2]] and result.data["not_member"] == [t[9]]
    assert [w.code for w in result.warnings] == ["not_member"]
    result = lib.crates.set_tracks(crate, [t[1], t[5]])
    assert (result.data["added"], result.data["removed"], result.data["unchanged"]) == ([t[5]], [t[0]], [t[1]])
    assert sorted(lib.crates.members(crate)) == sorted([t[1], t[5]])


def test_dry_run_renders_statements_only(sandbox, manifest):
    lib = sandbox.library()
    crate = lib.crates.get("Empty Crate")
    result = lib.crates.add(crate, [manifest["tracks"][0]], dry_run=True)
    assert result.dry_run and result.backup is None
    assert result.statements == [f"INSERT INTO crate_tracks (crate_id, track_id) VALUES ({crate.id}, {manifest['tracks'][0]})"]
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM crate_tracks WHERE crate_id = ?", crate.id) == 0


def test_export_m3u8(sandbox, manifest):
    lib = sandbox.library()
    text = lib.crates.export_m3u8(lib.crates.get("Fixture Loops"))
    lines = text.splitlines()
    assert lines[0] == "#EXTM3U"
    assert len(lines) == 1 + 2 * 5
    assert lines[1].startswith("#EXTINF:") and " - " in lines[1]
    assert lines[2] == str(sandbox.audio / manifest["audio"][str(manifest["crate_members"]["loops"][0])])


def test_nested_commands_need_schema_41(sandbox):
    lib = sandbox.library()
    crate = lib.crates.get("Empty Crate")
    assert lib.nested is False
    for call in (lambda: lib.crates.create("Child", parent=crate),
                 lambda: lib.crates.move(crate, parent=None),
                 lambda: lib.crates.list(parent=crate),
                 lambda: lib.crates.members(crate, recursive=True)):
        with pytest.raises(SchemaUnsupportedError) as e:
            call()
        assert e.value.details["needs"] == 41
    roots = lib.crates.tree()
    assert all(node.children == () for node in roots) and len(roots) == len(lib.crates.list())


def test_nested_tree_lookup_and_sibling_uniqueness(sandbox_42, manifest_42):
    lib = sandbox_42.library()
    assert lib.nested and lib.schema.version == 42
    c = manifest_42["crates"]
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError) as e:
        lib.crates.get("Dupe")
    assert sorted(x["id"] for x in e.value.details["candidates"]) == sorted([c["dupe_a"], c["dupe_b"]])
    genres = lib.crates.get("Genres")
    bass = lib.crates.get("Bass", parent=genres)
    assert bass.parent_id == genres.id
    tree = lib.crates.tree()
    genres_node = next(n for n in tree if n.crate.id == genres.id)
    assert isinstance(genres_node, CrateNode)
    assert [n.crate.name for n in genres_node.children] == ["Bass"]
    assert [n.crate.name for n in genres_node.children[0].children] == ["Dubstep"]
    assert [x.name for x in lib.crates.list(parent=genres)] == ["Bass"]
    assert all(x.parent_id is None for x in lib.crates.list(roots_only=True))
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        lib.crates.create("Dupe")  # root duplicate: SQLite would allow it, we refuse
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        lib.crates.create("Bass", parent=genres)
    child = lib.crates.create("Bass", parent=bass).data["crate"]  # same name under a different parent is fine
    assert child.parent_id == bass.id
    assert sorted(lib.crates.members(genres, recursive=True)) == sorted(manifest_42["crate_members"]["bass"] + manifest_42["crate_members"]["dubstep"])
    assert lib.crates.members(genres) == []


def test_nested_move_and_delete(sandbox_42, manifest_42):
    lib = sandbox_42.library()
    genres, bass, dubstep = (lib.crates.get(n) for n in ("Genres", "Bass", "Dubstep"))
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError) as e:
        lib.crates.move(genres, parent=dubstep)
    assert e.value.details["reason"] == "cycle"
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError):
        lib.crates.move(bass, parent=bass)
    moved = lib.crates.move(dubstep, parent=None).data["crate"]
    assert moved.parent_id is None
    moved = lib.crates.move(moved, parent=genres).data["crate"]
    assert moved.parent_id == genres.id
    with pytest.raises(ValidationError) as e:
        lib.crates.delete(genres)
    assert e.value.details["children"] == sorted([bass.id, dubstep.id])
    result = lib.crates.delete(genres, recursive=True)
    assert sorted(d["crate"].id for d in result.data["deleted"]) == sorted([genres.id, bass.id, dubstep.id])
    assert sandbox_42.scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM crates WHERE id IN (?, ?, ?)", genres.id, bass.id, dubstep.id) == 0
    assert sandbox_42.scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM crate_tracks WHERE crate_id IN (?, ?)", bass.id, dubstep.id) == 0


def test_library_open_reports_missing_database(tmp_path, clean_env):
    from mixxxlib import Library

    with pytest.raises(NotFoundError) as e:
        Library.open(db=str(tmp_path / "none.sqlite"))
    assert e.value.details["path"].endswith("none.sqlite")
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/mixxxlib/db/crates.py`**

```python
"""All SQL for crates (spec §6.1, §8.2). Works at schema ≤ 40 and, when `nested`, at ≥ 41."""

from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
from collections.abc import Iterable

from mixxxlib.db.connection import MutationPlan, Statement
from mixxxlib.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from mixxxlib.model import Crate, CrateNode, WarningMessage

MAX_NAME_LENGTH = 48


def _select(nested: bool) -> str:
    parent = "c.parent_id" if nested else "NULL"
    return (
        f"SELECT c.id, c.name, {parent} AS parent_id, c.locked, c.show, c.autodj_source, "
        "(SELECT count(*) FROM crate_tracks ct WHERE ct.crate_id = c.id) AS track_count FROM crates c"
    )


def row_to_crate(row: sqlite3.Row) -> Crate:
    return Crate(id=row["id"], name=row["name"], parent_id=row["parent_id"], locked=bool(row["locked"]),
                 show=bool(row["show"]), autodj_source=bool(row["autodj_source"]), track_count=row["track_count"])


def list_crates(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, nested: bool, parent_id: int | None = None,
                roots_only: bool = False) -> list[Crate]:
    sql, params = _select(nested), ()
    if nested and roots_only:
        sql += " WHERE c.parent_id IS NULL"
    elif nested and parent_id is not None:
        sql, params = sql + " WHERE c.parent_id = ?", (parent_id,)
    sql += " ORDER BY c.name, c.id"
    return [row_to_crate(r) for r in conn.execute(sql, params)]


def get_crate_by_id(conn: sqlite3.Connection, crate_id: int, *, nested: bool) -> Crate:
    row = conn.execute(_select(nested) + " WHERE c.id = ?", (crate_id,)).fetchone()
    if row is None:
        raise NotFoundError(f"crate {crate_id} does not exist", crate_id=crate_id)
    return row_to_crate(row)


def find_crate_by_name(conn: sqlite3.Connection, name: str, *, nested: bool, parent_id: int | None = None,
                       parent_given: bool = False) -> Crate:
    sql, params = _select(nested) + " WHERE c.name = ?", [name]
    if nested and parent_given:
        sql += " AND c.parent_id IS ?"
        params.append(parent_id)
    rows = conn.execute(sql + " ORDER BY c.id", params).fetchall()
    if not rows:
        raise NotFoundError(f"crate {name!r} does not exist", name=name)
    if len(rows) > 1:
        raise ValidationError(
            f"crate name {name!r} matches {len(rows)} crates under different parents; use --parent or --id",
            name=name, candidates=[{"id": r["id"], "parent_id": r["parent_id"]} for r in rows],
        )
    return row_to_crate(rows[0])


def descendant_ids(conn: sqlite3.Connection, crate_id: int) -> list[int]:
    """Every crate below crate_id, parents before children. Requires the parent_id column."""
    rows = conn.execute(
        "WITH RECURSIVE sub(id, depth) AS ("
        "  SELECT id, 1 FROM crates WHERE parent_id = ?"
        "  UNION ALL SELECT c.id, sub.depth + 1 FROM crates c JOIN sub ON c.parent_id = sub.id WHERE sub.depth < 64)"
        " SELECT id FROM sub ORDER BY depth, id", (crate_id,)
    ).fetchall()
    return [r[0] for r in rows]


def tree(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, nested: bool) -> list[CrateNode]:
    crates = list_crates(conn, nested=nested)
    by_parent: dict[int | None, list[Crate]] = {}
    for crate in crates:
        by_parent.setdefault(crate.parent_id, []).append(crate)
    ids = {c.id for c in crates}

    def build(crate: Crate, seen: frozenset[int]) -> CrateNode:
        children = tuple(build(child, seen | {child.id}) for child in by_parent.get(crate.id, ()) if child.id not in seen)
        return CrateNode(crate, children)

    roots = [c for c in crates if c.parent_id is None or c.parent_id not in ids]
    return [build(root, frozenset({root.id})) for root in roots]


def validate_name(name: str) -> str:
    if not isinstance(name, str) or not name.strip():
        raise ValidationError("crate name must not be empty", name=name)
    if len(name) > MAX_NAME_LENGTH:
        raise ValidationError(f"crate name is {len(name)} characters; Mixxx allows at most {MAX_NAME_LENGTH}",
                              name=name, length=len(name), max_length=MAX_NAME_LENGTH)
    return name


def sibling_exists(conn: sqlite3.Connection, name: str, parent_id: int | None, *, nested: bool,
                   exclude_id: int | None = None) -> bool:
    sql, params = "SELECT 1 FROM crates WHERE name = ?", [name]
    if nested:
        sql += " AND parent_id IS ?"
        params.append(parent_id)
    if exclude_id is not None:
        sql += " AND id != ?"
        params.append(exclude_id)
    return conn.execute(sql + " LIMIT 1", params).fetchone() is not None


def _refuse_duplicate(conn: sqlite3.Connection, name: str, parent_id: int | None, *, nested: bool,
                      exclude_id: int | None = None) -> None:
    if sibling_exists(conn, name, parent_id, nested=nested, exclude_id=exclude_id):
        where = f"under crate {parent_id}" if parent_id is not None else "at the root"
        raise ValidationError(f"a crate named {name!r} already exists {where}", name=name, parent_id=parent_id)


def member_ids(conn: sqlite3.Connection, crate_id: int) -> set[int]:
    return {r[0] for r in conn.execute("SELECT track_id FROM crate_tracks WHERE crate_id = ?", (crate_id,))}


def existing_track_ids(conn: sqlite3.Connection, ids: Iterable[int]) -> set[int]:
    wanted = list(dict.fromkeys(ids))
    found: set[int] = set()
    for start in range(0, len(wanted), 500):
        chunk = wanted[start:start + 500]
        marks = ",".join("?" * len(chunk))
        found |= {r[0] for r in conn.execute(f"SELECT id FROM library WHERE id IN ({marks})", chunk)}
    return found


def ensure_unlocked(crate: Crate) -> None:
    if crate.locked:
        raise ValidationError(f"crate {crate.name!r} is locked; run `crate update --no-locked` first",
                              crate_id=crate.id, name=crate.name)


def _refresh(crate_id: int, nested: bool):
    return lambda conn: {"crate": get_crate_by_id(conn, crate_id, nested=nested)}


def plan_create(conn: sqlite3.Connection, name: str, *, parent: Crate | None, nested: bool) -> MutationPlan:
    validate_name(name)
    parent_id = parent.id if parent else None
    _refuse_duplicate(conn, name, parent_id, nested=nested)
    if nested:
        statement = Statement("INSERT INTO crates (name, parent_id) VALUES (?, ?)", (name, parent_id))
    else:
        statement = Statement("INSERT INTO crates (name) VALUES (?)", (name,))
    preview = Crate(id=-1, name=name, parent_id=parent_id, locked=False, show=True, autodj_source=False, track_count=0)

    def after_commit(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> dict:
        new_id = conn.execute("SELECT last_insert_rowid()").fetchone()[0]
        return {"crate": get_crate_by_id(conn, new_id, nested=nested)}

    return MutationPlan(statements=[statement], data={"crate": preview}, after_commit=after_commit)


def plan_rename(conn: sqlite3.Connection, crate: Crate, new_name: str, *, nested: bool) -> MutationPlan:
    validate_name(new_name)
    _refuse_duplicate(conn, new_name, crate.parent_id, nested=nested, exclude_id=crate.id)
    return MutationPlan(statements=[Statement("UPDATE crates SET name = ? WHERE id = ?", (new_name, crate.id))],
                        data={"crate": crate}, after_commit=_refresh(crate.id, nested))


def plan_move(conn: sqlite3.Connection, crate: Crate, *, parent: Crate | None) -> MutationPlan:
    new_parent_id = parent.id if parent else None
    if parent is not None and (parent.id == crate.id or parent.id in descendant_ids(conn, crate.id)):
        raise ValidationError(f"moving crate {crate.name!r} under {parent.name!r} would create a cycle",
                              crate_id=crate.id, parent_id=parent.id, reason="cycle")
    _refuse_duplicate(conn, crate.name, new_parent_id, nested=True, exclude_id=crate.id)
    return MutationPlan(statements=[Statement("UPDATE crates SET parent_id = ? WHERE id = ?", (new_parent_id, crate.id))],
                        data={"crate": crate}, after_commit=_refresh(crate.id, True))


def plan_update(conn: sqlite3.Connection, crate: Crate, *, locked: bool | None, show: bool | None,
                autodj_source: bool | None, nested: bool) -> MutationPlan:
    changes = {k: v for k, v in (("locked", locked), ("show", show), ("autodj_source", autodj_source)) if v is not None}
    if not changes:
        raise ValidationError("nothing to update: pass at least one of --locked/--show/--autodj-source", crate_id=crate.id)
    assignments = ", ".join(f"{column} = ?" for column in changes)
    statement = Statement(f"UPDATE crates SET {assignments} WHERE id = ?", (*(int(v) for v in changes.values()), crate.id))
    return MutationPlan(statements=[statement], data={"crate": crate}, after_commit=_refresh(crate.id, nested))


def plan_delete(conn: sqlite3.Connection, crate: Crate, *, recursive: bool, nested: bool) -> MutationPlan:
    ensure_unlocked(crate)
    children = descendant_ids(conn, crate.id) if nested else []
    if children and not recursive:
        raise ValidationError(f"crate {crate.name!r} has {len(children)} descendant crate(s); pass --recursive to delete the subtree",
                              crate_id=crate.id, children=sorted(children))
    victims = [get_crate_by_id(conn, cid, nested=nested) for cid in reversed(children)] + [crate]
    for victim in victims:
        ensure_unlocked(victim)
    statements: list[Statement] = []
    for victim in victims:
        statements.append(Statement("DELETE FROM crate_tracks WHERE crate_id = ?", (victim.id,)))
        statements.append(Statement("DELETE FROM crates WHERE id = ?", (victim.id,)))
    return MutationPlan(statements=statements,
                        data={"deleted": [{"crate": v, "track_count": v.track_count} for v in victims]})


def _check_tracks_exist(conn: sqlite3.Connection, track_ids: list[int]) -> None:
    unknown = sorted(set(track_ids) - existing_track_ids(conn, track_ids))
    if unknown:
        raise ValidationError(f"{len(unknown)} track id(s) do not exist: {unknown[:10]}", track_ids=unknown)


def plan_add(conn: sqlite3.Connection, crate: Crate, track_ids: Iterable[int], *, nested: bool) -> MutationPlan:
    ensure_unlocked(crate)
    wanted = list(dict.fromkeys(int(t) for t in track_ids))
    _check_tracks_exist(conn, wanted)
    current = member_ids(conn, crate.id)
    added = [t for t in wanted if t not in current]
    already = [t for t in wanted if t in current]
    warnings = [WarningMessage("already_member", f"{len(already)} track(s) were already in the crate", {"track_ids": already})] if already else []
    statements = [Statement("INSERT INTO crate_tracks (crate_id, track_id) VALUES (?, ?)", (crate.id, t)) for t in added]
    return MutationPlan(statements=statements, data={"crate": crate, "added": added, "already_member": already},
                        warnings=warnings, after_commit=lambda c: {"crate": get_crate_by_id(c, crate.id, nested=nested),
                                                                   "added": added, "already_member": already})


def plan_remove(conn: sqlite3.Connection, crate: Crate, track_ids: Iterable[int], *, nested: bool) -> MutationPlan:
    ensure_unlocked(crate)
    wanted = list(dict.fromkeys(int(t) for t in track_ids))
    current = member_ids(conn, crate.id)
    removed = [t for t in wanted if t in current]
    missing = [t for t in wanted if t not in current]
    warnings = [WarningMessage("not_member", f"{len(missing)} track(s) were not in the crate", {"track_ids": missing})] if missing else []
    statements = [Statement("DELETE FROM crate_tracks WHERE crate_id = ? AND track_id = ?", (crate.id, t)) for t in removed]
    return MutationPlan(statements=statements, data={"crate": crate, "removed": removed, "not_member": missing},
                        warnings=warnings, after_commit=lambda c: {"crate": get_crate_by_id(c, crate.id, nested=nested),
                                                                   "removed": removed, "not_member": missing})


def plan_set_tracks(conn: sqlite3.Connection, crate: Crate, track_ids: Iterable[int], *, nested: bool) -> MutationPlan:
    ensure_unlocked(crate)
    wanted = list(dict.fromkeys(int(t) for t in track_ids))
    _check_tracks_exist(conn, wanted)
    current = member_ids(conn, crate.id)
    added = [t for t in wanted if t not in current]
    removed = sorted(current - set(wanted))
    unchanged = [t for t in wanted if t in current]
    statements = [Statement("DELETE FROM crate_tracks WHERE crate_id = ? AND track_id = ?", (crate.id, t)) for t in removed]
    statements += [Statement("INSERT INTO crate_tracks (crate_id, track_id) VALUES (?, ?)", (crate.id, t)) for t in added]
    payload = {"added": added, "removed": removed, "unchanged": unchanged}
    return MutationPlan(statements=statements, data={"crate": crate, **payload},
                        after_commit=lambda c: {"crate": get_crate_by_id(c, crate.id, nested=nested), **payload})


def export_m3u8(conn: sqlite3.Connection, crate_ids: list[int]) -> str:
    """#EXTM3U text with #EXTINF:<seconds>,<artist> - <title> and absolute paths, tracks by id."""
    marks = ",".join("?" * len(crate_ids))
    rows = conn.execute(
        "SELECT DISTINCT l.id, l.artist, l.title, l.duration, tl.location FROM library l "
        "JOIN track_locations tl ON tl.id = l.location JOIN crate_tracks ct ON ct.track_id = l.id "
        f"WHERE ct.crate_id IN ({marks}) ORDER BY l.id", crate_ids,
    ).fetchall()
    lines = ["#EXTM3U"]
    for row in rows:
        seconds = int(round(row["duration"] or 0))
        lines.append(f"#EXTINF:{seconds},{row['artist'] or ''} - {row['title'] or ''}")
        lines.append(row["location"] or "")
    return "\n".join(lines) + "\n"
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/mixxxlib/library.py`**

```python
"""The public API façade (spec §10.1). Never imports Typer."""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sqlite3
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterable, Iterator, Mapping
from contextlib import closing, contextmanager
from typing import Any

from mixxxlib import __version__, paths as paths_mod
from mixxxlib.db import connection as conn_mod, crates as crates_sql, schema as schema_mod
from mixxxlib.model import Crate, CrateNode, MutationResult, SchemaInfo, WarningMessage
from mixxxlib.safety import backup as backup_mod
from mixxxlib.safety.guard import mixxx_process_running
from mixxxlib.settings import load_mixxx_cfg


class Library:
    def __init__(self, resolved: paths_mod.ResolvedPaths, *, schema_ceiling: int,
                 environ: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,
                 process_check: Callable[[], bool | None] | None = None) -> None:
        self.paths = resolved
        self.schema_ceiling = schema_ceiling
        self._environ = os.environ if environ is None else environ
        self._process_check = process_check or mixxx_process_running
        self.retention = backup_mod.Retention.from_env(self._environ)
        with closing(conn_mod.open_ro(resolved.db)) as conn, conn_mod.translating_errors():
            self.schema: SchemaInfo = schema_mod.schema_info(conn, ceiling=schema_ceiling)
        self.crates = CratesAPI(self)

    @classmethod
    def open(cls, settings_path: str | None = None, db: str | None = None, backup_dir: str | None = None, *,
             schema_ceiling: int | None = None, environ: Mapping[str, str] | None = None,
             process_check: Callable[[], bool | None] | None = None) -> "Library":
        resolved = paths_mod.resolve(settings_path, db, backup_dir, environ=environ)
        ceiling = schema_mod.SCHEMA_CEILING if schema_ceiling is None else schema_ceiling
        return cls(resolved, schema_ceiling=ceiling, environ=environ, process_check=process_check)

    # -- schema / state -------------------------------------------------------------

    @property
    def nested(self) -> bool:
        return "nested_crates" in self.schema.capabilities

    def require_nested(self) -> None:
        schema_mod.require_schema(self.schema, capability="nested_crates")

    def mixxx_running(self) -> bool | None:
        return self._process_check()

    def read_warnings(self) -> list[WarningMessage]:
        if self.mixxx_running() is True:
            return [WarningMessage("mixxx_running", "Mixxx is running; values may be stale", {})]
        return []

    # -- connections ----------------------------------------------------------------

    @contextmanager
    def read(self) -> Iterator[sqlite3.Connection]:
        with closing(conn_mod.open_ro(self.paths.db)) as conn, conn_mod.translating_errors():
            yield conn

    def mutate(self, label: str, build: Callable[[sqlite3.Connection], conn_mod.MutationPlan], *,
               dry_run: bool = False, requires: str | None = None) -> MutationResult:
        with closing(conn_mod.open_rw(self.paths.db)) as conn:
            return conn_mod.run_mutation(
                conn, label=label, backup_dir=self.paths.backup_dir, build=build, schema_ceiling=self.schema_ceiling,
                requires=requires, dry_run=dry_run, retention=self.retention, process_check=self._process_check,
            )

    # -- settings / info ------------------------------------------------------------

    @property
    def settings(self) -> dict[str, dict[str, str]]:
        return load_mixxx_cfg(self.paths.config)

    def info(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        backups = backup_mod.list_backups(self.paths.backup_dir)
        return {
            "version": __version__,
            "paths": {"settings_dir": str(self.paths.settings_dir), "db": str(self.paths.db),
                      "config": str(self.paths.config), "config_exists": self.paths.config.is_file(),
                      "backup_dir": str(self.paths.backup_dir)},
            "schema": {"version": self.schema.version, "ceiling": self.schema.ceiling,
                       "capabilities": sorted(self.schema.capabilities), "verdict": self.schema.verdict,
                       "message": self.schema.message},
            "mixxx_running": self.mixxx_running(),
            "backups": {"count": len(backups), "newest": backups[0].name if backups else None},
        }


class CratesAPI:
    def __init__(self, lib: Library) -> None:
        self._lib = lib

    def list(self, *, parent: Crate | None = None, roots_only: bool = False) -> list[Crate]:
        if parent is not None:
            self._lib.require_nested()
        with self._lib.read() as conn:
            return crates_sql.list_crates(conn, nested=self._lib.nested, parent_id=parent.id if parent else None,
                                          roots_only=roots_only)

    def get(self, selector: str | int, *, by_id: bool = False, parent: Crate | None = None) -> Crate:
        if parent is not None:
            self._lib.require_nested()
        with self._lib.read() as conn:
            if by_id:
                return crates_sql.get_crate_by_id(conn, int(selector), nested=self._lib.nested)
            return crates_sql.find_crate_by_name(conn, str(selector), nested=self._lib.nested,
                                                 parent_id=parent.id if parent else None, parent_given=parent is not None)

    def tree(self) -> list[CrateNode]:
        with self._lib.read() as conn:
            return crates_sql.tree(conn, nested=self._lib.nested)

    def subtree_ids(self, crate: Crate, *, recursive: bool) -> list[int]:
        if not recursive:
            return [crate.id]
        self._lib.require_nested()
        with self._lib.read() as conn:
            return [crate.id, *crates_sql.descendant_ids(conn, crate.id)]

    def members(self, crate: Crate, *, recursive: bool = False) -> list[int]:
        ids = self.subtree_ids(crate, recursive=recursive)
        with self._lib.read() as conn:
            found: set[int] = set()
            for cid in ids:
                found |= crates_sql.member_ids(conn, cid)
        return sorted(found)

    def create(self, name: str, *, parent: Crate | None = None, dry_run: bool = False) -> MutationResult:
        nested = self._lib.nested
        return self._lib.mutate("crate create", lambda c: crates_sql.plan_create(c, name, parent=parent, nested=nested),
                                dry_run=dry_run, requires="nested_crates" if parent is not None else None)

    def rename(self, crate: Crate, new_name: str, *, dry_run: bool = False) -> MutationResult:
        nested = self._lib.nested
        return self._lib.mutate("crate rename", lambda c: crates_sql.plan_rename(c, crate, new_name, nested=nested), dry_run=dry_run)

    def move(self, crate: Crate, *, parent: Crate | None, dry_run: bool = False) -> MutationResult:
        return self._lib.mutate("crate move", lambda c: crates_sql.plan_move(c, crate, parent=parent),
                                dry_run=dry_run, requires="nested_crates")

    def update(self, crate: Crate, *, locked: bool | None = None, show: bool | None = None,
               autodj_source: bool | None = None, dry_run: bool = False) -> MutationResult:
        nested = self._lib.nested
        return self._lib.mutate("crate update", lambda c: crates_sql.plan_update(
            c, crate, locked=locked, show=show, autodj_source=autodj_source, nested=nested), dry_run=dry_run)

    def delete(self, crate: Crate, *, recursive: bool = False, dry_run: bool = False) -> MutationResult:
        nested = self._lib.nested
        return self._lib.mutate("crate delete", lambda c: crates_sql.plan_delete(c, crate, recursive=recursive, nested=nested),
                                dry_run=dry_run)

    def add(self, crate: Crate, track_ids: Iterable[int], *, dry_run: bool = False) -> MutationResult:
        nested, ids = self._lib.nested, list(track_ids)
        return self._lib.mutate("crate add", lambda c: crates_sql.plan_add(c, crate, ids, nested=nested), dry_run=dry_run)

    def remove(self, crate: Crate, track_ids: Iterable[int], *, dry_run: bool = False) -> MutationResult:
        nested, ids = self._lib.nested, list(track_ids)
        return self._lib.mutate("crate remove", lambda c: crates_sql.plan_remove(c, crate, ids, nested=nested), dry_run=dry_run)

    def set_tracks(self, crate: Crate, track_ids: Iterable[int], *, dry_run: bool = False) -> MutationResult:
        nested, ids = self._lib.nested, list(track_ids)
        return self._lib.mutate("crate set-tracks", lambda c: crates_sql.plan_set_tracks(c, crate, ids, nested=nested), dry_run=dry_run)

    def export_m3u8(self, crate: Crate, *, recursive: bool = False) -> str:
        ids = self.subtree_ids(crate, recursive=recursive)
        with self._lib.read() as conn:
            return crates_sql.export_m3u8(conn, ids)
```

```python
from mixxxlib.library import Library  # noqa: E402

__all__ = ["Library", "__version__"]
```

```python
import sqlite3

from mixxxlib.doctor import summarize


def by_check(findings, name):
    return [f for f in findings if f.check == name]


def test_fixture_doctor_has_no_errors_and_the_expected_warnings(sandbox, manifest):
    findings = sandbox.library().doctor()
    summary = summarize(findings)
    assert summary["errors"] == 0
    assert [f.check for f in findings][:3] == ["schema", "mixxx_running", "integrity"]
    cues = by_check(findings, "cues")
    reasons = {f.details["reason"] for f in cues}
    assert {"zero_colour", "duplicate_slot"} <= reasons
    zero = next(f for f in cues if f.details["reason"] == "zero_colour")
    assert zero.severity == "warning" and manifest["hotcue_ids"]["zero_colour"] in zero.details["cue_ids"]
    keys = by_check(findings, "keys")
    assert len(keys) == 1 and keys[0].details["track_ids"] == [manifest["key_mismatch_track"]]
    unanalyzed = by_check(findings, "unanalyzed")
    assert unanalyzed[0].severity == "info" and unanalyzed[0].details["track_ids"] == manifest["unanalyzed_tracks"]
    assert by_check(findings, "integrity")[0].severity == "info"
    assert by_check(findings, "backups")[0].details["count"] == 0


def test_orphans_are_errors(sandbox, manifest):
    with sqlite3.connect(sandbox.db, autocommit=True) as w:
        w.execute("DELETE FROM library WHERE id = ?", (manifest["hotcue_track"],))
    findings = sandbox.library().doctor()
    orphans = by_check(findings, "orphans")
    assert {f.details["relation"] for f in orphans} >= {"cues.track_id -> library.id", "crate_tracks.track_id -> library.id"}
    assert all(f.severity == "error" for f in orphans)
    assert summarize(findings)["errors"] >= 2
    sandbox.__dict__["_skip_doctor"] = True  # this test corrupted the database on purpose


def test_schema_above_ceiling_is_an_error_but_doctor_still_runs(sandbox):
    with sqlite3.connect(sandbox.db, autocommit=True) as w:
        w.execute("UPDATE settings SET value = '41' WHERE name = 'mixxx.schema.version'")
    findings = sandbox.library().doctor()
    schema = by_check(findings, "schema")[0]
    assert schema.severity == "error" and schema.details["version"] == 41
    with sqlite3.connect(sandbox.db, autocommit=True) as w:
        w.execute("UPDATE settings SET value = '39' WHERE name = 'mixxx.schema.version'")


def test_missing_file_is_a_warning(sandbox, manifest):
    track_id = manifest["tracks"][3]
    (sandbox.audio / manifest["audio"][str(track_id)]).unlink()
    findings = sandbox.library().doctor()
    missing = by_check(findings, "missing_files")
    assert missing and missing[0].severity == "warning" and track_id in missing[0].details["track_ids"]


def test_duplicate_sibling_names_are_a_warning_at_42(sandbox_42, manifest_42):
    findings = sandbox_42.library().doctor()
    crates = by_check(findings, "crates")
    assert len(crates) == 1 and crates[0].details["duplicates"] == [{"name": "Dupe", "parent_id": None, "count": 2}]
    assert summarize(findings)["errors"] == 0


def test_cycle_is_an_error_at_42(sandbox_42, manifest_42):
    c = manifest_42["crates"]
    with sqlite3.connect(sandbox_42.db, autocommit=True) as w:
        w.execute("UPDATE crates SET parent_id = ? WHERE id = ?", (c["dubstep"], c["genres"]))
    findings = sandbox_42.library().doctor()
    cycles = [f for f in by_check(findings, "orphans") if f.details.get("relation") == "crates.parent_id cycle"]
    assert cycles and cycles[0].severity == "error"
    sandbox_42.__dict__["_skip_doctor"] = True
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/mixxxlib/doctor.py`**

```python
"""Pure-read health checks (spec §8.5). Every check returns findings; nothing is modified."""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sqlite3
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from pathlib import Path

from mixxxlib.blobs.beats import KNOWN_VERSIONS
from mixxxlib.db.connection import integrity_check
from mixxxlib.errors import ValidationError
from mixxxlib.model import CUE_TYPES, Finding, MusicalKey, SchemaInfo
from mixxxlib.safety import backup as backup_mod

KNOWN_KEYS_VERSIONS = frozenset({"KeyMap-1.0"})
SAMPLE = 10


@dataclass
class DoctorContext:
    conn: sqlite3.Connection
    schema: SchemaInfo
    backup_dir: Path
    process_check: Callable[[], bool | None]
    nested: bool


def _ids(ctx: DoctorContext, sql: str, *params) -> list[int]:
    return [r[0] for r in ctx.conn.execute(sql, params)]


def check_schema(ctx: DoctorContext) -> list[Finding]:
    severity = {"supported": "info", "unverified": "warning"}.get(ctx.schema.verdict, "error")
    return [Finding("schema", severity, ctx.schema.message,
                    {"version": ctx.schema.version, "ceiling": ctx.schema.ceiling,
                     "capabilities": sorted(ctx.schema.capabilities), "verdict": ctx.schema.verdict})]


def check_mixxx_running(ctx: DoctorContext) -> list[Finding]:
    running = ctx.process_check()
    text = {True: "Mixxx is running", False: "Mixxx is not running", None: "cannot tell whether Mixxx is running (no pgrep)"}[running]
    return [Finding("mixxx_running", "info", text, {"process": running})]


def check_integrity(ctx: DoctorContext) -> list[Finding]:
    result = integrity_check(ctx.conn)
    severity = "info" if result == "ok" else "error"
    return [Finding("integrity", severity, f"PRAGMA integrity_check: {result}", {"result": result})]


ORPHAN_QUERIES: list[tuple[str, str]] = [
    ("crate_tracks.crate_id -> crates.id",
     "SELECT ct.track_id FROM crate_tracks ct LEFT JOIN crates c ON c.id = ct.crate_id WHERE c.id IS NULL"),
    ("crate_tracks.track_id -> library.id",
     "SELECT ct.track_id FROM crate_tracks ct LEFT JOIN library l ON l.id = ct.track_id WHERE l.id IS NULL"),
    ("cues.track_id -> library.id",
     "SELECT cu.id FROM cues cu LEFT JOIN library l ON l.id = cu.track_id WHERE l.id IS NULL"),
    ("PlaylistTracks.playlist_id -> Playlists.id",
     "SELECT pt.id FROM PlaylistTracks pt LEFT JOIN Playlists p ON p.id = pt.playlist_id WHERE p.id IS NULL"),
    ("PlaylistTracks.track_id -> library.id",
     "SELECT pt.id FROM PlaylistTracks pt LEFT JOIN library l ON l.id = pt.track_id WHERE l.id IS NULL"),
    ("library.location -> track_locations.id",
     "SELECT l.id FROM library l LEFT JOIN track_locations tl ON tl.id = l.location WHERE tl.id IS NULL"),
    ("track_analysis.track_id -> library.id",
     "SELECT ta.id FROM track_analysis ta LEFT JOIN library l ON l.id = ta.track_id WHERE l.id IS NULL"),
]


def check_orphans(ctx: DoctorContext) -> list[Finding]:
    findings = []
    queries = list(ORPHAN_QUERIES)
    if ctx.nested:
        queries.append(("crates.parent_id -> crates.id",
                        "SELECT c.id FROM crates c LEFT JOIN crates p ON p.id = c.parent_id WHERE c.parent_id IS NOT NULL AND p.id IS NULL"))
    for relation, sql in queries:
        ids = _ids(ctx, sql)
        if ids:
            findings.append(Finding("orphans", "error", f"{len(ids)} row(s) violate {relation}",
                                    {"relation": relation, "count": len(ids), "sample_ids": ids[:SAMPLE]}))
    if ctx.nested:
        parents = dict(ctx.conn.execute("SELECT id, parent_id FROM crates"))
        cyclic = []
        for start in parents:
            node, steps = parents[start], 0
            while node is not None and steps <= len(parents):
                if node == start:
                    cyclic.append(start)
                    break
                node, steps = parents.get(node), steps + 1
        if cyclic:
            findings.append(Finding("orphans", "error", f"{len(cyclic)} crate(s) sit in a parent_id cycle",
                                    {"relation": "crates.parent_id cycle", "count": len(cyclic), "sample_ids": sorted(cyclic)[:SAMPLE]}))
    return findings


def check_crates(ctx: DoctorContext) -> list[Finding]:
    parent = "parent_id" if ctx.nested else "NULL"
    rows = ctx.conn.execute(f"SELECT name, {parent} AS parent_id, count(*) AS n FROM crates GROUP BY name, {parent} HAVING n > 1 ORDER BY name").fetchall()
    if not rows:
        return []
    duplicates = [{"name": r["name"], "parent_id": r["parent_id"], "count": r["n"]} for r in rows]
    return [Finding("crates", "warning", f"{len(rows)} crate name(s) are duplicated among siblings", {"duplicates": duplicates})]


def check_missing_files(ctx: DoctorContext) -> list[Finding]:
    findings = []
    deleted = _ids(ctx, "SELECT l.id FROM library l JOIN track_locations tl ON tl.id = l.location WHERE tl.fs_deleted = 1 ORDER BY l.id")
    if deleted:
        findings.append(Finding("missing_files", "warning", f"{len(deleted)} track(s) are flagged fs_deleted",
                                {"reason": "fs_deleted", "count": len(deleted), "track_ids": deleted[:SAMPLE]}))
    gone = [tid for tid, loc in ctx.conn.execute(
        "SELECT l.id, tl.location FROM library l JOIN track_locations tl ON tl.id = l.location WHERE tl.fs_deleted = 0 OR tl.fs_deleted IS NULL ORDER BY l.id")
        if not loc or not os.path.exists(loc)]
    if gone:
        findings.append(Finding("missing_files", "warning", f"{len(gone)} track file(s) do not exist on disk",
                                {"reason": "not_on_disk", "count": len(gone), "track_ids": gone[:SAMPLE]}))
    return findings


def check_unanalyzed(ctx: DoctorContext) -> list[Finding]:
    ids = _ids(ctx, "SELECT id FROM library WHERE beats IS NULL OR bpm IS NULL OR bpm = 0 ORDER BY id")
    if not ids:
        return []
    return [Finding("unanalyzed", "info", f"{len(ids)} track(s) have no beatgrid or a zero BPM", {"count": len(ids), "track_ids": ids})]


def check_cues(ctx: DoctorContext) -> list[Finding]:
    findings = []
    zero = _ids(ctx, "SELECT id FROM cues WHERE type = 1 AND color = 0 ORDER BY id")
    if zero:
        findings.append(Finding("cues", "warning", f"{len(zero)} hotcue(s) have colour 0 and will be discarded by Mixxx",
                                {"reason": "zero_colour", "count": len(zero), "cue_ids": zero[:SAMPLE]}))
    dup_rows = ctx.conn.execute("SELECT track_id, type, hotcue, count(*) AS n FROM cues WHERE hotcue >= 0 "
                                "GROUP BY track_id, type, hotcue HAVING n > 1 ORDER BY track_id, type, hotcue").fetchall()
    if dup_rows:
        findings.append(Finding("cues", "warning", f"{len(dup_rows)} (track, type, slot) combination(s) have duplicate cues",
                                {"reason": "duplicate_slot", "count": len(dup_rows),
                                 "slots": [{"track_id": r[0], "type_id": r[1], "slot": r[2], "count": r[3]} for r in dup_rows[:SAMPLE]]}))
    outside = _ids(ctx, "SELECT c.id FROM cues c JOIN library l ON l.id = c.track_id WHERE c.position < 0 OR "
                        "(l.duration IS NOT NULL AND l.samplerate IS NOT NULL AND l.channels IS NOT NULL AND "
                        "c.position + c.length > l.duration * l.samplerate * l.channels) ORDER BY c.id")
    if outside:
        findings.append(Finding("cues", "warning", f"{len(outside)} cue(s) lie outside their track",
                                {"reason": "outside_track", "count": len(outside), "cue_ids": outside[:SAMPLE]}))
    known = ",".join(str(t) for t in CUE_TYPES)
    unknown = _ids(ctx, f"SELECT id FROM cues WHERE type NOT IN ({known}) ORDER BY id")
    if unknown:
        findings.append(Finding("cues", "warning", f"{len(unknown)} cue(s) have an unknown type",
                                {"reason": "unknown_type", "count": len(unknown), "cue_ids": unknown[:SAMPLE]}))
    return findings


def check_keys(ctx: DoctorContext) -> list[Finding]:
    bad = []
    for track_id, text, key_id in ctx.conn.execute("SELECT id, key, key_id FROM library ORDER BY id"):
        try:
            parsed = MusicalKey.parse(text)
        except ValidationError:
            bad.append(track_id)
            continue
        if parsed.id != int(key_id or 0):
            bad.append(track_id)
    if not bad:
        return []
    return [Finding("keys", "warning", f"{len(bad)} track(s) have key text and key_id that disagree", {"count": len(bad), "track_ids": bad[:SAMPLE]})]


def check_blob_versions(ctx: DoctorContext) -> list[Finding]:
    findings = []
    beats = [r[0] for r in ctx.conn.execute("SELECT DISTINCT beats_version FROM library WHERE beats_version IS NOT NULL") if r[0] not in KNOWN_VERSIONS]
    if beats:
        findings.append(Finding("blob_versions", "warning", f"unrecognised beats_version(s): {sorted(beats)}", {"column": "beats_version", "versions": sorted(beats)}))
    keys = [r[0] for r in ctx.conn.execute("SELECT DISTINCT keys_version FROM library WHERE keys_version IS NOT NULL") if r[0] not in KNOWN_KEYS_VERSIONS]
    if keys:
        findings.append(Finding("blob_versions", "warning", f"unrecognised keys_version(s): {sorted(keys)}", {"column": "keys_version", "versions": sorted(keys)}))
    return findings


def check_backups(ctx: DoctorContext) -> list[Finding]:
    entries = [e for e in backup_mod.list_backups(ctx.backup_dir) if e.kind == "database"]
    details = {"backup_dir": str(ctx.backup_dir), "count": len(entries),
               "newest": entries[0].name if entries else None, "oldest": entries[-1].name if entries else None}
    return [Finding("backups", "info", f"{len(entries)} database backup(s) in {ctx.backup_dir}", details)]


CHECKS: list[Callable[[DoctorContext], list[Finding]]] = [
    check_schema, check_mixxx_running, check_integrity, check_orphans, check_crates, check_missing_files,
    check_unanalyzed, check_cues, check_keys, check_blob_versions, check_backups,
]


def run_doctor(ctx: DoctorContext) -> list[Finding]:
    findings: list[Finding] = []
    for check in CHECKS:
        findings.extend(check(ctx))
    return findings


def summarize(findings: list[Finding]) -> dict[str, int]:
    return {"errors": sum(f.severity == "error" for f in findings),
            "warnings": sum(f.severity == "warning" for f in findings),
            "info": sum(f.severity == "info" for f in findings)}
```

```python
                                           backup_dir=self.paths.backup_dir, process_check=self._process_check,
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Add the doctor-at-teardown rule to `tests/conftest.py`**

Replace the `sandbox` and `sandbox_42` fixtures with:

```python
def _doctor_errors(box: Sandbox) -> set[tuple[str, str]]:
    from mixxxlib.library import Library

    lib = Library.open(settings_path=str(box.settings_dir), backup_dir=str(box.backup_dir), process_check=lambda: False)
    return {(f.check, f.message) for f in lib.doctor() if f.severity == "error"}


def _with_doctor_check(box: Sandbox):
    before = _doctor_errors(box)
    yield box
    if box.__dict__.get("_skip_doctor"):
        return
    after = _doctor_errors(box)
    assert after <= before, f"doctor found new error findings: {sorted(after - before)}"


@pytest.fixture
def sandbox(tmp_path, clean_env):
    yield from _with_doctor_check(materialise("mixxxdb.sqlite", tmp_path))


@pytest.fixture
def sandbox_42(tmp_path, clean_env, monkeypatch):
    from mixxxlib.db import schema

    monkeypatch.setattr(schema, "SCHEMA_CEILING", 42)
    yield from _with_doctor_check(materialise("mixxxdb-42.sqlite", tmp_path))
```

Tests that deliberately corrupt the database set `box.__dict__["_skip_doctor"] = True` (as the doctor tests above do).

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (`output.py`): `as_json(obj) -> Any` (dataclasses → dicts; `MusicalKey` → `{id, text}`; `Cue` → spec cue shape with `type` name and `color_hex`); `envelope(command, data, warnings, mutation=None) -> dict`; `mutation_block(result) -> dict`; `dumps(payload) -> str`; `error_text(err) -> str`; `cell(value) -> str`; `table(rows, columns) -> str`; `kv(mapping, indent=0) -> str`; `render_crates`, `render_crate`, `render_tree`, `render_tracks`, `render_track`, `render_cues`, `render_findings`, `render_backups`, `render_mutation`.
- Produces (`cli/common.py`): `Globals` dataclass; `@global_options` decorator; `get_globals(ctx)`; `open_library(ctx) -> Library`; `command(ctx, name)` context manager yielding an `Emitter` with `.emit(data, *, warnings=(), mutation=None, text=None)`; `emit_error(err, json_mode)`; `require_yes(yes, action)`; `read_selectors(values, use_stdin) -> list[str]`; `parse_int(value, what) -> int`; option aliases `DryRunOption`, `YesOption`.
- Produces (`cli/__init__.py`): `app`, `main(argv=None) -> int`. Exit-code mapping happens only in `common.command` and `main`.
- Produces: commands `info`, `settings show [SELECTOR]`.

```python
import json

from mixxxlib import output
from mixxxlib.errors import ValidationError
from mixxxlib.model import Crate, Cue, MusicalKey, MutationResult, WarningMessage


def test_as_json_shapes():
    crate = Crate(1, "A", None, False, True, False, 3)
    assert output.as_json(crate) == {"id": 1, "name": "A", "parent_id": None, "locked": False, "show": True,
                                     "autodj_source": False, "track_count": 3}
    assert output.as_json(MusicalKey(17, "Em")) == {"id": 17, "text": "Em"}
    cue = Cue(4251, 612, 1, 88200.0, 0.0, 0, "drop", 12913160, 1.0, 0.0)
    assert output.as_json(cue) == {"id": 4251, "track_id": 612, "type": "hotcue", "type_id": 1, "slot": 0,
                                   "position_samples": 88200.0, "position_seconds": 1.0, "length_samples": 0.0,
                                   "length_seconds": 0.0, "label": "drop", "color": 12913160, "color_hex": "#C50A08"}
    assert output.as_json({"crate": crate, "ids": (1, 2), "s": frozenset({3, 1})}) == \
        {"crate": output.as_json(crate), "ids": [1, 2], "s": [1, 3]}


def test_envelope_without_and_with_mutation():
    env = output.envelope("crate list", [], [WarningMessage("mixxx_running", "stale", {})])
    assert env == {"ok": True, "command": "crate list", "data": [],
                   "warnings": [{"code": "mixxx_running", "message": "stale", "details": {}}]}
    result = MutationResult(data={"x": 1}, statements=["UPDATE a", "UPDATE b"], backup="/b.sqlite", integrity="ok", dry_run=False)
    env = output.envelope("crate add", result.data, [], result)
    assert env["mutation"] == {"dry_run": False, "backup": "/b.sqlite", "integrity": "ok", "statements": 2}
    dry = MutationResult(data={}, statements=["UPDATE a"], backup=None, integrity=None, dry_run=True, file_operations=["write x"])
    assert output.envelope("track set", {}, [], dry)["mutation"] == {"dry_run": True, "backup": None, "integrity": None,
                                                                      "statements": ["UPDATE a", "write x"]}


def test_dumps_is_deterministic_and_unicode_safe():
    text = output.dumps({"b": 1, "a": "é"})
    assert text == '{"b": 1, "a": "é"}'
    assert json.loads(text) == {"b": 1, "a": "é"}


def test_error_text():
    assert output.error_text(ValidationError("bad slot", slot=0)) == "mixxxlib: validation: bad slot"


def test_table_and_kv():
    rows = [{"id": 1, "name": "A", "locked": False}, {"id": 22, "name": None, "locked": True}]
    assert output.table(rows, ["id", "name", "locked"]).splitlines() == ["id  name  locked", "1   A     no", "22        yes"]
    assert output.kv({"a": 1, "b": {"c": None, "d": 1.5}}).splitlines() == ["a: 1", "b:", "  c: ", "  d: 1.5"]
```

```python
import json

from mixxxlib import __version__
from tests.conftest import run_cli


def test_version():
    r = run_cli("--version")
    assert (r.code, r.stdout.strip()) == (0, __version__)


def test_info_json_envelope(sandbox):
    r = sandbox.run("info", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0 and r.stderr == ""
    payload = r.json
    assert payload["ok"] is True and payload["command"] == "info"
    assert payload["warnings"] == [] and "mutation" not in payload

                              "message": "schema revision 39 is supported"}
    assert data["paths"]["db"] == str(sandbox.db) and data["paths"]["backup_dir"] == str(sandbox.backup_dir)
    assert data["backups"] == {"count": 0, "newest": None}
    assert data["mixxx_running"] in (True, False, None)


def test_global_options_work_before_or_after_the_subcommand(sandbox):
    before = run_cli("--json", "--settings-path", str(sandbox.settings_dir), "info")
    after = run_cli("info", "--settings-path", str(sandbox.settings_dir), "--json")
    assert before.code == after.code == 0
    assert before.json["data"]["paths"] == after.json["data"]["paths"]


def test_info_human_output_is_key_value(sandbox):
    r = sandbox.run("info")
    assert r.code == 0 and "schema:" in r.stdout and "version: 39" in r.stdout


def test_missing_database_is_exit_3_with_json_error_on_stderr(tmp_path, clean_env):
    r = run_cli("--db", str(tmp_path / "none.sqlite"), "info", "--json")
    assert r.code == 3 and r.stdout == ""
    err = r.error
    assert err["ok"] is False and err["error"]["code"] == "not_found" and err["error"]["exit_code"] == 3
    assert err["error"]["details"]["path"].endswith("none.sqlite")
    r = run_cli("--db", str(tmp_path / "none.sqlite"), "info")
    assert r.code == 3 and r.stderr.startswith("mixxxlib: not_found: ")


def test_usage_error_is_exit_2(sandbox):
    r = sandbox.run("info", "--bogus")
    assert r.code == 2 and r.stdout == "" and "--bogus" in r.stderr
    r = sandbox.run("info", "--bogus", "--json")
    assert r.code == 2 and r.error["error"]["code"] == "usage"


def test_settings_show(sandbox):
    r = sandbox.run("settings", "show", "Library.SyncTrackMetadataExport", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0 and r.json["data"] == {"selector": "Library.SyncTrackMetadataExport", "value": "0"}
    r = sandbox.run("settings", "show", "Library")
    assert r.code == 0 and "SyncTrackMetadataExport: 0" in r.stdout
    r = sandbox.run("settings", "show", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0 and "Config" in r.json["data"]["value"]
    r = sandbox.run("settings", "show", "Nope.x", "--json")
    assert r.code == 3 and r.error["error"]["details"] == {"section": "Nope"}


def test_settings_show_without_cfg_is_exit_3(sandbox):
    (sandbox.settings_dir / "mixxx.cfg").unlink()
    r = sandbox.run("settings", "show", "--json")
    assert r.code == 3 and r.error["error"]["code"] == "not_found"
    assert sandbox.run("info", "--json").code == 0  # mixxx.cfg is optional for everything else
```
