- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/mixxxlib/output.py`**

```python
"""JSON envelope, error payloads and plain-text rendering (spec §4, §5)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import dataclasses
import json
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from mixxxlib.errors import MixxxlibError
from mixxxlib.model import (
    BackupEntry, Crate, CrateNode, Cue, Finding, MusicalKey, MutationResult, Track, WarningMessage, format_color,
)


def as_json(obj: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(obj, MusicalKey):
        return obj.to_json()
    if isinstance(obj, Cue):
        return {"id": obj.id, "track_id": obj.track_id, "type": obj.type, "type_id": obj.type_id, "slot": obj.slot,
                "position_samples": obj.position_samples, "position_seconds": obj.position_seconds,
                "length_samples": obj.length_samples, "length_seconds": obj.length_seconds,
                "label": obj.label, "color": obj.color, "color_hex": format_color(obj.color)}
    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(obj) and not isinstance(obj, type):
        return {f.name: as_json(getattr(obj, f.name)) for f in dataclasses.fields(obj)}
    if isinstance(obj, dict):
        return {str(k): as_json(v) for k, v in obj.items()}
    if isinstance(obj, (list, tuple)):
        return [as_json(v) for v in obj]
    if isinstance(obj, (set, frozenset)):
        return [as_json(v) for v in sorted(obj)]
    if isinstance(obj, Path):
        return str(obj)
    return obj


def mutation_block(result: MutationResult) -> dict[str, Any]:
    operations = list(result.statements) + list(result.file_operations)
    return {"dry_run": result.dry_run, "backup": result.backup, "integrity": result.integrity,
            "statements": operations if result.dry_run else len(operations)}


def envelope(command: str, data: Any, warnings: list[WarningMessage] | tuple[WarningMessage, ...],
             mutation: MutationResult | None = None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    payload: dict[str, Any] = {"ok": True, "command": command, "data": as_json(data), "warnings": as_json(list(warnings))}
    if mutation is not None:
        payload["mutation"] = mutation_block(mutation)
    return payload


def dumps(payload: Any) -> str:
    return json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False)


def error_text(err: MixxxlibError) -> str:
    return f"mixxxlib: {err.code}: {err.message}"


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- plain text

def cell(value: Any) -> str:
    if value is None:
        return ""
    if isinstance(value, bool):
        return "yes" if value else "no"
    if isinstance(value, float):
        return f"{value:g}"
    return str(value)


def table(rows: list[dict[str, Any]], columns: list[str]) -> str:
    text_rows = [[cell(row.get(col)) for col in columns] for row in rows]
    widths = [max(len(col), *(len(r[i]) for r in text_rows)) if text_rows else len(col) for i, col in enumerate(columns)]
    lines = ["  ".join(col.ljust(widths[i]) for i, col in enumerate(columns)).rstrip()]
    for row in text_rows:
        lines.append("  ".join(value.ljust(widths[i]) for i, value in enumerate(row)).rstrip())
    return "\n".join(lines)


def kv(mapping: dict[str, Any], indent: int = 0) -> str:
    lines = []
    pad = "  " * indent
    for key, value in mapping.items():
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            lines.append(f"{pad}{key}:")
            lines.append(kv(value, indent + 1))
        elif isinstance(value, list):
            lines.append(f"{pad}{key}: {', '.join(cell(v) if not isinstance(v, dict) else dumps(v) for v in value)}")
        else:
            lines.append(f"{pad}{key}: {cell(value)}")
    return "\n".join(lines)


def render_crates(crates: list[Crate]) -> str:
    rows = [{**as_json(c), "tracks": c.track_count} for c in crates]
    return table(rows, ["id", "name", "parent_id", "tracks", "locked", "show", "autodj_source"])


def render_crate(crate: Crate) -> str:
    return kv(as_json(crate))


def render_tree(nodes: list[CrateNode], depth: int = 0) -> str:
    lines = []
    for node in nodes:
        lines.append(f"{'  ' * depth}{node.crate.name} (id {node.crate.id}, {node.crate.track_count} tracks)")
        if node.children:
            lines.append(render_tree(list(node.children), depth + 1))
    return "\n".join(lines)


def render_tracks(tracks: list[Track]) -> str:
    rows = [{"id": t.id, "artist": t.metadata.artist, "title": t.metadata.title, "album": t.metadata.album,
             "bpm": t.metadata.bpm, "key": t.metadata.key.text, "duration": t.audio.duration} for t in tracks]
    return table(rows, ["id", "artist", "title", "album", "bpm", "key", "duration"])


def render_track(track: Track) -> str:
    return kv(as_json(track))


def render_cues(cues: list[Cue]) -> str:
    rows = [{"id": c.id, "type": c.type, "slot": c.slot if c.slot >= 0 else None, "position": c.position_seconds,
             "length": c.length_seconds, "label": c.label, "color": format_color(c.color)} for c in cues]
    return table(rows, ["id", "type", "slot", "position", "length", "label", "color"])


def render_findings(findings: list[Finding]) -> str:
    return "\n".join(f"[{f.severity}] {f.check}: {f.message}" for f in findings)


def render_backups(entries: list[BackupEntry]) -> str:
    rows = [{"name": e.name, "kind": e.kind, "size": e.size, "modified": e.modified, "age_hours": round(e.age_seconds / 3600, 1)} for e in entries]
    return table(rows, ["name", "kind", "size", "modified", "age_hours"])


def render_mutation(result: MutationResult) -> str:
    operations = list(result.statements) + list(result.file_operations)
    if result.dry_run:
        return "\n".join(["dry run; nothing was changed. Would run:"] + [f"  {op}" for op in operations])
    return kv({"backup": result.backup, "integrity": result.integrity, "statements": len(operations)})
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/mixxxlib/cli/common.py`**

```python
"""Shared CLI plumbing: global options, the command context, exit-code mapping (spec §4, §8)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import functools
import inspect
import os
import sys
import traceback
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterable, Iterator
from contextlib import contextmanager
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Annotated, Any, Optional

import click
import typer

from mixxxlib import output
from mixxxlib.errors import ConfirmationRequiredError, InternalError, MixxxlibError, ValidationError
from mixxxlib.library import Library
from mixxxlib.model import MutationResult, WarningMessage


@dataclass
class Globals:
    settings_path: Optional[str] = None
    db: Optional[str] = None
    backup_dir: Optional[str] = None
    json: bool = False


GLOBAL_PARAMS = [
    inspect.Parameter("settings_path", inspect.Parameter.KEYWORD_ONLY, default=None,
                      annotation=Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Option("--settings-path", metavar="DIR", help="Mixxx settings directory.")]),
    inspect.Parameter("db", inspect.Parameter.KEYWORD_ONLY, default=None,
                      annotation=Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Option("--db", metavar="FILE", help="Database file (overrides --settings-path for the database).")]),
    inspect.Parameter("backup_dir", inspect.Parameter.KEYWORD_ONLY, default=None,
                      annotation=Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Option("--backup-dir", metavar="DIR", help="Where backups go.")]),
    inspect.Parameter("json_", inspect.Parameter.KEYWORD_ONLY, default=False,
                      annotation=Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--json", help="Machine-readable output.")]),
]

DryRunOption = Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--dry-run", help="Print the statements and change nothing.")]
YesOption = Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--yes", help="Confirm a destructive operation.")]
StdinOption = Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--stdin", help="Also read one selector per line from stdin.")]
PathsOption = Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--paths", help="Track selectors are file paths, not ids.")]


def get_globals(ctx: typer.Context) -> Globals:
    return ctx.ensure_object(Globals)


def global_options(fn: Callable[..., Any]) -> Callable[..., Any]:
    """Append the four global options to a command so they are accepted after the subcommand too."""
    signature = inspect.signature(fn)
    params = [p for p in signature.parameters.values() if p.kind != inspect.Parameter.VAR_KEYWORD]

    @functools.wraps(fn)
    def wrapper(*args: Any, **kwargs: Any) -> Any:
        ctx = kwargs.get("ctx") or next(a for a in args if isinstance(a, typer.Context))
        g = get_globals(ctx)
        for name in ("settings_path", "db", "backup_dir"):
            value = kwargs.pop(name, None)
            if value is not None:
                setattr(g, name, value)
        if kwargs.pop("json_", False):
            g.json = True
        return fn(*args, **kwargs)

    wrapper.__signature__ = signature.replace(parameters=params + GLOBAL_PARAMS)  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
    return wrapper


def open_library(ctx: typer.Context) -> Library:
    g = get_globals(ctx)
    return Library.open(settings_path=g.settings_path, db=g.db, backup_dir=g.backup_dir)


def emit_error(err: MixxxlibError, json_mode: bool) -> None:
    if json_mode:
        print(output.dumps(err.to_payload()), file=sys.stderr)
    else:
        print(output.error_text(err), file=sys.stderr)


class Emitter:
    def __init__(self, command_name: str, json_mode: bool) -> None:
        self.command_name = command_name
        self.json_mode = json_mode

    def emit(self, data: Any, *, warnings: Iterable[WarningMessage] = (), mutation: MutationResult | None = None,
             text: str | Callable[[], str] | None = None) -> None:
        warning_list = list(warnings) + (list(mutation.warnings) if mutation is not None else [])
        if self.json_mode:
            print(output.dumps(output.envelope(self.command_name, data, warning_list, mutation)))
            return
        for warning in warning_list:
            print(f"mixxxlib: warning: {warning.code}: {warning.message}", file=sys.stderr)
        body = text() if callable(text) else text
        if body is None:
            rendered = output.as_json(data)
            body = output.kv(rendered) if isinstance(rendered, dict) else output.dumps(rendered)
        if body:
            print(body)
        if mutation is not None:
            print(output.render_mutation(mutation))

    def emit_raw(self, text: str) -> None:
        """For outputs that are not JSON even under --json (crate export)."""
        sys.stdout.write(text)


@contextmanager
def command(ctx: typer.Context, name: str) -> Iterator[Emitter]:
    json_mode = get_globals(ctx).json
    try:
        yield Emitter(name, json_mode)
    except MixxxlibError as err:
        emit_error(err, json_mode)
        raise typer.Exit(err.exit_code) from None
    except (click.exceptions.Exit, click.ClickException):
        raise
    except Exception as exc:  # exit 1: always a bug
        if os.environ.get("MIXXXLIB_DEBUG"):
            traceback.print_exc()
        emit_error(InternalError(f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}"), json_mode)
        raise typer.Exit(1) from None


def require_yes(yes: bool, action: str) -> None:
    if not yes:
        raise ConfirmationRequiredError(f"{action} is destructive; pass --yes to confirm", action=action)


def read_selectors(values: Iterable[str], use_stdin: bool) -> list[str]:
    selectors = list(values)
    if use_stdin:
        selectors.extend(line.strip() for line in sys.stdin.read().splitlines() if line.strip())
    return selectors


def parse_int(value: str, what: str) -> int:
    try:
        return int(value)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        raise ValidationError(f"{what} must be an integer, got {value!r}", value=value) from None
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write `src/mixxxlib/cli/info.py` and `src/mixxxlib/cli/settings.py`**

`src/mixxxlib/cli/info.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import typer

from mixxxlib import output
from mixxxlib.cli.common import command, global_options, open_library


@global_options
def info(ctx: typer.Context) -> None:
    """Resolved paths, schema verdict, whether Mixxx is running, backups, version. Pure read."""
    with command(ctx, "info") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        data = lib.info()
        out.emit(data, warnings=lib.read_warnings(), text=lambda: output.kv(data))
```

`src/mixxxlib/cli/settings.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Annotated, Optional

import typer

from mixxxlib import output
from mixxxlib.cli.common import command, global_options, open_library
from mixxxlib.settings import select

app = typer.Typer(no_args_is_help=True, help="Mixxx settings (mixxx.cfg) introspection.")


@app.command("show")
@global_options
def show(ctx: typer.Context,
         selector: Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Argument(metavar="[SECTION[.KEY]]", help="Section or section.key.")] = None) -> None:
    """Print mixxx.cfg, one section, or one value."""
    with command(ctx, "settings show") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        value = select(lib.settings, selector)
        data = {"selector": selector, "value": value}
        text = output.kv(value) if isinstance(value, dict) else str(value)
        out.emit(data, warnings=lib.read_warnings(), text=text)
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Replace `src/mixxxlib/cli/__init__.py`**

```python
"""Root Typer app. Thin shell over mixxxlib.library; maps errors to exit codes in exactly one place (common.command/main)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import sys
from typing import Annotated, Optional

import click
import typer

from mixxxlib import __version__
from mixxxlib.cli import info as info_cmd, settings as settings_cmd
from mixxxlib.cli.common import Globals, emit_error
from mixxxlib.errors import InternalError, UsageError

app = typer.Typer(
    name="mixxxlib",
    add_completion=False,
    rich_markup_mode=None,
    pretty_exceptions_enable=False,
    no_args_is_help=True,
    help="Offline reader/writer for a Mixxx library. Never run while Mixxx is open.",
)


def _version(value: bool) -> None:
    if value:
        typer.echo(__version__)
        raise typer.Exit(0)


@app.callback()
def root(
    ctx: typer.Context,
    settings_path: Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Option("--settings-path", metavar="DIR", help="Mixxx settings directory.")] = None,
    db: Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Option("--db", metavar="FILE", help="Database file.")] = None,
    backup_dir: Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Option("--backup-dir", metavar="DIR", help="Where backups go.")] = None,
    json_: Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--json", help="Machine-readable output.")] = False,
    version: Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--version", callback=_version, is_eager=True, help="Print the version and exit.")] = False,
) -> None:
    ctx.obj = Globals(settings_path=settings_path, db=db, backup_dir=backup_dir, json=json_)


app.command("info")(info_cmd.info)
app.add_typer(settings_cmd.app, name="settings")


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    args = list(sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv)
    json_mode = "--json" in args
    try:
        app(args=args, standalone_mode=False, prog_name="mixxxlib")
    except click.exceptions.Exit as exc:
        return int(exc.exit_code or 0)
    except click.UsageError as exc:
        if type(exc).__name__ == "NoArgsIsHelpError":
            typer.echo(exc.format_message(), err=True)
            return 2
        emit_error(UsageError(exc.format_message()), json_mode)
        return 2
    except click.ClickException as exc:
        emit_error(InternalError(exc.format_message()), json_mode)
        return 1
    except click.Abort:
        return 1
    return 0
```

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (`db/tracks.py`): `WRITABLE_FIELDS: dict[str, str]` (field → column; `key` maps to `key` and also writes `key_id`); `FILE_BACKED_FIELDS: frozenset[str]`; `row_to_track(row) -> tuple[Track, list[WarningMessage]]`; `list_tracks(conn, *, has_tuning, crate_ids=None, search=None) -> list[Track]`; `get_track(conn, track_id, *, has_tuning) -> Track`; `get_track_by_path(conn, path, *, has_tuning) -> Track`; `resolve_track_ids(conn, selectors, *, paths, missing_error=ValidationError) -> list[int]`; `escape_like(text) -> str`.

- Produces: commands `track list [--crate CRATE] [--id] [--parent CRATE] [--recursive] [--search TEXT]`, `track show TRACK [--paths]`.

`tests/integration/test_cli_track.py`:

```python
import pytest

TRACK_KEYS = ["id", "location", "metadata", "audio", "bookkeeping", "analysis", "coverart"]
METADATA_KEYS = ["artist", "title", "album", "album_artist", "year", "genre", "tracknumber", "tracktotal", "composer",
                 "grouping", "comment", "rating", "color", "url", "bpm_lock", "bpm", "key"]


def test_track_list_json_is_sorted_by_id_with_the_documented_shape(sandbox, manifest):
    r = sandbox.run("track", "list", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0
    tracks = r.json["data"]
    assert [t["id"] for t in tracks] == manifest["tracks"]
    first = tracks[0]
    assert list(first) == TRACK_KEYS
    assert list(first["metadata"]) == METADATA_KEYS
    assert list(first["location"]) == ["id", "path", "filename", "directory", "filesize", "fs_deleted"]
    assert list(first["audio"]) == ["duration", "bitrate", "samplerate", "channels", "filetype", "replaygain", "replaygain_peak", "tuning_frequency_hz"]
    assert list(first["analysis"]) == ["beats_version", "beats_sub_version", "keys_version", "keys_sub_version", "beatgrid"]
    assert set(first["metadata"]["key"]) == {"id", "text"}
    assert first["audio"]["tuning_frequency_hz"] is None
    assert first["location"]["path"].startswith(str(sandbox.audio))


def test_json_is_byte_identical_across_runs(sandbox):
    assert sandbox.run("track", "list", "--json").stdout == sandbox.run("track", "list", "--json").stdout


def test_nulls_stay_null_and_beatgrid_decodes(sandbox, manifest):
    r = sandbox.run("track", "show", str(manifest["unanalyzed_tracks"][0]), "--json")
    data = r.json["data"]
    assert data["track"]["analysis"]["beatgrid"] == {"version": None, "decoded": False, "bpm": None, "first_beat_frame": None, "first_beat_seconds": None}
    r = sandbox.run("track", "show", str(manifest["hotcue_track"]), "--json")
    track = r.json["data"]["track"]
    grid = track["analysis"]["beatgrid"]
    assert grid["decoded"] is True and grid["bpm"] == track["metadata"]["bpm"]
    assert grid["first_beat_seconds"] == pytest.approx(grid["first_beat_frame"] / track["audio"]["samplerate"])
    assert r.json["data"]["beatgrid"] == grid
    assert track["metadata"]["color"] == 0xFF8000
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM library WHERE album IS NULL") == sum(1 for t in sandbox.run("track", "list", "--json").json["data"] if t["metadata"]["album"] is None)


def test_track_list_filters(sandbox, manifest):
    r = sandbox.run("track", "list", "--crate", "Fixture Loops", "--json")
    assert [t["id"] for t in r.json["data"]] == sorted(manifest["crate_members"]["loops"])
    r = sandbox.run("track", "list", "--crate", str(manifest["crates"]["loops"]), "--id", "--json")
    assert len(r.json["data"]) == 5
    title = sandbox.scalar("SELECT title FROM library WHERE id = ?", manifest["tracks"][0])
    r = sandbox.run("track", "list", "--search", title.upper()[:4], "--json")
    assert manifest["tracks"][0] in [t["id"] for t in r.json["data"]]
    r = sandbox.run("track", "list", "--search", "100%_nothing", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0 and r.json["data"] == []


def test_track_show_by_path_and_not_found(sandbox, manifest):
    track_id = manifest["tracks"][2]
    path = sandbox.audio / manifest["audio"][str(track_id)]
    r = sandbox.run("track", "show", str(path), "--paths", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0 and r.json["data"]["track"]["id"] == track_id
    r = sandbox.run("track", "show", "999999", "--json")
    assert r.code == 3 and r.error["error"]["details"] == {"track_id": 999999}
    r = sandbox.run("track", "show", "/nowhere.aiff", "--paths", "--json")
    assert r.code == 3 and r.error["error"]["details"] == {"path": "/nowhere.aiff"}
    r = sandbox.run("track", "show", "abc", "--json")
    assert r.code == 4


def test_track_human_output(sandbox, manifest):
    r = sandbox.run("track", "list")
    assert r.code == 0 and r.stdout.splitlines()[0].startswith("id  ")
    r = sandbox.run("track", "show", str(manifest["tracks"][0]))
    assert r.code == 0 and "metadata:" in r.stdout


def test_recursive_crate_listing_at_42(sandbox_42, manifest_42):
    r = sandbox_42.run("track", "list", "--crate", "Genres", "--recursive", "--json")
    assert [t["id"] for t in r.json["data"]] == sorted(manifest_42["crate_members"]["bass"] + manifest_42["crate_members"]["dubstep"])
    r = sandbox_42.run("track", "list", "--crate", "Dupe", "--json")
    assert r.code == 4


def test_recursive_needs_41(sandbox):
    r = sandbox.run("track", "list", "--crate", "Fixture Loops", "--recursive", "--json")
    assert r.code == 6 and r.error["error"]["details"]["needs"] == 41
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/mixxxlib/db/tracks.py`**

```python
"""All SQL for library + track_locations (spec §6.2, §8.3). Always join; never assume id == location."""

from __future__ import annotations

import os
import sqlite3
from collections.abc import Iterable

from mixxxlib.blobs import beats
from mixxxlib.errors import MixxxlibError, NotFoundError, ValidationError
from mixxxlib.model import (
    Analysis, Audio, Bookkeeping, CoverArt, Location, Metadata, MusicalKey, Track, WarningMessage,
)

# field name → library column. `key` also writes key_id (never independently).
WRITABLE_FIELDS: dict[str, str] = {
    "artist": "artist", "title": "title", "album": "album", "album_artist": "album_artist", "year": "year",
    "genre": "genre", "tracknumber": "tracknumber", "tracktotal": "tracktotal", "composer": "composer",
    "grouping": "grouping", "comment": "comment", "rating": "rating", "color": "color", "url": "url",
    "bpm_lock": "bpm_lock", "bpm": "bpm", "key": "key",
}
FILE_BACKED_FIELDS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({
    "artist", "title", "album", "album_artist", "year", "genre", "tracknumber", "tracktotal", "composer",
    "grouping", "comment", "bpm", "key",
})
BLOB_AUTHORITATIVE_FIELDS: frozenset[str] = frozenset({"bpm", "key"})


def _select(has_tuning: bool) -> str:
    tuning = "l.tuning_frequency_hz" if has_tuning else "NULL"
    return (
        f"SELECT l.*, {tuning} AS tuning_frequency_hz, tl.id AS tl_id, tl.location AS tl_location, "
        "tl.filename AS tl_filename, tl.directory AS tl_directory, tl.filesize AS tl_filesize, "
        "tl.fs_deleted AS tl_fs_deleted FROM library l JOIN track_locations tl ON tl.id = l.location"
    )


def _float(value) -> float | None:
    return None if value is None else float(value)


def _int(value) -> int | None:
    return None if value is None else int(value)


def row_to_track(row: sqlite3.Row) -> tuple[Track, list[WarningMessage]]:
    grid, warnings = beats.decode(row["beats"], row["beats_version"], row["samplerate"])
    track = Track(
        id=row["id"],
        location=Location(id=row["tl_id"], path=row["tl_location"], filename=row["tl_filename"],
                          directory=row["tl_directory"], filesize=_int(row["tl_filesize"]), fs_deleted=bool(row["tl_fs_deleted"])),
        metadata=Metadata(artist=row["artist"], title=row["title"], album=row["album"], album_artist=row["album_artist"],
                          year=row["year"], genre=row["genre"], tracknumber=row["tracknumber"], tracktotal=row["tracktotal"],
                          composer=row["composer"], grouping=row["grouping"], comment=row["comment"],
                          rating=_int(row["rating"]), color=_int(row["color"]), url=row["url"], bpm_lock=bool(row["bpm_lock"]),
                          bpm=_float(row["bpm"]), key=MusicalKey.from_db(row["key"], row["key_id"])),
        audio=Audio(duration=_float(row["duration"]), bitrate=_int(row["bitrate"]), samplerate=_int(row["samplerate"]),
                    channels=_int(row["channels"]), filetype=row["filetype"], replaygain=_float(row["replaygain"]),
                    replaygain_peak=_float(row["replaygain_peak"]), tuning_frequency_hz=_float(row["tuning_frequency_hz"])),
        bookkeeping=Bookkeeping(datetime_added=row["datetime_added"], played=bool(row["played"]),
                                timesplayed=_int(row["timesplayed"]), last_played_at=row["last_played_at"],
                                mixxx_deleted=bool(row["mixxx_deleted"]), header_parsed=bool(row["header_parsed"]),
                                source_synchronized_ms=_int(row["source_synchronized_ms"])),
        analysis=Analysis(beats_version=row["beats_version"], beats_sub_version=row["beats_sub_version"],
                          keys_version=row["keys_version"], keys_sub_version=row["keys_sub_version"], beatgrid=grid),
        coverart=CoverArt(source=_int(row["coverart_source"]), type=_int(row["coverart_type"]),
                          location=row["coverart_location"], hash=_int(row["coverart_hash"]), color=_int(row["coverart_color"])),
    )
    return track, warnings


def escape_like(text: str) -> str:
    return text.replace("\\", "\\\\").replace("%", "\\%").replace("_", "\\_")


def list_tracks(conn: sqlite3.Connection, *, has_tuning: bool, crate_ids: list[int] | None = None,
                search: str | None = None) -> list[Track]:
    sql, params, where = _select(has_tuning), [], []
    if crate_ids is not None:
        if not crate_ids:
            return []
        marks = ",".join("?" * len(crate_ids))
        sql = sql.replace("SELECT l.*", "SELECT DISTINCT l.*", 1)
        sql += f" JOIN crate_tracks ct ON ct.track_id = l.id"
        where.append(f"ct.crate_id IN ({marks})")
        params.extend(crate_ids)
    if search:
        pattern = f"%{escape_like(search)}%"
        where.append("(l.artist LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\' OR l.title LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\' OR l.album LIKE ? ESCAPE '\\')")
        params.extend([pattern, pattern, pattern])
    if where:
        sql += " WHERE " + " AND ".join(where)
    sql += " ORDER BY l.id"
    return [row_to_track(row)[0] for row in conn.execute(sql, params)]


def get_track(conn: sqlite3.Connection, track_id: int, *, has_tuning: bool) -> Track:
    row = conn.execute(_select(has_tuning) + " WHERE l.id = ?", (track_id,)).fetchone()
    if row is None:
        raise NotFoundError(f"track {track_id} does not exist", track_id=track_id)
    return row_to_track(row)[0]


def get_track_by_path(conn: sqlite3.Connection, path: str, *, has_tuning: bool) -> Track:
    absolute = os.path.abspath(path)
    row = conn.execute(_select(has_tuning) + " WHERE tl.location = ?", (absolute,)).fetchone()
    if row is None:
        raise NotFoundError(f"no track at {absolute}", path=absolute)
    return row_to_track(row)[0]


def resolve_track_ids(conn: sqlite3.Connection, selectors: Iterable[str], *, paths: bool,
                      missing_error: type[MixxxlibError] = ValidationError) -> list[int]:
    """Ids as ints; with `paths`, file paths resolved through track_locations.location after abspath."""
    ids: list[int] = []
    unknown: list[str] = []
    for selector in selectors:
        if paths:
            absolute = os.path.abspath(selector)
            row = conn.execute("SELECT l.id FROM library l JOIN track_locations tl ON tl.id = l.location WHERE tl.location = ?",
                               (absolute,)).fetchone()
            if row is None:
                unknown.append(absolute)
            else:
                ids.append(row[0])
        else:
            try:
                ids.append(int(selector))
            except ValueError:
                raise ValidationError(f"track id must be an integer, got {selector!r} (use --paths for file paths)", value=selector) from None
    if unknown:
        raise missing_error(f"{len(unknown)} path(s) are not in the library: {unknown[:5]}", paths=unknown)
    return ids
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add `TracksAPI` to `src/mixxxlib/library.py`**

Add the import `from mixxxlib.db import tracks as tracks_sql`, `from mixxxlib.model import Track`, set `self.tracks = TracksAPI(self)` in `Library.__init__`, and add:

```python
class TracksAPI:
    def __init__(self, lib: Library) -> None:
        self._lib = lib

    @property
    def _has_tuning(self) -> bool:
        return "tuning_frequency" in self._lib.schema.capabilities

    def list(self, *, crate: Crate | None = None, search: str | None = None, recursive: bool = False) -> list[Track]:
        crate_ids = self._lib.crates.subtree_ids(crate, recursive=recursive) if crate is not None else None
        with self._lib.read() as conn:
            return tracks_sql.list_tracks(conn, has_tuning=self._has_tuning, crate_ids=crate_ids, search=search)

    def get(self, track_id: int) -> Track:
        with self._lib.read() as conn:
            return tracks_sql.get_track(conn, int(track_id), has_tuning=self._has_tuning)

    def get_by_path(self, path: str) -> Track:
        with self._lib.read() as conn:
            return tracks_sql.get_track_by_path(conn, path, has_tuning=self._has_tuning)

    def resolve(self, selectors: Iterable[str], *, paths: bool, missing_error=ValidationError) -> list[int]:
        with self._lib.read() as conn:
            return tracks_sql.resolve_track_ids(conn, selectors, paths=paths, missing_error=missing_error)
```

(`ValidationError` needs importing from `mixxxlib.errors` in `library.py`.)

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/mixxxlib/cli/track.py`**

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Annotated, Optional

import typer

from mixxxlib import output
from mixxxlib.cli.common import PathsOption, command, global_options, open_library, parse_int
from mixxxlib.errors import NotFoundError
from mixxxlib.library import Library
from mixxxlib.model import Crate, Track

app = typer.Typer(no_args_is_help=True, help="Track metadata, analysis summary and cues.")


def resolve_crate(lib: Library, selector: str, *, by_id: bool, parent: str | None) -> Crate:
    parent_crate = lib.crates.get(parent) if parent else None
    if by_id:
        return lib.crates.get(parse_int(selector, "crate id"), by_id=True)
    return lib.crates.get(selector, parent=parent_crate)


def resolve_track(lib: Library, selector: str, *, paths: bool) -> Track:
    if paths:
        return lib.tracks.get_by_path(selector)
    return lib.tracks.get(parse_int(selector, "track id"))


@app.command("list")
@global_options
def list_(ctx: typer.Context,
          crate: Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Option("--crate", metavar="CRATE", help="Only tracks in this crate.")] = None,
          by_id: Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--id", help="CRATE is an integer id.")] = False,
          parent: Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Option("--parent", metavar="CRATE", help="Disambiguate CRATE by its parent (schema >= 41).")] = None,
          recursive: Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--recursive", help="Include descendant crates' tracks (schema >= 41).")] = False,
          search: Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Option("--search", metavar="TEXT", help="Case-insensitive match on artist, title or album.")] = None) -> None:
    """List tracks, sorted by id."""
    with command(ctx, "track list") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        crate_obj = resolve_crate(lib, crate, by_id=by_id, parent=parent) if crate else None
        tracks = lib.tracks.list(crate=crate_obj, search=search, recursive=recursive)
        out.emit(tracks, warnings=lib.read_warnings(), text=lambda: output.render_tracks(tracks))


@app.command("show")
@global_options
def show(ctx: typer.Context, track: Annotated[str, typer.Argument(metavar="TRACK")], paths: PathsOption = False) -> None:
    """One track with its analysis summary and cues."""
    with command(ctx, "track show") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        obj = resolve_track(lib, track, paths=paths)
        data = {"track": obj, "beatgrid": obj.analysis.beatgrid, "cues": []}
        out.emit(data, warnings=lib.read_warnings(), text=lambda: output.render_track(obj))
```

`tests/integration/test_cli_crate.py`:

```python
import io
import json

import pytest


def test_list_show_tree(sandbox, manifest):
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "list", "--json")
    names = [c["name"] for c in r.json["data"]]
    assert names == sorted(names) and "Fixture Loops" in names
    assert set(r.json["data"][0]) == {"id", "name", "parent_id", "locked", "show", "autodj_source", "track_count"}
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "show", "Fixture Loops", "--json")
    assert r.json["data"] == {"crate": r.json["data"]["crate"]} and r.json["data"]["crate"]["track_count"] == 5
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "show", "Fixture Loops", "--tracks", "--json")
    assert [t["id"] for t in r.json["data"]["tracks"]] == sorted(manifest["crate_members"]["loops"])
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "show", str(manifest["crates"]["loops"]), "--id")
    assert r.code == 0 and "name: Fixture Loops" in r.stdout
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "tree", "--json")
    assert all(node["children"] == [] for node in r.json["data"])
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "list")
    assert r.stdout.splitlines()[0].startswith("id  name")


def test_create_rename_update_delete(sandbox):
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "create", "Fresh", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0 and r.json["data"]["crate"]["name"] == "Fresh"
    assert r.json["mutation"]["dry_run"] is False and r.json["mutation"]["integrity"] == "ok" and r.json["mutation"]["statements"] == 1
    assert r.json["mutation"]["backup"].startswith(str(sandbox.backup_dir))
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "create", "Fresh", "--json")
    assert r.code == 4 and r.error["error"]["code"] == "validation"
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "rename", "Fresh", "Fresher", "--json")
    assert r.json["data"]["crate"]["name"] == "Fresher"
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "update", "Fresher", "--locked", "--no-show", "--json")
    c = r.json["data"]["crate"]
    assert (c["locked"], c["show"]) == (True, False)
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "delete", "Fresher", "--yes", "--json")
    assert r.code == 4  # locked
    sandbox.run("crate", "update", "Fresher", "--no-locked")
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "delete", "Fresher", "--json")
    assert r.code == 8 and r.error["error"]["code"] == "confirmation_required"
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "delete", "Fresher", "--yes", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0 and r.json["data"]["deleted"][0]["crate"]["name"] == "Fresher"
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM crates WHERE name = 'Fresher'") == 0


def test_membership_commands(sandbox, manifest, monkeypatch):
    t = manifest["tracks"]
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "add", "Empty Crate", str(t[0]), str(t[1]), "--json")
    assert r.code == 0 and r.json["data"]["added"] == [t[0], t[1]]
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "add", "Empty Crate", str(t[1]), "--json")
    assert r.json["data"]["already_member"] == [t[1]] and r.json["warnings"][0]["code"] == "already_member"
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "add", "Empty Crate", "999999", "--json")
    assert r.code == 4 and r.error["error"]["details"] == {"track_ids": [999999]}
    monkeypatch.setattr("sys.stdin", io.StringIO(f"{t[2]}\n\n{t[3]}\n"))
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "add", "Empty Crate", "--stdin", "--json")
    assert r.json["data"]["added"] == [t[2], t[3]]
    path = sandbox.audio / manifest["audio"][str(t[4])]
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "add", "Empty Crate", str(path), "--paths", "--json")
    assert r.json["data"]["added"] == [t[4]]
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "add", "Empty Crate", "/nowhere.aiff", "--paths", "--json")
    assert r.code == 4
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "remove", "Empty Crate", str(t[0]), "--json")
    assert r.code == 8
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "remove", "Empty Crate", str(t[0]), str(t[9]), "--yes", "--json")
    assert r.json["data"] == {"crate": r.json["data"]["crate"], "removed": [t[0]], "not_member": [t[9]]}
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "set-tracks", "Empty Crate", str(t[1]), str(t[7]), "--yes", "--json")
    d = r.json["data"]
    assert (d["added"], sorted(d["removed"]), d["unchanged"]) == ([t[7]], sorted([t[2], t[3], t[4]]), [t[1]])
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "set-tracks", "Empty Crate", "--yes", "--json")
    assert r.json["data"]["crate"]["track_count"] == 0


def test_dry_run_makes_no_change_and_no_backup(sandbox, manifest):
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "delete", "Fixture Loops", "--dry-run", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0 and r.json["mutation"]["dry_run"] is True and r.json["mutation"]["backup"] is None
    assert r.json["mutation"]["statements"][0].startswith("DELETE FROM crate_tracks WHERE crate_id = ")
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM crates WHERE name = 'Fixture Loops'") == 1
    assert not sandbox.backup_dir.exists()
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "create", "Dry", "--dry-run")
    assert r.code == 0 and "dry run" in r.stdout and "INSERT INTO crates" in r.stdout


def test_export_m3u8_ignores_json(sandbox, manifest):
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "export", "Fixture Loops", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0 and r.stdout.startswith("#EXTM3U\n")
    with pytest.raises(json.JSONDecodeError):
        r.json
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "export", "Fixture Loops", "--format", "xspf", "--json")
    assert r.code == 4


def test_nested_commands_below_41(sandbox):
    assert sandbox.run("crate", "move", "Empty Crate", "--root", "--json").code == 6
    assert sandbox.run("crate", "create", "Kid", "--parent", "Empty Crate", "--json").code == 6
    assert sandbox.run("crate", "list", "--parent", "Empty Crate", "--json").code == 6
    assert sandbox.run("crate", "show", "Empty Crate", "--recursive", "--json").code == 6
    r = sandbox.run("crate", "list", "--root", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0 and len(r.json["data"]) == sandbox.scalar("SELECT count(*) FROM crates")


def test_nested_commands_at_42(sandbox_42, manifest_42):
    c = manifest_42["crates"]
    r = sandbox_42.run("crate", "show", "Dupe", "--json")
    assert r.code == 4 and sorted(x["id"] for x in r.error["error"]["details"]["candidates"]) == sorted([c["dupe_a"], c["dupe_b"]])
    r = sandbox_42.run("crate", "show", "Bass", "--parent", "Genres", "--tracks", "--recursive", "--json")
    assert [t["id"] for t in r.json["data"]["tracks"]] == sorted(manifest_42["crate_members"]["bass"] + manifest_42["crate_members"]["dubstep"])
    r = sandbox_42.run("crate", "tree", "--json")
    genres = next(n for n in r.json["data"] if n["crate"]["name"] == "Genres")
    assert genres["children"][0]["crate"]["name"] == "Bass" and genres["children"][0]["children"][0]["crate"]["name"] == "Dubstep"
    r = sandbox_42.run("crate", "list", "--parent", "Genres", "--json")
    assert [x["name"] for x in r.json["data"]] == ["Bass"]
    r = sandbox_42.run("crate", "create", "Dupe", "--json")
    assert r.code == 4
    r = sandbox_42.run("crate", "create", "Techno", "--parent", "Genres", "--json")
    assert r.json["data"]["crate"]["parent_id"] == c["genres"]
    techno_id = r.json["data"]["crate"]["id"]
    r = sandbox_42.run("crate", "move", "Dubstep", "--parent", "Techno", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0 and r.json["data"]["crate"]["parent_id"] == techno_id
    r = sandbox_42.run("crate", "move", "Genres", "--parent", "Dubstep", "--json")
    assert r.code == 4 and r.error["error"]["details"]["reason"] == "cycle"
    r = sandbox_42.run("crate", "move", "Dubstep", "--root", "--json")
    assert r.json["data"]["crate"]["parent_id"] is None
    r = sandbox_42.run("crate", "delete", "Genres", "--yes", "--json")
    assert r.code == 4 and "children" in r.error["error"]["details"]
    r = sandbox_42.run("crate", "delete", "Genres", "--yes", "--recursive", "--json")
    assert sorted(d["crate"]["name"] for d in r.json["data"]["deleted"]) == ["Bass", "Genres", "Techno"]
    r = sandbox_42.run("crate", "tree")
    assert r.code == 0 and "Dubstep" in r.stdout
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `src/mixxxlib/cli/crate.py`**

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Annotated, Optional

import typer

from mixxxlib import output
from mixxxlib.cli.common import (
    DryRunOption, PathsOption, StdinOption, YesOption, command, global_options, open_library, read_selectors,
    require_yes, resolve_crate,
)
from mixxxlib.errors import ValidationError
from mixxxlib.library import Library

app = typer.Typer(no_args_is_help=True, help="Crate CRUD and membership. Crates are addressed by name, or by id with --id.")

CrateArg = Annotated[str, typer.Argument(metavar="CRATE", help="Crate name (or id with --id).")]
ByIdOption = Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--id", help="CRATE is an integer id.")]
ParentOption = Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Option("--parent", metavar="CRATE", help="Parent crate (schema >= 41).")]
RecursiveOption = Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--recursive", help="Include descendant crates (schema >= 41).")]
TracksArg = Annotated[Optional[list[str]], typer.Argument(metavar="[TRACK...]", help="Track ids (or paths with --paths).")]


def _track_ids(lib: Library, tracks: list[str] | None, *, paths: bool, use_stdin: bool) -> list[int]:
    selectors = read_selectors(tracks or [], use_stdin)
    return lib.tracks.resolve(selectors, paths=paths)


@app.command("list")
@global_options
def list_(ctx: typer.Context, parent: ParentOption = None,
          root: Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--root", help="Only root crates.")] = False) -> None:
    """All crates sorted by name; with nesting, optionally one level."""
    with command(ctx, "crate list") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        parent_crate = lib.crates.get(parent) if parent else None
        crates = lib.crates.list(parent=parent_crate, roots_only=root)
        out.emit(crates, warnings=lib.read_warnings(), text=lambda: output.render_crates(crates))


@app.command("show")
@global_options
def show(ctx: typer.Context, crate: CrateArg, by_id: ByIdOption = False, parent: ParentOption = None,
         tracks: Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--tracks", help="Include the crate's tracks.")] = False,
         recursive: RecursiveOption = False) -> None:
    """One crate, optionally with its tracks."""
    with command(ctx, "crate show") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        obj = resolve_crate(lib, crate, by_id=by_id, parent=parent)
        data: dict = {"crate": obj}
        if recursive:
            lib.require_nested()
        if tracks:
            data["tracks"] = lib.tracks.list(crate=obj, recursive=recursive)
        text = output.render_crate(obj) + ("\n\n" + output.render_tracks(data["tracks"]) if tracks else "")
        out.emit(data, warnings=lib.read_warnings(), text=text)


@app.command("tree")
@global_options
def tree(ctx: typer.Context) -> None:
    """The crate hierarchy (schema >= 41); a flat list of roots below."""
    with command(ctx, "crate tree") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        nodes = lib.crates.tree()
        out.emit(nodes, warnings=lib.read_warnings(), text=lambda: output.render_tree(nodes))


@app.command("create")
@global_options
def create(ctx: typer.Context, name: Annotated[str, typer.Argument(metavar="NAME")], parent: ParentOption = None,
           dry_run: DryRunOption = False) -> None:
    """Create a crate. A duplicate name among siblings is refused."""
    with command(ctx, "crate create") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        parent_crate = lib.crates.get(parent) if parent else None
        result = lib.crates.create(name, parent=parent_crate, dry_run=dry_run)
        out.emit(result.data, mutation=result, text=lambda: output.render_crate(result.data["crate"]))


@app.command("rename")
@global_options
def rename(ctx: typer.Context, crate: CrateArg, new_name: Annotated[str, typer.Argument(metavar="NEW_NAME")],
           by_id: ByIdOption = False, parent: ParentOption = None, dry_run: DryRunOption = False) -> None:
    """Rename a crate."""
    with command(ctx, "crate rename") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        result = lib.crates.rename(resolve_crate(lib, crate, by_id=by_id, parent=parent), new_name, dry_run=dry_run)
        out.emit(result.data, mutation=result, text=lambda: output.render_crate(result.data["crate"]))


@app.command("move")
@global_options
def move(ctx: typer.Context, crate: CrateArg, by_id: ByIdOption = False,
         parent: Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Option("--parent", metavar="CRATE", help="New parent crate.")] = None,
         root: Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--root", help="Move to the root.")] = False,
         dry_run: DryRunOption = False) -> None:
    """Re-parent a crate and its subtree (schema >= 41). Refuses a cycle."""
    with command(ctx, "crate move") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        if (parent is None) == (not root):
            raise ValidationError("pass exactly one of --parent CRATE or --root")
        lib.require_nested()
        obj = resolve_crate(lib, crate, by_id=by_id, parent=None)
        target = lib.crates.get(parent) if parent else None
        result = lib.crates.move(obj, parent=target, dry_run=dry_run)
        out.emit(result.data, mutation=result, text=lambda: output.render_crate(result.data["crate"]))


@app.command("update")
@global_options
def update(ctx: typer.Context, crate: CrateArg, by_id: ByIdOption = False, parent: ParentOption = None,
           locked: Annotated[Optional[bool], typer.Option("--locked/--no-locked")] = None,
           show_flag: Annotated[Optional[bool], typer.Option("--show/--no-show")] = None,
           autodj_source: Annotated[Optional[bool], typer.Option("--autodj-source/--no-autodj-source")] = None,
           dry_run: DryRunOption = False) -> None:
    """Set the locked / show / autodj_source flags."""
    with command(ctx, "crate update") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        obj = resolve_crate(lib, crate, by_id=by_id, parent=parent)
        result = lib.crates.update(obj, locked=locked, show=show_flag, autodj_source=autodj_source, dry_run=dry_run)
        out.emit(result.data, mutation=result, text=lambda: output.render_crate(result.data["crate"]))


@app.command("delete")
@global_options
def delete(ctx: typer.Context, crate: CrateArg, by_id: ByIdOption = False, parent: ParentOption = None,
           recursive: RecursiveOption = False, yes: YesOption = False, dry_run: DryRunOption = False) -> None:
    """Delete a crate and its membership rows. Needs --yes."""
    with command(ctx, "crate delete") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        obj = resolve_crate(lib, crate, by_id=by_id, parent=parent)
        if not dry_run:
            require_yes(yes, "crate delete")
        result = lib.crates.delete(obj, recursive=recursive, dry_run=dry_run)
        out.emit(result.data, mutation=result,
                 text=lambda: "\n".join(f"deleted {d['crate'].name} (id {d['crate'].id}, {d['track_count']} tracks)" for d in result.data["deleted"]))


@app.command("add")
@global_options
def add(ctx: typer.Context, crate: CrateArg, tracks: TracksArg = None, by_id: ByIdOption = False, parent: ParentOption = None,
        paths: PathsOption = False, stdin: StdinOption = False, dry_run: DryRunOption = False) -> None:
    """Add tracks to a crate (idempotent)."""
    with command(ctx, "crate add") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        obj = resolve_crate(lib, crate, by_id=by_id, parent=parent)
        result = lib.crates.add(obj, _track_ids(lib, tracks, paths=paths, use_stdin=stdin), dry_run=dry_run)
        out.emit(result.data, mutation=result, text=lambda: output.kv({"added": result.data["added"], "already_member": result.data["already_member"]}))


@app.command("remove")
@global_options
def remove(ctx: typer.Context, crate: CrateArg, tracks: TracksArg = None, by_id: ByIdOption = False, parent: ParentOption = None,
           paths: PathsOption = False, stdin: StdinOption = False, yes: YesOption = False, dry_run: DryRunOption = False) -> None:
    """Remove tracks from a crate (idempotent). Needs --yes."""
    with command(ctx, "crate remove") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        obj = resolve_crate(lib, crate, by_id=by_id, parent=parent)
        if not dry_run:
            require_yes(yes, "crate remove")
        result = lib.crates.remove(obj, _track_ids(lib, tracks, paths=paths, use_stdin=stdin), dry_run=dry_run)
        out.emit(result.data, mutation=result, text=lambda: output.kv({"removed": result.data["removed"], "not_member": result.data["not_member"]}))


@app.command("set-tracks")
@global_options
def set_tracks(ctx: typer.Context, crate: CrateArg, tracks: TracksArg = None, by_id: ByIdOption = False, parent: ParentOption = None,
               paths: PathsOption = False, stdin: StdinOption = False, yes: YesOption = False, dry_run: DryRunOption = False) -> None:
    """Make the crate's membership exactly this set. Needs --yes."""
    with command(ctx, "crate set-tracks") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        obj = resolve_crate(lib, crate, by_id=by_id, parent=parent)
        if not dry_run:
            require_yes(yes, "crate set-tracks")
        result = lib.crates.set_tracks(obj, _track_ids(lib, tracks, paths=paths, use_stdin=stdin), dry_run=dry_run)
        out.emit(result.data, mutation=result, text=lambda: output.kv({k: result.data[k] for k in ("added", "removed", "unchanged")}))


@app.command("export")
@global_options
def export(ctx: typer.Context, crate: CrateArg, by_id: ByIdOption = False, parent: ParentOption = None,
           fmt: Annotated[str, typer.Option("--format", help="Only m3u8 in v1.")] = "m3u8",
           recursive: RecursiveOption = False) -> None:
    """Write an #EXTM3U playlist with absolute paths to stdout (--json is ignored)."""
    with command(ctx, "crate export") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        if fmt != "m3u8":
            raise ValidationError(f"unsupported export format {fmt!r}; only m3u8 is available", format=fmt)
        obj = resolve_crate(lib, crate, by_id=by_id, parent=parent)
        out.emit_raw(lib.crates.export_m3u8(obj, recursive=recursive))
```

```python
import os
import time


def test_backup_create_list_prune(sandbox, monkeypatch):
    r = sandbox.run("backup", "create", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0
    first = r.json["data"]["backup"]
    assert first.startswith(str(sandbox.backup_dir / "mixxxdb-")) and first.endswith("-backup-create.sqlite")
    r = sandbox.run("backup", "create", "--label", "before import", "--json")
    second = r.json["data"]["backup"]
    assert second.endswith("-before-import.sqlite") and r.json["data"]["size"] > 0
    r = sandbox.run("backup", "list", "--json")
    names = [b["name"] for b in r.json["data"]["backups"]]
    assert len(names) == 2 and all(b["kind"] == "database" for b in r.json["data"]["backups"])
    r = sandbox.run("backup", "list")
    assert r.code == 0 and "before-import" in r.stdout
    old = time.time() - 90 * 86400
    os.utime(first, (old, old))
    monkeypatch.setenv("MIXXXLIB_BACKUP_KEEP_MIN", "1")
    r = sandbox.run("backup", "prune", "--json")
    assert r.json["data"]["removed"] == [first]
    assert not os.path.exists(first) and os.path.exists(second)


def test_backup_restore(sandbox, manifest):
    original = sandbox.scalar("SELECT name FROM crates WHERE id = ?", manifest["crates"]["loops"])
    backup = sandbox.run("backup", "create", "--json").json["data"]["backup"]
    sandbox.run("crate", "rename", "Fixture Loops", "Changed")
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT name FROM crates WHERE id = ?", manifest["crates"]["loops"]) == "Changed"
    r = sandbox.run("backup", "restore", backup, "--json")
    assert r.code == 8
    r = sandbox.run("backup", "restore", backup, "--yes", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0
    data = r.json["data"]
    assert data["restored_from"] == backup and data["integrity"] == "ok"
    assert data["backup"].endswith("-backup-restore.sqlite") and os.path.exists(data["backup"])
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT name FROM crates WHERE id = ?", manifest["crates"]["loops"]) == original


def test_backup_restore_refuses_bad_candidates(sandbox, tmp_path):
    bogus = tmp_path / "bogus.sqlite"
    bogus.write_bytes(b"not a database at all" * 100)
    r = sandbox.run("backup", "restore", str(bogus), "--yes", "--json")
    assert r.code == 7 and r.error["error"]["code"] == "database"
    r = sandbox.run("backup", "restore", str(tmp_path / "missing.sqlite"), "--yes", "--json")
    assert r.code == 3
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add `BackupsAPI` to `src/mixxxlib/library.py`**

Add imports `import shutil`, `from pathlib import Path`, `from mixxxlib.errors import DatabaseError, NotFoundError`, `from mixxxlib.safety.guard import assert_mixxx_closed`, `from mixxxlib.model import BackupEntry`; set `self.backups = BackupsAPI(self)` in `__init__`; add:

```python
class BackupsAPI:
    def __init__(self, lib: Library) -> None:
        self._lib = lib

    def list(self) -> list[BackupEntry]:
        return backup_mod.list_backups(self._lib.paths.backup_dir)

    def create(self, label: str | None = None) -> Path:
        with closing(conn_mod.open_rw(self._lib.paths.db)) as conn, conn_mod.translating_errors():
            return backup_mod.create_backup(conn, self._lib.paths.backup_dir, label or "backup create")

    def prune(self) -> list[Path]:
        return backup_mod.prune(self._lib.paths.backup_dir, self._lib.retention)

    def restore(self, candidate: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Full mutation guard, back up the current database, verify the candidate, copy it into place."""
        candidate = Path(candidate)
        if not candidate.is_file():
            raise NotFoundError(f"backup file not found at {candidate}", path=str(candidate))
        try:
            with closing(sqlite3.connect(f"{candidate.resolve().as_uri()}?mode=ro", uri=True)) as check:
                result = conn_mod.integrity_check(check)
                version = schema_mod.read_schema_version(check)
        except sqlite3.Error as exc:
            raise DatabaseError(f"candidate is not a readable Mixxx database: {exc}", path=str(candidate)) from exc
        if result != "ok":
            raise DatabaseError(f"candidate failed integrity_check: {result}", path=str(candidate), integrity=result)
        if version is None:
            raise DatabaseError("candidate has no mixxx.schema.version row", path=str(candidate))
        db = self._lib.paths.db
        with closing(conn_mod.open_rw(db)) as conn, conn_mod.translating_errors():
            assert_mixxx_closed(conn, process_check=self._lib._process_check)
            pre = backup_mod.create_backup(conn, self._lib.paths.backup_dir, "backup restore")
        staging = db.with_name(db.name + ".mixxxlib-restore")
        shutil.copyfile(candidate, staging)
        os.replace(staging, db)
        journal = db.with_name(db.name + "-journal")
        if journal.exists():
            journal.unlink()
        with closing(conn_mod.open_ro(db)) as verify:
            integrity = conn_mod.integrity_check(verify)
        return {"restored_from": str(candidate), "backup": str(pre), "integrity": integrity}
```

`src/mixxxlib/cli/doctor.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import typer

from mixxxlib import output
from mixxxlib.cli.common import command, global_options, open_library
from mixxxlib.doctor import summarize


@global_options
def doctor(ctx: typer.Context) -> None:
    """Pure-read health check. Exit 0 when clean, 10 when any error-severity finding exists."""
    with command(ctx, "doctor") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        findings = lib.doctor()
        summary = summarize(findings)
        out.emit({"findings": findings, "summary": summary}, warnings=lib.read_warnings(),
                 text=lambda: output.render_findings(findings) + "\n" + output.kv(summary))
        if summary["errors"]:
            raise typer.Exit(10)
```

```python
from __future__ import annotations

import os
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Annotated, Optional

import typer

from mixxxlib import output
from mixxxlib.cli.common import YesOption, command, global_options, open_library, require_yes

app = typer.Typer(no_args_is_help=True, help="Database backups and tag snapshots.")


@app.command("list")
@global_options
def list_(ctx: typer.Context) -> None:
    """Backups and tag snapshots with size and age, newest first."""
    with command(ctx, "backup list") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        entries = lib.backups.list()
        out.emit({"backup_dir": str(lib.paths.backup_dir), "backups": entries}, text=lambda: output.render_backups(entries))


@app.command("create")
@global_options
def create(ctx: typer.Context, label: Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Option("--label", help="Replaces the command name in the file name.")] = None) -> None:
    """Manual VACUUM INTO backup."""
    with command(ctx, "backup create") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        path = lib.backups.create(label)
        data = {"backup": str(path), "size": os.path.getsize(path)}
        out.emit(data, warnings=lib.read_warnings(), text=lambda: output.kv(data))


@app.command("prune")
@global_options
def prune(ctx: typer.Context) -> None:
    """Apply the retention rule now (TTL 30 days, floor of 10, per series)."""
    with command(ctx, "backup prune") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        removed = [str(p) for p in lib.backups.prune()]
        out.emit({"removed": removed, "retention": {"ttl_days": lib.retention.ttl_days, "keep_min": lib.retention.keep_min}},
                 text=lambda: "\n".join(["removed:"] + [f"  {p}" for p in removed]) if removed else "nothing to prune")


@app.command("restore")
@global_options
def restore(ctx: typer.Context, file: Annotated[Path, typer.Argument(metavar="FILE")], yes: YesOption = False) -> None:
    """Replace the database with FILE after backing up the current one. Needs --yes."""
    with command(ctx, "backup restore") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        require_yes(yes, "backup restore")
        data = lib.backups.restore(file)
        out.emit(data, text=lambda: output.kv(data))
```

Register in `src/mixxxlib/cli/__init__.py`: `from mixxxlib.cli import backup as backup_cmd, doctor as doctor_cmd`, then `app.command("doctor")(doctor_cmd.doctor)` and `app.add_typer(backup_cmd.app, name="backup")`.

```python
import json
import shutil
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from mutagen import id3
from mutagen.aiff import AIFF

from mixxxlib.errors import TagError
from mixxxlib.tags import files

FIXTURE_AUDIO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1] / "fixtures" / "audio"
ALL = {"artist": "A", "title": "T", "album": "L", "album_artist": "AA", "year": "2019", "genre": "G",
       "tracknumber": "3", "tracktotal": "12", "composer": "C", "grouping": "GR", "comment": "hello",
       "bpm": "128", "key": "Em"}


def copy(tmp_path, ext):
    src = next(FIXTURE_AUDIO.glob(f"*.{ext}"))
    dst = tmp_path / src.name
    shutil.copy(src, dst)
    return dst


@pytest.mark.parametrize("ext", ["aiff", "mp3", "flac"])
def test_round_trip_all_fields(tmp_path, ext):
    path = copy(tmp_path, ext)
    assert set(files.read_tags(path)) == files.TAG_FIELDS
    files.write_tags(path, ALL)
    assert files.read_tags(path) == ALL
    files.write_tags(path, {"album": None, "tracktotal": None, "comment": None})
    back = files.read_tags(path)
    assert back["album"] is None and back["tracktotal"] is None and back["comment"] is None
    assert back["tracknumber"] == "3" and back["artist"] == "A"


def test_trck_combines_number_and_total(tmp_path):
    path = copy(tmp_path, "mp3")
    files.write_tags(path, {"tracknumber": "3", "tracktotal": "12"})
    assert str(files.TagFile(path)._audio.tags["TRCK"].text[0]) == "3/12"
    files.write_tags(path, {"tracknumber": None})
    assert str(files.TagFile(path)._audio.tags["TRCK"].text[0]) == "/12"
    files.write_tags(path, {"tracktotal": None})
    assert "TRCK" not in files.TagFile(path)._audio.tags


def test_grouping_reads_grp1_as_fallback_and_writes_tit1(tmp_path):
    path = copy(tmp_path, "aiff")
    audio = AIFF(path)
    audio.tags.delall("TIT1")
    audio.tags.add(id3.GRP1(encoding=3, text=["legacy"]))
    audio.save()
    assert files.read_tags(path)["grouping"] == "legacy"
    files.write_tags(path, {"grouping": "new"})
    audio = AIFF(path)
    assert str(audio.tags["TIT1"].text[0]) == "new"
    assert files.read_tags(path)["grouping"] == "new"


def test_comment_only_touches_the_undescribed_frame(tmp_path):
    path = copy(tmp_path, "mp3")
    audio = files.TagFile(path)._audio
    audio.tags.add(id3.COMM(encoding=3, lang="eng", desc="iTunNORM", text=["keep me"]))
    audio.save()
    files.write_tags(path, {"comment": "mine"})
    comms = files.TagFile(path)._audio.tags.getall("COMM")
    assert {c.desc: c.text[0] for c in comms} == {"iTunNORM": "keep me", "": "mine"}
    assert files.read_tags(path)["comment"] == "mine"


def test_snapshot_and_restore(tmp_path):
    path = copy(tmp_path, "flac")
    before = files.read_tags(path)
    snap = files.snapshot_tags(path, tmp_path / "backups", 42)
    assert snap.name.startswith("tags-") and snap.name.endswith("-42.json")
    payload = json.loads(snap.read_text())
    assert payload["path"] == str(path) and payload["track_id"] == 42 and payload["tags"] == before
    files.write_tags(path, {"artist": "Changed", "title": None})
    files.restore_tags(snap)
    assert files.read_tags(path) == before


def test_tag_write_action_verifies_and_reports_mtime(tmp_path):
    path = copy(tmp_path, "aiff")
    action = files.TagWriteAction(path, 7, {"artist": "Verified"}, tmp_path / "backups")
    assert "artist" in action.describe() and str(path) in action.describe()
    result = action.apply()
    assert result["mtime_ms"] == int(path.stat().st_mtime * 1000)
    assert Path(result["snapshot"]).exists()
    assert files.read_tags(path)["artist"] == "Verified"
    action.rollback()
    assert files.read_tags(path)["artist"] != "Verified"


def test_tag_write_action_restores_on_verification_mismatch(tmp_path, monkeypatch):
    path = copy(tmp_path, "mp3")
    original = files.read_tags(path)
    real_read = files.read_tags
    monkeypatch.setattr(files, "read_tags", lambda p: {**real_read(p), "artist": "WRONG"})
    action = files.TagWriteAction(path, 7, {"artist": "Wanted"}, tmp_path / "backups")
    with pytest.raises(TagError) as e:
        action.apply()
    assert e.value.details["mismatches"] == {"artist": {"expected": "Wanted", "read_back": "WRONG"}}
    monkeypatch.undo()
    assert files.read_tags(path) == original


def test_unsupported_and_missing_files_are_tag_errors(tmp_path):
    text = tmp_path / "notes.txt"
    text.write_text("hi")
    with pytest.raises(TagError):
        files.read_tags(text)
    with pytest.raises(TagError) as e:
        files.read_tags(tmp_path / "missing.mp3")
    assert e.value.details["path"].endswith("missing.mp3")
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/mixxxlib/tags/files.py`**

```python
"""Audio-file tags via mutagen (spec §6.2, §7.4).

MP3 and AIFF carry ID3v2; FLAC (and other Vorbis-comment formats mutagen opens) carry Vorbis comments.
Anything else is refused with TagError.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import mutagen
from mutagen import id3
from mutagen._vorbis import VCommentDict

from mixxxlib.errors import TagError
from mixxxlib.safety.backup import TAG_PREFIX, utc_stamp

ID3_SIMPLE = {"artist": "TPE1", "title": "TIT2", "album": "TALB", "album_artist": "TPE2", "year": "TDRC",
              "genre": "TCON", "composer": "TCOM", "bpm": "TBPM", "key": "TKEY"}
VORBIS_TAGS = {"artist": "ARTIST", "title": "TITLE", "album": "ALBUM", "album_artist": "ALBUMARTIST", "year": "DATE",
               "genre": "GENRE", "tracknumber": "TRACKNUMBER", "tracktotal": "TRACKTOTAL", "composer": "COMPOSER",
               "grouping": "GROUPING", "comment": "COMMENT", "bpm": "BPM", "key": "INITIALKEY"}
TAG_FIELDS: frozenset[str] = frozenset(VORBIS_TAGS)


def _text(frames: list) -> str | None:
    if not frames or not getattr(frames[0], "text", None):
        return None
    return str(frames[0].text[0])


class TagFile:
    def __init__(self, path: os.PathLike[str] | str) -> None:
        self.path = Path(path)
        if not self.path.is_file():
            raise TagError(f"audio file not found: {self.path}", path=str(self.path))
        try:
            audio = mutagen.File(self.path)
        except Exception as exc:  # mutagen raises many types
            raise TagError(f"mutagen could not read {self.path}: {exc}", path=str(self.path)) from exc
        if audio is None:
            raise TagError(f"unsupported audio format: {self.path}", path=str(self.path))
        if isinstance(audio.tags, VCommentDict) or type(audio).__name__ in ("FLAC", "OggVorbis", "OggOpus", "OggFLAC"):
            self.kind = "vorbis"
            if audio.tags is None:
                audio.add_tags()
        elif isinstance(audio.tags, id3.ID3) or type(audio).__name__ in ("MP3", "AIFF", "WAVE", "DSF"):
            self.kind = "id3"
            if audio.tags is None:
                audio.add_tags()
        else:
            raise TagError(f"{self.path} has a tag family mixxxlib does not handle ({type(audio).__name__})", path=str(self.path))
        self._audio = audio

    # -- read -----------------------------------------------------------------

    def read(self) -> dict[str, str | None]:
        if self.kind == "vorbis":
            tags = self._audio.tags
            return {field: (tags[tag][0] if tag in tags and tags[tag] else None) for field, tag in VORBIS_TAGS.items()}
        tags = self._audio.tags
        values: dict[str, str | None] = {field: _text(tags.getall(frame)) for field, frame in ID3_SIMPLE.items()}
        trck = _text(tags.getall("TRCK"))
        number, _, total = (trck or "").partition("/")
        values["tracknumber"] = number or None
        values["tracktotal"] = total or None
        values["grouping"] = _text(tags.getall("TIT1")) or _text(tags.getall("GRP1"))
        comments = tags.getall("COMM")
        preferred = [c for c in comments if c.desc == ""] or comments
        values["comment"] = _text(preferred)
        return values

    # -- write ----------------------------------------------------------------

    def write(self, values: dict[str, str | None]) -> None:
        unknown = set(values) - TAG_FIELDS
        if unknown:
            raise TagError(f"not file-backed fields: {sorted(unknown)}", fields=sorted(unknown))
        try:
            if self.kind == "vorbis":
                self._write_vorbis(values)
            else:
                self._write_id3(values)
            self._audio.save()
        except TagError:
            raise
        except Exception as exc:
            raise TagError(f"mutagen could not write {self.path}: {exc}", path=str(self.path)) from exc

    def _write_vorbis(self, values: dict[str, str | None]) -> None:
        tags = self._audio.tags
        for field, value in values.items():
            tag = VORBIS_TAGS[field]
            if value is None:
                if tag in tags:
                    del tags[tag]
            else:
                tags[tag] = [value]

    def _write_id3(self, values: dict[str, str | None]) -> None:
        tags = self._audio.tags
        current = self.read()
        for field, value in values.items():
            if field in ID3_SIMPLE:
                frame = ID3_SIMPLE[field]
                tags.delall(frame)
                if value is not None:
                    tags.add(getattr(id3, frame)(encoding=3, text=[value]))
            elif field == "grouping":
                tags.delall("TIT1")
                if value is not None:
                    tags.add(id3.TIT1(encoding=3, text=[value]))
            elif field == "comment":
                for comm in tags.getall("COMM"):
                    if comm.desc == "":
                        tags.delall(comm.HashKey)
                if value is not None:
                    tags.add(id3.COMM(encoding=3, lang="eng", desc="", text=[value]))
        if "tracknumber" in values or "tracktotal" in values:
            number = values.get("tracknumber", current["tracknumber"])
            total = values.get("tracktotal", current["tracktotal"])
            tags.delall("TRCK")
            if total:
                tags.add(id3.TRCK(encoding=3, text=[f"{number or ''}/{total}"]))
            elif number:
                tags.add(id3.TRCK(encoding=3, text=[number]))

    def mtime_ms(self) -> int:
        return int(self.path.stat().st_mtime * 1000)


def read_tags(path: os.PathLike[str] | str) -> dict[str, str | None]:
    return TagFile(path).read()


def write_tags(path: os.PathLike[str] | str, values: dict[str, str | None]) -> None:
    TagFile(path).write(values)


def snapshot_tags(path: os.PathLike[str] | str, backup_dir: Path, track_id: int, *, now: datetime | None = None) -> Path:
    stamp = utc_stamp(now or datetime.now(timezone.utc))
    backup_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    target = backup_dir / f"{TAG_PREFIX}{stamp}-{track_id}.json"
    n = 1
    while target.exists():
        n += 1
        target = backup_dir / f"{TAG_PREFIX}{stamp}-{track_id}-{n}.json"
    payload = {"path": str(Path(path)), "track_id": track_id, "taken": stamp, "tags": read_tags(path)}
    target.write_text(json.dumps(payload, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")
    return target


def restore_tags(snapshot_path: os.PathLike[str] | str) -> None:
    payload = json.loads(Path(snapshot_path).read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    write_tags(payload["path"], {field: payload["tags"].get(field) for field in TAG_FIELDS})


class TagWriteAction:
    """FileAction for connection.run_mutation: snapshot → write → re-read to confirm → (rollback on demand)."""

    def __init__(self, path: os.PathLike[str] | str, track_id: int, values: dict[str, str | None], backup_dir: Path) -> None:
        self.path = Path(path)
        self.track_id = track_id
        self.values = dict(values)
        self.backup_dir = backup_dir
        self.snapshot: Path | None = None

    def describe(self) -> str:
        return f"write tags {sorted(self.values)} to {self.path}"

    def apply(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        self.snapshot = snapshot_tags(self.path, self.backup_dir, self.track_id)
        write_tags(self.path, self.values)
        back = read_tags(self.path)
        mismatches = {field: {"expected": value, "read_back": back.get(field)}
                      for field, value in self.values.items() if back.get(field) != value}
        if mismatches:
            restore_tags(self.snapshot)
            raise TagError(f"tags did not read back as written in {self.path}", path=str(self.path), mismatches=mismatches)
        return {"mtime_ms": TagFile(self.path).mtime_ms(), "snapshot": str(self.snapshot)}

    def rollback(self) -> None:
        if self.snapshot is not None:
            restore_tags(self.snapshot)
```

### Task 17: `track set` with two-store writes

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/integration/test_cli_track_set.py`
- Modify: `src/mixxxlib/db/tracks.py` (coercion + `plan_set`), `src/mixxxlib/library.py` (`TracksAPI.set`), `src/mixxxlib/cli/track.py` (`track set`)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (`db/tracks.py`): `coerce_field(name, raw) -> Any`; `file_tag_value(name, value) -> str | None`; `update_sql(columns: list[str]) -> str`; `plan_set(conn, track, changes, clears, *, write_file, write_db, backup_dir) -> MutationPlan`.
- Produces: `TracksAPI.set(track, fields: dict[str, str], *, clear=(), file=True, db=True, dry_run=False) -> MutationResult` (`file=False` is `--mixxx-only`; `db=False` is `--file-only`).
- Produces: command `track set TRACK FIELD=VALUE... [--clear FIELD]... [--mixxx-only | --file-only] [--paths] [--yes] [--dry-run]`. `data` is `{track, changed: {field: {old, new}}, file_written: bool}`.
- Warnings: `blob_authoritative` (bpm/key written), `file_backed` (`--mixxx-only` touched a file-backed field), `no_file_tag` (a database-only field was written while files were in scope).

```python
import os
import sqlite3

import pytest

from mixxxlib.tags.files import read_tags


def path_of(sandbox, manifest, track_id):
    return sandbox.audio / manifest["audio"][str(track_id)]


def test_default_write_updates_file_then_database(sandbox, manifest):
    track_id = manifest["tracks_by_ext"]["aiff"][0]
    path = path_of(sandbox, manifest, track_id)
    r = sandbox.run("track", "set", str(track_id), "artist=New Artist", "title=New Title", "--json")
    assert r.code == 8 and r.error["error"]["code"] == "confirmation_required"
    assert read_tags(path)["artist"] != "New Artist"
    r = sandbox.run("track", "set", str(track_id), "artist=New Artist", "title=New Title", "--yes", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0, r.stderr
    data = r.json["data"]
    assert data["file_written"] is True
    assert data["changed"]["artist"]["new"] == "New Artist" and data["changed"]["title"]["new"] == "New Title"
    assert data["track"]["metadata"]["artist"] == "New Artist"
    assert read_tags(path)["artist"] == "New Artist" and read_tags(path)["title"] == "New Title"
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT artist FROM library WHERE id = ?", track_id) == "New Artist"
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT source_synchronized_ms FROM library WHERE id = ?", track_id) == int(path.stat().st_mtime * 1000)
    assert r.json["warnings"] == []
    assert r.json["mutation"]["statements"] == 2  # one UPDATE + one file write
    assert any(p.name.startswith("tags-") for p in sandbox.backup_dir.iterdir())
    assert any(p.name.startswith("mixxxdb-") and p.name.endswith("-track-set.sqlite") for p in sandbox.backup_dir.iterdir())


@pytest.mark.parametrize("ext", ["mp3", "flac"])
def test_other_formats_round_trip(sandbox, manifest, ext):
    track_id = manifest["tracks_by_ext"][ext][0]
    r = sandbox.run("track", "set", str(track_id), "genre=Bass", "tracknumber=7", "--yes", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0, r.stderr
    tags = read_tags(path_of(sandbox, manifest, track_id))
    assert tags["genre"] == "Bass" and tags["tracknumber"] == "7"


def test_mixxx_only_leaves_the_file_alone_and_warns(sandbox, manifest):
    track_id = manifest["tracks_by_ext"]["mp3"][1]
    path = path_of(sandbox, manifest, track_id)
    before = read_tags(path)
    mtime = path.stat().st_mtime
    r = sandbox.run("track", "set", str(track_id), "album=DB Only", "--mixxx-only", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0 and r.json["data"]["file_written"] is False
    assert [w["code"] for w in r.json["warnings"]] == ["file_backed"]
    assert read_tags(path) == before and path.stat().st_mtime == mtime
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT album FROM library WHERE id = ?", track_id) == "DB Only"


def test_file_only_skips_database_and_backup(sandbox, manifest):

    assert r.code == 0 and r.json["mutation"]["backup"] is None and r.json["mutation"]["integrity"] is None
    assert read_tags(path_of(sandbox, manifest, track_id))["comment"] == "file side"
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT comment FROM library WHERE id = ?", track_id) != "file side"
    assert not any(p.name.startswith("mixxxdb-") for p in sandbox.backup_dir.iterdir())


def test_caveated_fields_and_key_pairing(sandbox, manifest):
    track_id = manifest["tracks_by_ext"]["aiff"][1]
    r = sandbox.run("track", "set", str(track_id), "bpm=128.5", "key=Em", "--yes", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0

    assert sandbox.query("SELECT bpm, key, key_id FROM library WHERE id = ?", track_id) == [(128.5, "Em", 17)]
    assert r.json["data"]["changed"]["key"]["new"] == {"id": 17, "text": "Em"}
    tags = read_tags(path_of(sandbox, manifest, track_id))
    assert tags["bpm"] == "128.5" and tags["key"] == "Em"
    r = sandbox.run("track", "set", str(track_id), "key=17", "--mixxx-only")
    assert r.code == 0 and "blob_authoritative" in r.stderr


def test_database_only_fields(sandbox, manifest):
    track_id = manifest["tracks"][6]
    r = sandbox.run("track", "set", str(track_id), "rating=4", "color=#FF8000", "bpm_lock=true", "url=https://x", "--json")

        r = sandbox.run("track", "set", str(track_id), bad, "--json")

def test_clear_sets_null_and_removes_the_tag(sandbox, manifest):
    track_id = manifest["tracks_by_ext"]["aiff"][2]
    path = path_of(sandbox, manifest, track_id)
    sandbox.run("track", "set", str(track_id), "comment=temp", "--yes")
    r = sandbox.run("track", "set", str(track_id), "--clear", "comment", "--clear", "key", "--yes", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0
    assert sandbox.query("SELECT comment, key, key_id FROM library WHERE id = ?", track_id) == [(None, "", 0)]
    tags = read_tags(path)
    assert tags["comment"] is None and tags["key"] is None
    r = sandbox.run("track", "set", str(track_id), "comment=x", "--clear", "comment", "--json")
    assert r.code == 4


def test_flags_and_dry_run(sandbox, manifest):
    track_id = manifest["tracks"][0]
    r = sandbox.run("track", "set", str(track_id), "artist=x", "--mixxx-only", "--file-only", "--json")
    assert r.code == 4
    r = sandbox.run("track", "set", str(track_id), "artist=Dry", "--dry-run", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0 and r.json["mutation"]["dry_run"] is True
    ops = r.json["mutation"]["statements"]
    assert any("<mtime_ms>" in op for op in ops) and any(op.startswith("write tags ['artist']") for op in ops)
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT artist FROM library WHERE id = ?", track_id) != "Dry"
    assert not sandbox.backup_dir.exists()


def test_database_failure_after_file_write_restores_the_file(sandbox, manifest, monkeypatch):
    from mixxxlib.db import tracks as tracks_sql

    track_id = manifest["tracks_by_ext"]["mp3"][2]
    path = path_of(sandbox, manifest, track_id)
    before = read_tags(path)
    monkeypatch.setattr(tracks_sql, "update_sql", lambda columns: "UPDATE no_such_table SET x = ? WHERE id = ?")
    r = sandbox.run("track", "set", str(track_id), "artist=Doomed", "--yes", "--json")
    assert r.code == 7
    err = r.error["error"]
    assert err["details"]["backup"].endswith(".sqlite") and err["details"]["file_rollback"]
    assert read_tags(path) == before
    assert sandbox.scalar("SELECT artist FROM library WHERE id = ?", track_id) == before["artist"]


def test_track_set_by_path(sandbox, manifest):
    track_id = manifest["tracks"][1]
    path = path_of(sandbox, manifest, track_id)
    r = sandbox.run("track", "set", str(path), "--paths", "rating=5", "--json")
    assert r.code == 0 and r.json["data"]["track"]["id"] == track_id
```
