- [ ] **Step 3: Add coercion and `plan_set` to `src/mixxxlib/db/tracks.py`**

Append (and add the imports `from pathlib import Path`, `from mixxxlib.db.connection import Deferred, MutationPlan, Statement`, `from mixxxlib.model import parse_color`, `from mixxxlib.tags.files import TagWriteAction`):

```python
_TRUE = {"1", "true", "yes", "on"}
_FALSE = {"0", "false", "no", "off"}


def coerce_field(name: str, raw: str) -> Any:
    """Type/range validation only (spec constraint 7). Returns the database value."""
    if name not in WRITABLE_FIELDS:
        raise ValidationError(f"unknown field {name!r}", field=name, fields=sorted(WRITABLE_FIELDS))
    if name == "rating":
        value = _parse_int(raw, name)
        if not 0 <= value <= 5:
            raise ValidationError(f"rating must be 0..5, got {value}", field=name, value=raw)
        return value
    if name == "color":
        return parse_color(raw, allow_zero=True)
    if name == "bpm":
        try:
            value = float(raw)
        except ValueError:
            raise ValidationError(f"bpm must be a number, got {raw!r}", field=name, value=raw) from None
        if value < 0:
            raise ValidationError(f"bpm must be >= 0, got {value}", field=name, value=raw)
        return value
    if name == "bpm_lock":
        lowered = raw.strip().lower()
        if lowered in _TRUE:
            return 1
        if lowered in _FALSE:
            return 0
        raise ValidationError(f"bpm_lock must be true/false, got {raw!r}", field=name, value=raw)
    if name == "key":
        return MusicalKey.parse(raw)
    return str(raw)


def _parse_int(raw: str, name: str) -> int:
    try:
        return int(raw)
    except ValueError:
        raise ValidationError(f"{name} must be an integer, got {raw!r}", field=name, value=raw) from None


def file_tag_value(name: str, value: Any) -> str | None:
    if value is None:
        return None
    if name == "key":
        return value.text or None
    if name == "bpm":
        return format(value, "g")
    return str(value)


def update_sql(columns: list[str]) -> str:
    assignments = ", ".join(f"{column} = ?" for column in columns)
    return f"UPDATE library SET {assignments} WHERE id = ?"


def plan_set(conn: sqlite3.Connection, track: Track, changes: dict[str, Any], clears: set[str], *,
             write_file: bool, write_db: bool, backup_dir: Path) -> MutationPlan:
    """Build the §7.4 plan: file action (if any file-backed field is in scope) then one UPDATE."""
    overlap = set(changes) & clears
    if overlap:
        raise ValidationError(f"fields both set and cleared: {sorted(overlap)}", fields=sorted(overlap))
    for name in clears:
        if name not in WRITABLE_FIELDS:
            raise ValidationError(f"unknown field {name!r}", field=name, fields=sorted(WRITABLE_FIELDS))
    if not changes and not clears:
        raise ValidationError("nothing to set: pass FIELD=VALUE or --clear FIELD")
    new_values: dict[str, Any] = {**changes, **{name: None for name in clears}}
    if "key" in clears:
        new_values["key"] = MusicalKey(0, "")
    touched = set(new_values)
    file_fields = touched & FILE_BACKED_FIELDS
    db_only_fields = touched - FILE_BACKED_FIELDS
    warnings: list[WarningMessage] = []
    if touched & BLOB_AUTHORITATIVE_FIELDS:
        warnings.append(WarningMessage("blob_authoritative",
                                       "bpm/key were written, but the beats/keys blob is authoritative and Mixxx may discard the change",
                                       {"fields": sorted(touched & BLOB_AUTHORITATIVE_FIELDS)}))
    if not write_file and file_fields:
        warnings.append(WarningMessage("file_backed",
                                       "these fields are re-imported from the audio file; the file's value wins the next time Mixxx re-reads it",
                                       {"fields": sorted(file_fields)}))
    if write_file and db_only_fields:
        warnings.append(WarningMessage("no_file_tag", "these fields have no audio-file counterpart; only the database changed",
                                       {"fields": sorted(db_only_fields)}))

    old_values = {name: getattr(track.metadata, name) for name in touched}
    changed = {name: {"old": old_values[name], "new": new_values[name]} for name in sorted(touched)}

    file_actions = []
    file_written = bool(write_file and file_fields and track.location.path)
    if write_file and file_fields and not track.location.path:
        raise ValidationError("track has no file location to write tags to", track_id=track.id)
    if file_written:
        tag_values = {name: file_tag_value(name, new_values[name]) for name in sorted(file_fields)}
        file_actions.append(TagWriteAction(track.location.path, track.id, tag_values, backup_dir))

    statements: list[Statement] = []
    if write_db:
        columns: list[str] = []
        params: list[Any] = []
        for name in sorted(touched):
            value = new_values[name]
            if name == "key":
                columns += ["key", "key_id"]
                params += [value.text, value.id]
            else:
                columns.append(WRITABLE_FIELDS[name])
                params.append(value)
        if file_written:
            columns.append("source_synchronized_ms")
            params.append(Deferred("mtime_ms"))
        statements.append(Statement(update_sql(columns), (*params, track.id)))

    def after_commit(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> dict[str, Any]:
        has_tuning = any(r[1] == "tuning_frequency_hz" for r in conn.execute("PRAGMA table_info(library)"))
        return {"track": get_track(conn, track.id, has_tuning=has_tuning), "changed": changed, "file_written": file_written}

    return MutationPlan(statements=statements, data={"track": track, "changed": changed, "file_written": file_written},
                        warnings=warnings, file_actions=file_actions, after_commit=after_commit if write_db else None)
```

(`Any` must be imported from `typing` at the top of `tracks.py`.)

```python
    def set(self, track: Track, fields: dict[str, str], *, clear: Iterable[str] = (), file: bool = True, db: bool = True,
            dry_run: bool = False) -> MutationResult:
        if not file and not db:
            raise ValidationError("--mixxx-only and --file-only exclude each other")
        changes = {name: tracks_sql.coerce_field(name, raw) for name, raw in fields.items()}
        clears = set(clear)
        backup_dir = self._lib.paths.backup_dir
        return self._lib.mutate("track set", lambda c: tracks_sql.plan_set(
            c, track, changes, clears, write_file=file, write_db=db, backup_dir=backup_dir), dry_run=dry_run)
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Add `track set` to `src/mixxxlib/cli/track.py`**

```python
@app.command("set")
@global_options
def set_(ctx: typer.Context, track: Annotated[str, typer.Argument(metavar="TRACK")],
         assignments: Annotated[Optional[list[str]], typer.Argument(metavar="[FIELD=VALUE...]")] = None,
         clear: Annotated[Optional[list[str]], typer.Option("--clear", metavar="FIELD", help="Set NULL in the database and remove the file tag.")] = None,
         mixxx_only: Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--mixxx-only", help="Write only the database.")] = False,
         file_only: Annotated[bool, typer.Option("--file-only", help="Write only the audio file.")] = False,
         paths: PathsOption = False, yes: YesOption = False, dry_run: DryRunOption = False) -> None:
    """Write metadata to the audio file and the database (file first). --yes is needed when the file is written."""
    with command(ctx, "track set") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        if mixxx_only and file_only:
            raise ValidationError("--mixxx-only and --file-only exclude each other")
        fields: dict[str, str] = {}
        for item in assignments or []:
            name, sep, value = item.partition("=")
            if not sep or not name:
                raise ValidationError(f"expected FIELD=VALUE, got {item!r}", value=item)
            fields[name] = value
        obj = resolve_track(lib, track, paths=paths)
        touches_file = not mixxx_only and bool((set(fields) | set(clear or [])) & FILE_BACKED_FIELDS)
        if touches_file and not dry_run:
            require_yes(yes, "track set (writes the audio file)")
        result = lib.tracks.set(obj, fields, clear=clear or [], file=not mixxx_only, db=not file_only, dry_run=dry_run)
        changed = output.as_json(result.data["changed"])
        out.emit(result.data, mutation=result, text=lambda: output.kv({"changed": changed, "file_written": result.data["file_written"]}))
```

### Task 18: Cues

**Files:**
- Create: `src/mixxxlib/db/cues.py`, `src/mixxxlib/cli/cue.py`, `tests/integration/test_cli_cue.py`
- Modify: `src/mixxxlib/library.py` (`CuesAPI`), `src/mixxxlib/cli/track.py` (`track show` includes cues), `src/mixxxlib/cli/__init__.py` (register `cue`)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (`db/cues.py`): `row_to_cue(row, samples_per_second) -> Cue`; `list_cues(conn, track, *, type_id=None) -> list[Cue]`; `get_cue_row(conn, cue_id) -> sqlite3.Row`; `parse_cue_type(value) -> tuple[str, int]`; `store_number(value: float) -> int | float`; `validate_cue(conn, track, *, type_name, position, length, slot, color, exclude_id=None) -> int` (returns the slot to store, `-1` when none); `plan_add`, `plan_set`, `plan_delete`, `plan_clear` → `MutationPlan`.
- Produces: `Library.cues: CuesAPI` with `list(track, *, type=None) -> list[Cue]`, `get(cue_id) -> tuple[Cue, Track]`, `add(track, type, position_samples, color, *, slot=None, length=0.0, label="", dry_run=False)`, `set(cue, track, *, position_samples=None, length_samples=None, slot=None, label=None, color=None, dry_run=False)`, `delete(cue, track, *, dry_run=False)`, `clear(track, *, type=None, dry_run=False)`.
- Produces: commands `cue list TRACK [--type TYPE] [--paths]`, `cue add TRACK --type TYPE (--position SECONDS | --position-samples N) --color C [--length SECONDS | --length-samples N] [--slot N] [--label TEXT]`, `cue set CUE [...]`, `cue delete CUE --yes`, `cue clear TRACK [--type TYPE] --yes`.
- Decision 8: `--slot` is required for `hotcue`, optional for `loop` (Mixxx stores saved loops in hotcue slots, as the reference library shows), and refused for `jump`/`intro`/`outro`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/mixxxlib/db/cues.py`**

```python
"""All SQL for cues plus slot/position validation (spec §6.3, §8.4)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import sqlite3
from typing import Any

from mixxxlib.db.connection import MutationPlan, Statement
from mixxxlib.db.tracks import get_track
from mixxxlib.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationError
from mixxxlib.model import CUE_TYPE_IDS, CUE_TYPES, WRITABLE_CUE_TYPES, Cue, Track

CUE_SELECT = "SELECT id, track_id, type, position, length, hotcue, label, color FROM cues"
SLOT_REQUIRED = frozenset({"hotcue"})
SLOT_OPTIONAL = frozenset({"loop"})


def row_to_cue(row: sqlite3.Row, samples_per_second: float | None) -> Cue:
    position, length = float(row["position"]), float(row["length"])
    rate = samples_per_second or None
    return Cue(id=row["id"], track_id=row["track_id"], type_id=row["type"], position_samples=position,
               length_samples=length, slot=row["hotcue"], label=row["label"] or "", color=row["color"],
               position_seconds=position / rate if rate else None, length_seconds=length / rate if rate else None)


def list_cues(conn: sqlite3.Connection, track: Track, *, type_id: int | None = None) -> list[Cue]:
    sql, params = CUE_SELECT + " WHERE track_id = ?", [track.id]
    if type_id is not None:
        sql, params = sql + " AND type = ?", params + [type_id]
    rows = conn.execute(sql + " ORDER BY type, hotcue, position, id", params).fetchall()
    return [row_to_cue(row, track.samples_per_second) for row in rows]


def get_cue_row(conn: sqlite3.Connection, cue_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row:
    row = conn.execute(CUE_SELECT + " WHERE id = ?", (cue_id,)).fetchone()
    if row is None:
        raise NotFoundError(f"cue {cue_id} does not exist", cue_id=cue_id)
    return row


def parse_cue_type(value: str | int) -> tuple[str, int]:
    text = str(value).strip().lower()
    if text.isdigit() and int(text) in CUE_TYPES:
        return CUE_TYPES[int(text)], int(text)
    if text in CUE_TYPE_IDS:
        return text, CUE_TYPE_IDS[text]
    raise ValidationError(f"unknown cue type {value!r}", type=value, types=sorted(CUE_TYPE_IDS))


def require_writable(type_name: str) -> None:
    if type_name not in WRITABLE_CUE_TYPES:
        raise ValidationError(f"cue type {type_name!r} is read-only in v1; writable types are {sorted(WRITABLE_CUE_TYPES)}",
                              type=type_name, writable=sorted(WRITABLE_CUE_TYPES))


def store_number(value: float) -> int | float:
    """Store whole numbers as INTEGER (as Mixxx mostly does) and fractions as REAL."""
    return int(value) if float(value).is_integer() else float(value)


def validate_cue(conn: sqlite3.Connection, track: Track, *, type_name: str, position: float, length: float,
                 slot: int | None, color: int, exclude_id: int | None = None) -> int:
    require_writable(type_name)
    if color <= 0 or color > 0xFFFFFF:
        raise ValidationError("cue colour must be a non-zero 24-bit RGB value", color=color)
    if type_name in SLOT_REQUIRED and slot is None:
        raise ValidationError(f"--slot is required for {type_name} cues", type=type_name)
    if type_name not in SLOT_REQUIRED | SLOT_OPTIONAL and slot is not None:
        raise ValidationError(f"--slot is not allowed for {type_name} cues", type=type_name, slot=slot)
    if slot is not None and slot < 0:
        raise ValidationError("slot must be >= 0 (0-based; the UI shows it 1-based)", slot=slot)
    if position < 0:
        raise ValidationError("position must be >= 0", position_samples=position)
    if length < 0:
        raise ValidationError("length must be >= 0", length_samples=length)
    limit = (track.audio.duration or 0) * (track.samples_per_second or 0)
    if limit and position + length > limit:
        raise ValidationError(f"position + length ({position + length:g} samples) exceeds the track ({limit:g} samples)",
                              position_samples=position, length_samples=length, track_samples=limit)
    type_id = CUE_TYPE_IDS[type_name]
    if slot is not None:
        sql, params = "SELECT id FROM cues WHERE track_id = ? AND type = ? AND hotcue = ?", [track.id, type_id, slot]
        if exclude_id is not None:
            sql, params = sql + " AND id != ?", params + [exclude_id]
        existing = conn.execute(sql + " ORDER BY id LIMIT 1", params).fetchone()
        if existing is not None:
            raise ValidationError(f"{type_name} slot {slot} is already occupied on track {track.id} by cue {existing[0]}",
                                  track_id=track.id, type=type_name, slot=slot, existing_cue_id=existing[0])
    return -1 if slot is None else slot


def _fetch(conn: sqlite3.Connection, cue_id: int, track: Track) -> Cue:
    return row_to_cue(get_cue_row(conn, cue_id), track.samples_per_second)


def plan_add(conn: sqlite3.Connection, track: Track, *, type_name: str, position: float, length: float,
             slot: int | None, color: int, label: str) -> MutationPlan:
    stored_slot = validate_cue(conn, track, type_name=type_name, position=position, length=length, slot=slot, color=color)
    type_id = CUE_TYPE_IDS[type_name]
    statement = Statement("INSERT INTO cues (track_id, type, position, length, hotcue, label, color) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                          (track.id, type_id, store_number(position), store_number(length), stored_slot, label, color))
    preview = Cue(id=-1, track_id=track.id, type_id=type_id, position_samples=float(position), length_samples=float(length),
                  slot=stored_slot, label=label, color=color,
                  position_seconds=position / track.samples_per_second if track.samples_per_second else None,
                  length_seconds=length / track.samples_per_second if track.samples_per_second else None)

    def after_commit(conn: sqlite3.Connection) -> dict[str, Any]:
        new_id = conn.execute("SELECT last_insert_rowid()").fetchone()[0]
        return {"cue": _fetch(conn, new_id, track)}

    return MutationPlan(statements=[statement], data={"cue": preview}, after_commit=after_commit)


def plan_set(conn: sqlite3.Connection, cue: Cue, track: Track, *, position: float | None, length: float | None,
             slot: int | None, label: str | None, color: int | None) -> MutationPlan:
    require_writable(cue.type)
    changes: dict[str, Any] = {}
    if position is not None and position != cue.position_samples:
        changes["position_samples"] = {"old": cue.position_samples, "new": float(position)}
    if length is not None and length != cue.length_samples:
        changes["length_samples"] = {"old": cue.length_samples, "new": float(length)}
    if slot is not None and slot != cue.slot:
        changes["slot"] = {"old": cue.slot, "new": slot}
    if label is not None and label != cue.label:
        changes["label"] = {"old": cue.label, "new": label}
    if color is not None and color != cue.color:
        changes["color"] = {"old": cue.color, "new": color}
    if position is None and length is None and slot is None and label is None and color is None:
        raise ValidationError("nothing to set: pass at least one of --position/--length/--slot/--label/--color", cue_id=cue.id)
    new_position = cue.position_samples if position is None else position
    new_length = cue.length_samples if length is None else length
    new_slot = (cue.slot if cue.slot >= 0 else None) if slot is None else slot
    new_color = cue.color if color is None else color
    stored_slot = validate_cue(conn, track, type_name=cue.type, position=new_position, length=new_length, slot=new_slot,
                               color=new_color, exclude_id=cue.id)
    statement = Statement("UPDATE cues SET position = ?, length = ?, hotcue = ?, label = ?, color = ? WHERE id = ?",
                          (store_number(new_position), store_number(new_length), stored_slot,
                           cue.label if label is None else label, new_color, cue.id))
    return MutationPlan(statements=[statement], data={"cue": cue, "changed": changes},
                        after_commit=lambda c: {"cue": _fetch(c, cue.id, track), "changed": changes})


def plan_delete(conn: sqlite3.Connection, cue: Cue) -> MutationPlan:
    require_writable(cue.type)
    return MutationPlan(statements=[Statement("DELETE FROM cues WHERE id = ?", (cue.id,))], data={"deleted": cue})


def plan_clear(conn: sqlite3.Connection, track: Track, *, type_name: str | None) -> MutationPlan:
    if type_name is not None:
        require_writable(type_name)
        type_ids = [CUE_TYPE_IDS[type_name]]
    else:
        type_ids = sorted(CUE_TYPE_IDS[name] for name in WRITABLE_CUE_TYPES)
    marks = ",".join("?" * len(type_ids))
    rows = conn.execute(CUE_SELECT + f" WHERE track_id = ? AND type IN ({marks}) ORDER BY type, hotcue, position, id",
                        [track.id, *type_ids]).fetchall()
    victims = [row_to_cue(r, track.samples_per_second) for r in rows]
    statements = [Statement("DELETE FROM cues WHERE id = ?", (c.id,)) for c in victims]
    return MutationPlan(statements=statements, data={"deleted": victims})
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add `CuesAPI` to `src/mixxxlib/library.py`**

Add `from mixxxlib.db import cues as cues_sql`, `from mixxxlib.model import Cue`, set `self.cues = CuesAPI(self)` in `__init__`, and:

```python
class CuesAPI:
    def __init__(self, lib: Library) -> None:
        self._lib = lib

    def list(self, track: Track, *, type: str | None = None) -> list[Cue]:
        type_id = cues_sql.parse_cue_type(type)[1] if type is not None else None
        with self._lib.read() as conn:
            return cues_sql.list_cues(conn, track, type_id=type_id)

    def get(self, cue_id: int) -> tuple[Cue, Track]:
        with self._lib.read() as conn:
            row = cues_sql.get_cue_row(conn, int(cue_id))
            track = tracks_sql.get_track(conn, row["track_id"], has_tuning=self._lib.tracks._has_tuning)
            return cues_sql.row_to_cue(row, track.samples_per_second), track

    def add(self, track: Track, type: str, position_samples: float, color: int, *, slot: int | None = None,
            length: float = 0.0, label: str = "", dry_run: bool = False) -> MutationResult:
        type_name, _ = cues_sql.parse_cue_type(type)
        return self._lib.mutate("cue add", lambda c: cues_sql.plan_add(
            c, track, type_name=type_name, position=position_samples, length=length, slot=slot, color=color, label=label), dry_run=dry_run)

    def set(self, cue: Cue, track: Track, *, position_samples: float | None = None, length_samples: float | None = None,
            slot: int | None = None, label: str | None = None, color: int | None = None, dry_run: bool = False) -> MutationResult:
        return self._lib.mutate("cue set", lambda c: cues_sql.plan_set(
            c, cue, track, position=position_samples, length=length_samples, slot=slot, label=label, color=color), dry_run=dry_run)

    def delete(self, cue: Cue, *, dry_run: bool = False) -> MutationResult:
        return self._lib.mutate("cue delete", lambda c: cues_sql.plan_delete(c, cue), dry_run=dry_run)

    def clear(self, track: Track, *, type: str | None = None, dry_run: bool = False) -> MutationResult:
        type_name = cues_sql.parse_cue_type(type)[0] if type is not None else None
        return self._lib.mutate("cue clear", lambda c: cues_sql.plan_clear(c, track, type_name=type_name), dry_run=dry_run)
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write `src/mixxxlib/cli/cue.py` and wire `track show`**

`src/mixxxlib/cli/cue.py`:

```python
from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Annotated, Optional

import typer

from mixxxlib import output
from mixxxlib.cli.common import (
    DryRunOption, PathsOption, YesOption, command, global_options, open_library, parse_int, require_yes, resolve_track,
)
from mixxxlib.errors import ValidationError
from mixxxlib.model import Track, parse_color

app = typer.Typer(no_args_is_help=True, help="Cue points: hotcue, loop, jump, intro, outro are writable; main/n60db/beat are read-only.")

TrackArg = Annotated[str, typer.Argument(metavar="TRACK")]
CueArg = Annotated[int, typer.Argument(metavar="CUE", help="Cue id.")]
TypeOption = Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Option("--type", metavar="TYPE", help="hotcue, loop, jump, intro, outro (or the integer).")]
PositionOption = Annotated[Optional[float], typer.Option("--position", metavar="SECONDS")]
PositionSamplesOption = Annotated[Optional[float], typer.Option("--position-samples", metavar="N", help="Interleaved stereo samples.")]
LengthOption = Annotated[Optional[float], typer.Option("--length", metavar="SECONDS")]
LengthSamplesOption = Annotated[Optional[float], typer.Option("--length-samples", metavar="N")]
SlotOption = Annotated[Optional[int], typer.Option("--slot", metavar="N", help="0-based hotcue slot.")]
LabelOption = Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Option("--label", metavar="TEXT")]
ColorOption = Annotated[Optional[str], typer.Option("--color", metavar="C", help="#RRGGBB, 0xRRGGBB, decimal or AARRGGBB (alpha stripped). Must be non-zero.")]


def _samples(track: Track, seconds: float | None, samples: float | None, what: str, *, required: bool) -> float | None:
    if seconds is not None and samples is not None:
        raise ValidationError(f"pass either --{what} or --{what}-samples, not both")
    if seconds is None and samples is None:
        if required:
            raise ValidationError(f"--{what} or --{what}-samples is required")
        return None
    if samples is not None:
        return float(samples)
    rate = track.samples_per_second
    if not rate:
        raise ValidationError(f"track {track.id} has no samplerate/channels; pass --{what}-samples", track_id=track.id)
    return float(seconds) * rate


@app.command("list")
@global_options
def list_(ctx: typer.Context, track: TrackArg, type_: TypeOption = None, paths: PathsOption = False) -> None:
    """Cues on a track, sorted by type, slot, position."""
    with command(ctx, "cue list") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        obj = resolve_track(lib, track, paths=paths)
        cues = lib.cues.list(obj, type=type_)
        out.emit(cues, warnings=lib.read_warnings(), text=lambda: output.render_cues(cues))


@app.command("add")
@global_options
def add(ctx: typer.Context, track: TrackArg, type_: TypeOption = None, position: PositionOption = None,
        position_samples: PositionSamplesOption = None, color: ColorOption = None, length: LengthOption = None,
        length_samples: LengthSamplesOption = None, slot: SlotOption = None, label: LabelOption = None,
        paths: PathsOption = False, dry_run: DryRunOption = False) -> None:
    """Validated insert. --slot is required for hotcue, optional for loop, refused otherwise."""
    with command(ctx, "cue add") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        if type_ is None:
            raise ValidationError("--type is required")
        if color is None:
            raise ValidationError("--color is required (Mixxx discards cues whose colour is 0)")
        obj = resolve_track(lib, track, paths=paths)
        pos = _samples(obj, position, position_samples, "position", required=True)
        length_value = _samples(obj, length, length_samples, "length", required=False) or 0.0
        result = lib.cues.add(obj, type_, pos, parse_color(color), slot=slot, length=length_value, label=label or "", dry_run=dry_run)
        out.emit(result.data, mutation=result, text=lambda: output.render_cues([result.data["cue"]]))


@app.command("set")
@global_options
def set_(ctx: typer.Context, cue: CueArg, position: PositionOption = None, position_samples: PositionSamplesOption = None,
         length: LengthOption = None, length_samples: LengthSamplesOption = None, slot: SlotOption = None,
         label: LabelOption = None, color: ColorOption = None, dry_run: DryRunOption = False) -> None:
    """Update a cue; the same validation as add."""
    with command(ctx, "cue set") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        cue_obj, track_obj = lib.cues.get(cue)
        result = lib.cues.set(cue_obj, track_obj,
                              position_samples=_samples(track_obj, position, position_samples, "position", required=False),
                              length_samples=_samples(track_obj, length, length_samples, "length", required=False),
                              slot=slot, label=label, color=parse_color(color) if color is not None else None, dry_run=dry_run)
        out.emit(result.data, mutation=result, text=lambda: output.render_cues([result.data["cue"]]))


@app.command("delete")
@global_options
def delete(ctx: typer.Context, cue: CueArg, yes: YesOption = False, dry_run: DryRunOption = False) -> None:
    """Delete one cue. Needs --yes."""
    with command(ctx, "cue delete") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        cue_obj, _ = lib.cues.get(cue)
        if not dry_run:
            require_yes(yes, "cue delete")
        result = lib.cues.delete(cue_obj, dry_run=dry_run)
        out.emit(result.data, mutation=result, text=lambda: f"deleted cue {cue_obj.id}")


@app.command("clear")
@global_options
def clear(ctx: typer.Context, track: TrackArg, type_: TypeOption = None, paths: PathsOption = False,
          yes: YesOption = False, dry_run: DryRunOption = False) -> None:
    """Delete every writable-type cue on a track, or only one writable type. Read-only types are never touched."""
    with command(ctx, "cue clear") as out:
        lib = open_library(ctx)
        obj = resolve_track(lib, track, paths=paths)
        if not dry_run:
            require_yes(yes, "cue clear")
        result = lib.cues.clear(obj, type=type_, dry_run=dry_run)
        out.emit(result.data, mutation=result, text=lambda: f"deleted {len(result.data['deleted'])} cue(s)")
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write `scripts/build-zipapp.sh`**

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Build the single-file zipapp: dist/mixxxlib (or $1). Needs the dev venv (shiv) on PATH or .venv/bin.
set -eu
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
OUT="${1:-dist/mixxxlib}"
if [ -x .venv/bin/shiv ]; then SHIV=.venv/bin/shiv; else SHIV=shiv; fi
mkdir -p "$(dirname "$OUT")"
"$SHIV" -c mixxxlib -o "$OUT" -p '/usr/bin/env python3' .
echo "built $OUT"
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `scripts/check-schema-upstream.sh`**

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Fetch res/schema.xml from Mixxx main and print every revision above our ceiling. Manual; uses the network.
set -eu
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.."
URL="${MIXXX_SCHEMA_URL:-https://raw.githubusercontent.com/mixxxdj/mixxx/main/res/schema.xml}"
CEILING="$(sed -n 's/^SCHEMA_CEILING = \([0-9]*\).*/\1/p' src/mixxxlib/db/schema.py)"
TMP="$(mktemp)"
trap 'rm -f "$TMP"' EXIT
curl -fsSL "$URL" -o "$TMP"
echo "ceiling: $CEILING"
grep -o 'revision="[0-9]*"' "$TMP" | sed 's/[^0-9]//g' | sort -n | uniq | awk -v c="$CEILING" '$1 > c { print "upstream revision above ceiling: " $1; found = 1 } END { if (!found) print "no revisions above the ceiling" }'
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `scripts/sandbox-mixxx.sh`**

```sh
#!/bin/sh
# Live Mixxx harness (spec §12.3). Linux only, manual. Usage: scripts/sandbox-mixxx.sh TRACK_ID [extra mixxxlib args...]
# Copies the real database and mixxx.cfg into /tmp/mixxx-sandbox, applies a test write with mixxxlib,
# launches Mixxx against the sandbox, screenshots it, stops it, and diffs the database against the pre-launch snapshot.
set -eu
TRACK="${1:?track id}"
shift
SANDBOX=/tmp/mixxx-sandbox
SRC="${MIXXX_SETTINGS:-$HOME/.mixxx}"
MIXXXLIB="${MIXXXLIB:-$(dirname "$0")/../.venv/bin/mixxxlib}"
if pgrep -x mixxx >/dev/null; then echo "Mixxx is running; close it first" >&2; exit 5; fi
rm -rf "$SANDBOX"
mkdir -p "$SANDBOX/backups"
cp "$SRC/mixxxdb.sqlite" "$SANDBOX/"
cp "$SRC/mixxx.cfg" "$SANDBOX/"
"$MIXXXLIB" --settings-path "$SANDBOX" --backup-dir "$SANDBOX/backups" cue add "$TRACK" --type hotcue --slot 7 --position 1.0 --color '#C50A08' --label mixxxlib "$@"
sqlite3 "$SANDBOX/mixxxdb.sqlite" ".dump cues" > "$SANDBOX/cues-before.sql"
sqlite3 "$SANDBOX/mixxxdb.sqlite" "select * from sqlite_sequence" > "$SANDBOX/sequence-before.txt"
QT_QPA_PLATFORM=xcb mixxx --settings-path "$SANDBOX" --safe-mode "$(sqlite3 "$SANDBOX/mixxxdb.sqlite" "select tl.location from library l join track_locations tl on tl.id=l.location where l.id=$TRACK")" > "$SANDBOX/mixxx.out" 2>&1 &
MIXXX_PID=$!
sleep 20
WID="$(xdotool search --name 'Mixxx' | head -1 || true)"
[ -n "$WID" ] && import -window "$WID" "$SANDBOX/screenshot.png" || echo "no Mixxx window found for screenshot" >&2
kill -TERM "$MIXXX_PID" || true
wait "$MIXXX_PID" 2>/dev/null || true
sqlite3 "$SANDBOX/mixxxdb.sqlite" ".dump cues" > "$SANDBOX/cues-after.sql"
sqlite3 "$SANDBOX/mixxxdb.sqlite" "select * from sqlite_sequence" > "$SANDBOX/sequence-after.txt"
echo "--- cues diff (empty means Mixxx kept the rows byte-identical)"
diff "$SANDBOX/cues-before.sql" "$SANDBOX/cues-after.sql" || true
echo "--- sqlite_sequence diff (one new Playlists history row per launch is expected noise)"
diff "$SANDBOX/sequence-before.txt" "$SANDBOX/sequence-after.txt" || true
echo "screenshot: $SANDBOX/screenshot.png  log: $SANDBOX/mixxx.out"
```

Make all three executable: `chmod +x scripts/*.sh`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the zipapp smoke test**

`tests/integration/test_zipapp.py`:

```python
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def zipapp(tmp_path_factory):
    if shutil.which("shiv") is None and not (ROOT / ".venv/bin/shiv").exists():
        pytest.skip("shiv not installed")
    out = tmp_path_factory.mktemp("dist") / "mixxxlib"
    env = {**os.environ, "PATH": f"{Path(sys.executable).parent}{os.pathsep}{os.environ.get('PATH', '')}"}
    subprocess.run([str(ROOT / "scripts/build-zipapp.sh"), str(out)], check=True, cwd=ROOT, env=env,
                   stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE)
    assert out.exists() and os.access(out, os.X_OK)
    return out


def run(zipapp, box, *args):
    env = {**os.environ, "SHIV_ROOT": str(box.settings_dir.parent / "shiv-cache")}
    return subprocess.run([str(zipapp), "--settings-path", str(box.settings_dir), "--backup-dir", str(box.backup_dir), *args],
                          capture_output=True, text=True, env=env)


def test_zipapp_info_and_doctor_match_the_venv_run(zipapp, sandbox):
    for args in (["info", "--json"], ["doctor", "--json"]):
        bundled = run(zipapp, sandbox, *args)
        native = sandbox.run(*args)
        assert bundled.returncode == native.code, bundled.stderr
        assert json.loads(bundled.stdout)["ok"] is True
        assert json.loads(bundled.stdout)["data"]["summary" if args[0] == "doctor" else "schema"] == native.json["data"]["summary" if args[0] == "doctor" else "schema"]
    missing = run(zipapp, sandbox, "track", "show", "999999", "--json")
    assert missing.returncode == 3 and json.loads(missing.stderr)["error"]["code"] == "not_found"
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write `README.md`**

````markdown
# mixxxlib

A deterministic, offline command-line tool and Python library for reading and writing a
[Mixxx](https://mixxx.org) library: the `mixxxdb.sqlite` database and the audio files' tags.
Built for programs first (`--json` everywhere, stable exit codes) and people second.

## What it does and does not do

**Does:** crate CRUD and membership (including the `locked` / `show` / `autodj_source` flags);
every user-facing track metadata field, written to the audio file and the database together;
cue points (read and write); beatgrid and key (read only); a `doctor` health check; `mixxx.cfg`
introspection; automatic, pruned database backups before every change.

**Does not:** run while Mixxx is running; write the `beats` or `keys` blobs; control a live
Mixxx; hold any opinion about what your metadata means. Playlists are not in v1. Nested
crates are prepared for (see below) but Mixxx does not ship them yet.

## Install

### Zipapp (recommended)

The release artifact is a single executable file that bundles the package and its two
dependencies. The only thing the machine needs is Python 3.12 or newer.

```sh
# download dist/mixxxlib from a release, or build it from a checkout:
python3 -m venv .venv && .venv/bin/pip install -e '.[dev]' && scripts/build-zipapp.sh
chmod +x dist/mixxxlib
cp dist/mixxxlib ~/.local/bin/
mixxxlib info
```

On first run the zipapp unpacks itself into `~/.shiv/` and reuses that afterwards.

### Developer install

```sh
python3 -m venv .venv
.venv/bin/pip install -e '.[dev]'
.venv/bin/mixxxlib --help
```

## Safety rules

- **Mixxx must be closed.** Mixxx keeps an in-memory track cache that overwrites external
  edits; every mutating command checks for a running `mixxx` process and probes the database
  lock, and refuses with exit 5 if either says Mixxx is up. Read commands work while Mixxx is
  running and carry a `mixxx_running` warning.
- **Backups are automatic.** Before any database write, `VACUUM INTO` copies the database to
  `~/.local/share/mixxxlib/backups/mixxxdb-<UTC timestamp>-<command>.sqlite` (override with
  `--backup-dir` or `MIXXXLIB_BACKUP_DIR`). Backups older than 30 days are pruned, but the ten
  newest are always kept (`MIXXXLIB_BACKUP_TTL_DAYS`, `MIXXXLIB_BACKUP_KEEP_MIN`). Before any
  audio-file write, the file's tags are snapshotted to `tags-<timestamp>-<track id>.json` in the
  same directory. There is no `--no-backup`.
- **Foreign keys are broken upstream and must never be enabled.** Mixxx's schema references a
  table (`library_old`) that does not exist, so `PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON` would break a healthy
  database. mixxxlib never enables it and never alters the schema; referential checks are done in
  application code and surfaced by `doctor`.
- **Schema ceiling.** mixxxlib knows schema revisions 39 (Mixxx 2.5.x, verified) and 40
  (allowed with a `schema_unverified` warning). Anything above refuses to mutate with exit 6.
- Destructive commands need `--yes`. `--dry-run` prints the exact statements and file operations
  and changes nothing; it is never the default.

## Commands

Global options, accepted before or after the subcommand: `--settings-path DIR`, `--db FILE`,
`--backup-dir DIR`, `--json`. Mutating commands also take `--dry-run` and `--yes`.

```sh
mixxxlib info                                   # paths, schema verdict, Mixxx running?, backups
mixxxlib settings show Library.SyncTrackMetadataExport

mixxxlib crate list --json
mixxxlib crate create "Peak Time"
mixxxlib crate add "Peak Time" 612 613 --json   # or: --paths /music/a.aiff, or --stdin
mixxxlib crate remove "Peak Time" 613 --yes
mixxxlib crate export "Peak Time" > peak.m3u8

mixxxlib track list --crate "Peak Time" --search "ashez"
mixxxlib track show 612 --json
mixxxlib track set 612 genre=Bass "comment=played 2026-10" --yes     # writes the file, then the database
mixxxlib track set 612 rating=5                                       # database-only field, no --yes needed
mixxxlib track set 612 --clear comment --yes

mixxxlib cue list 612
mixxxlib cue add 612 --type hotcue --slot 0 --position 61.5 --color '#C50A08' --label drop
mixxxlib cue clear 612 --type hotcue --yes

mixxxlib doctor --json
mixxxlib backup list
mixxxlib backup restore ~/.local/share/mixxxlib/backups/mixxxdb-20261008T142233Z-crate-add.sqlite --yes
```

Crates are addressed by name (`--id` for an integer id). Tracks are integer ids (`--paths` for
file paths). Cues are cue ids.

## JSON envelope and exit codes

Every successful command with `--json` prints exactly one object to stdout:

```json
{"ok": true, "command": "crate add", "data": {"crate": {"id": 7, "name": "Peak Time", "parent_id": null,
 "locked": false, "show": true, "autodj_source": false, "track_count": 2}, "added": [612, 613], "already_member": []},
 "warnings": [],
 "mutation": {"dry_run": false, "backup": "/home/me/.local/share/mixxxlib/backups/mixxxdb-20261008T142233Z-crate-add.sqlite",
              "integrity": "ok", "statements": 2}}
```

`warnings` is always present. `mutation` appears only on mutating commands; in a dry run
`statements` becomes the list of SQL statements (with bound values rendered) and file operations.
Errors go to stderr as `{"ok": false, "error": {"code", "exit_code", "message", "details"}}`
(or `mixxxlib: <code>: <message>` without `--json`). Stdout never carries anything but a
successful payload. Lists are sorted deterministically and no timestamps are invented, so the same
database produces byte-identical output.

| Code | Name | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| 0 | `ok` | Success (warnings may be present) |
| 1 | `internal` | Unexpected exception; a bug |
| 2 | `usage` | Bad arguments or flags |
| 3 | `not_found` | Crate, track, cue, file or backup does not exist |
| 4 | `validation` | Input refused before any write (range, duplicate name, occupied slot, locked crate, …) |
| 5 | `mixxx_running` | Mixxx is running or the database is locked |
| 6 | `schema_unsupported` | Schema above the ceiling, below what a command needs, or unreadable |
| 7 | `database` | SQLite error, backup failure, or `integrity_check` not `ok` |
| 8 | `confirmation_required` | Destructive command without `--yes` |
| 9 | `tag_error` | Audio file missing, unsupported, or tags failed to read or write |
| 10 | `unhealthy` | `doctor` found at least one error-severity problem |

## Caveats: `bpm`, `key`, and `--mixxx-only`

`bpm` and `key` are writable because every metadata field is, but Mixxx derives them from the
`beats` and `keys` analysis blobs, which mixxxlib never writes. Mixxx will most likely resolve any
disagreement in the blob's favour and silently discard your value. Every write to these fields
succeeds with exit 0 and a `blob_authoritative` warning. Re-analyse the track in Mixxx if you need
the grid to move.

`track set` writes the audio file first, re-reads it to confirm, and only then updates the
database (and `source_synchronized_ms`), because that is the only order that stays consistent if
the process dies halfway. `--mixxx-only` skips the file. For file-backed fields (artist, title,
album, album artist, year, genre, track number/total, composer, grouping, comment, bpm, key) that
means your value lasts only until Mixxx next re-imports the file's tags; the command says so with a
`file_backed` warning. `--file-only` skips the database.

## Nested crates

Mixxx has no crate hierarchy today. An upstream draft (mixxxdj/mixxx PR #16871) adds
`crates.parent_id` at schema revision 41 and a `UNIQUE (parent_id, name)` rebuild at 42. mixxxlib
is shaped for it: every crate carries `parent_id` (always `null` for now), `crate tree`,
`crate move`, `--parent` and `--recursive` exist and return exit 6 below revision 41, and
sibling-name uniqueness is enforced in application code because SQLite treats `NULL` parents as
distinct. The ceiling stays at 40 until the PR merges and its SQL is verified against a real
migrated database.

## Path discovery

Highest precedence first: `--db FILE` / `--settings-path DIR`; `MIXXXLIB_DB` /
`MIXXXLIB_SETTINGS_PATH`; then the platform default settings directory (Linux `~/.mixxx`, macOS
`~/Library/Containers/org.mixxx.mixxx/Data/Library/Application Support/Mixxx` falling back to
`~/Library/Application Support/Mixxx`, Windows `%LOCALAPPDATA%\Mixxx`). The database is
`<settings>/mixxxdb.sqlite` unless `--db` says otherwise; `mixxx.cfg` is only needed by
`settings show`. Nothing is ever created implicitly; a missing database is exit 3.

## Library API

```python
from mixxxlib import Library

lib = Library.open()                       # or settings_path=..., db=..., backup_dir=...
crate = lib.crates.get("Peak Time")
result = lib.crates.add(crate, [612, 613])  # runs the full safety protocol, returns MutationResult
track = lib.tracks.get(612)
lib.tracks.set(track, {"genre": "Bass"}, file=True)
lib.cues.add(track, "hotcue", 61.5 * track.samples_per_second, 0xC50A08, slot=0)
findings = lib.doctor()
```

Errors are `mixxxlib.errors` subclasses with `.code`, `.exit_code` and `.details`.

## Tests and the live harness

```sh
.venv/bin/pytest                      # fixture-based; never touches ~/.mixxx
scripts/check-schema-upstream.sh      # manual: does Mixxx main carry a revision above our ceiling?
scripts/sandbox-mixxx.sh 612          # manual, Linux: write a hotcue into a sandbox copy, launch Mixxx against it,
                                      # screenshot, stop, and diff the database
```

Fixtures live in `tests/fixtures/`: a real Mixxx 2.5.6 database trimmed to ~20 tracks with
one-second audio files, plus a derived schema-42 copy. Regenerate them with
`scripts/make-fixture.py` (see its docstring). Every integration test ends by running `doctor`
and asserting it found no new errors.
````

- [ ] **Step 2: Check the README renders the facts the code enforces**

Run: `.venv/bin/mixxxlib --help` and compare the command list with the README's examples; run `.venv/bin/pytest -q` once more.
Expected: every command named in the README exists; the suite passes.

- [ ] **Step 3: Commit**

```sh
git add README.md
git commit -m "docs: README with install, safety rules, command surface, envelope, caveats"
```

## Self-review (done while writing; recorded for the executor)

**Spec coverage.** §1.1 scope → Tasks 10, 13, 14, 17, 18 (crates, tracks, cues), 9 (beatgrid read), 11/15 (doctor), 4/12 (settings), 7 (backups). §2 constraints → `--json` on every command (Task 12 `global_options`), exit codes (Tasks 2, 12), no prompts (none exist), `--yes`/`--dry-run` (Tasks 8, 14, 15, 17, 18), composable single-purpose commands, importable library (Task 10), no `PRAGMA foreign_keys` anywhere, schema degradation (Tasks 5, 8), safety (Tasks 7, 8). §3 tech → Task 1 (pyproject), Task 19 (zipapp, scripts), Task 5 (schema permalink). §4/§5 → Tasks 2, 12. §6.1 nested crates → Tasks 10, 14 (sibling uniqueness, no path syntax, exit 6). §6.2 fields/mapping → Tasks 13, 16, 17. §6.3 cues → Task 18. §6.4 blobs and caveats → Tasks 9, 17. §6.5 → Task 4. §7 → Tasks 7, 8, 16, 17 (two-store order, snapshot/restore, `--mixxx-only`, `--file-only`, what counts as destructive). §8 surface → Tasks 12–18 (every row). §9 → Task 3. §10 structure → File Structure section (plus `settings.py` and `cli/common.py`, noted). §11 → Task 20. §12 → Task 6 (fixtures incl. every listed anomaly, schema pin), unit tests in Tasks 2–5, 7, 9, 12, 16, integration tests in Tasks 8, 10, 11, 13–18, zipapp smoke in Task 19, live harness script in Task 19; doctor-at-teardown in Task 11.

**Known gaps, deliberate:** `doctor`'s `mixxx_running` check reports only the process probe, because a read-only connection cannot run `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`; a locked database already surfaces as exit 5. `Library.open` does not refuse reads above the ceiling (so `info` and `doctor` can report the problem); only mutations are guarded, per spec constraint 9.

**Type consistency checked:** `MutationPlan`/`Statement`/`Deferred` (Task 8) are what Tasks 10, 17, 18 build; `FileAction` protocol (Task 8) is what `TagWriteAction` (Task 16) implements and `plan_set` (Task 17) uses; `Crate.parent_id`, `Track.samples_per_second`, `Cue` field order (Task 2) match `as_json` (Task 12) and the CLI tests' expected key lists (Tasks 13, 18); `resolve_crate`/`resolve_track` move to `common.py` in Task 14 and are imported from there by `track.py` and `cue.py`; `WarningMessage` codes used: `schema_unverified`, `mixxx_running`, `already_member`, `not_member`, `blob_authoritative`, `file_backed`, `no_file_tag`, plus the blob-decoder codes `beats_undecodable` / `unknown_blob_version` (spec §6.4 asks for "a warning" without naming it).
